# Upstream Backlog

Upstream features from anthropics/claude-code not yet implemented in OpenClaude.
Source: https://github.com/anthropics/claude-code/blob/main/CHANGELOG.md

Only entries after v2.1.87 (our fork base). Refresh by fetching:
`curl -fsSL https://raw.githubusercontent.com/anthropics/claude-code/refs/heads/main/CHANGELOG.md`

## 2.1.161

- [-] `OTEL_RESOURCE_ATTRIBUTES values are now included as labels on metric datapoints, so you can slice usage metrics by custom dimensions like team or repo` — SKIP (telemetry neutralized in this fork)
- [ ] `claude agents rows now show done/total before the detail when work is fanned out; peek shows the longest-running item` — TODO (agents-view dashboard exists; assess `done/total` + longest-running peek)
- [-] `/mcp now collapses claude.ai connectors you've never signed in to behind a "Show unused connectors" row` — SKIP (claude.ai connector-specific; we don't surface claude.ai OAuth connectors)
- [x] (7ddb669) `Parallel tool calls: a failed Bash command no longer cancels other calls in the same batch — each tool returns its own result independently` — TODO (general fix; applies to our parallel tool execution)
- [x] (1fa21eb) `Fullscreen mode: clipboard now uses wl-copy/xclip/xsel on Linux when available, copies to both the clipboard and PRIMARY selection for middle-click paste, and the "hold {key} for native selection" hint now shows the correct key per terminal` — DONE: PRIMARY-selection copy added in osc.ts (getLinuxClipboardArgs); wl-copy/xclip/xsel backend + per-terminal native-select hint (option+click macOS / shift+click else) were already present.
- [x] (7ddb669) `Fixed the /effort dialog, workflow animations, and prompt keyword shimmer not honoring the "Reduce motion" setting` — TODO (we have /effort + workflow animations + keyword shimmer)
- [-] `Fixed forceLoginOrgUUID/forceLoginMethod managed-settings policies blocking third-party provider sessions (Bedrock, Vertex, Foundry, Mantle) alongside the org pin (regression in 2.1.146)` — SKIP (managed-settings login-policy + provider-specific; not our flow, post-base regression)
- [x] (02e9ce7) `Fixed background subagent output corrupting claude -p stdout when using --output-format text or json` — DONE: installOpaqueStdoutGuard() diverts stray mid-run stdout to stderr in text/json print modes; blessed final result bypasses via getBlessedStdoutWrite(). stream-json unchanged.
- [-] `Fixed /usage-credits starting a re-login for Team and Enterprise admins instead of pointing to the organization's usage settings page` — SKIP (claude.ai Team/Enterprise admin + /usage-credits, not our flow)
- [-] `Fixed /autofix-pr reporting "cannot run on the default branch" when the session is inside a git worktree or another repository` — N/A (assessed 2026-06-03): our `/autofix-pr` (src/commands/autofix-pr/autofix-pr.ts) is a ~10-line prompt-launcher stub that just emits "Launching autofix…" + `shouldQuery` — it has no branch / default-branch / worktree detection logic, so the upstream bug surface (the branch check that errors) doesn't exist in this fork.
- [-] (7ddb669) `Fixed --resume picker not showing sessions from the current directory when it isn't a git worktree (e.g., jj workspaces)` — N/A (already handled): in a non-git/jj dir `getWorktreePathsPortable` returns `[]`, so `gatherProjectCandidates` falls through to the single-dir scan of `findProjectDir(canonicalDir)`, which lists current-dir sessions. Verified during 7ddb669 batch; no code change needed.
- [-] `Fixed Windows hooks that invoke bash explicitly (e.g., /usr/bin/bash script.sh) failing with "command not found" or "cannot execute binary file"` — SKIP (Windows-specific)
- [-] `Fixed OpenTelemetry log events (user_prompt, api_request, tool_result, tool_decision) being silently dropped when emitted before telemetry initialization completed` — SKIP (telemetry neutralized in this fork)
- [x] (7ddb669) `Fixed claude mcp list/get/add printing secrets to the terminal: ${VAR} references are no longer expanded, and credential headers and URL secrets are redacted` — TODO (security; we have claude mcp list/get/add)
- [x] (7ddb669) `Fixed Workflow agents spawned with isolation: "worktree" in background sessions being blocked from editing files inside their own worktree` — TODO (we have dynamic workflows + worktree isolation in bg sessions)
- [-] `Fixed background sessions dispatched from claude agents booting on a stale model from the daemon's environment instead of the model in settings.json` — N/A mostly (assessed 2026-06-03): in this fork `ANTHROPIC_MODEL` is authoritative-by-design (set in the container `.env`) and `getUserSpecifiedModelSetting()` (utils/model/model.ts) ranks it ABOVE `settings.model`. `claude --bg` (cli/bg.ts) spawns from the live foreground env (`...process.env`) so the bg session resolves the SAME model — no staleness. The only staleness window is the long-lived supervised daemon (daemon/main.ts, frozen start-time env) for settings.json-model users who set no `ANTHROPIC_MODEL` — an edge case for our deployment. A fork-correct fix (forward the resolved model as `--model`) is possible but low-value and would risk the `.env` path. Revisit only if a settings.json-model user reports it.
- [x] (7ddb669) `Fixed a potential crash when rendering Write tool results after resuming a session` — TODO (general render crash on resume)
- [-] `Fixed completed subagents getting stuck showing as running when an error occurs while finalizing their result` — N/A already-handled (assessed 2026-06-03): we have the gh-20236 backport. In both finalize paths (agentToolUtils.ts:625-715 async lifecycle + AgentTool.tsx:1051-1131 backgrounded), `completeAsyncAgent` fires BEFORE the throwable embellishments (classifyHandoffIfNeeded / getWorktreeResult), and every catch path calls `failAsyncAgent`/`killAsyncAgent` (which set a terminal status when status is still 'running') BEFORE worktree cleanup. No path leaves status stuck at 'running'.
- [x] (7ddb669) `Fixed EADDRINUSE errors from tools that bind Unix sockets under $TMPDIR when CLAUDE_CODE_TMPDIR is set to a deep path` — TODO (we have UDS pipes/sockets)
- [-] `Improved terminal rendering performance by stabilizing the layout engine's JIT compilation profile` — SKIP (Ink layout-engine perf internals; "Don't Touch" rendering area, no source diff)
- [-] `Improved rendering performance for large file writes` — SKIP/already-optimized (assessed 2026-06-03). Our FileWriteTool create-result renderer (`src/tools/FileWriteTool/UI.tsx`) already does exactly this: caps the preview at `MAX_LINES_TO_RENDER = 10` + truncates long lines at `MAX_LINE_CHARS = 500` before passing to `HighlightedCode`, memoizes the syntax-highlight + truncation via the React-compiler cache (only recompute when `content`/`verbose`/`width` change), and shows `… +N lines` + Ctrl+O for the rest. So large writes never re-highlight thousands of lines per frame. (The Edit/Update diff path is intentionally uncapped — full diff visibility — but its highlighting is WeakMap-cached in `StructuredDiff`, so it's not a per-frame cost either, and that's a separate component from "file writes".)
- [-] `[VSCode] Added a tip suggesting disabling terminal GPU acceleration (or running /terminal-setup) to fix garbled glyphs` — SKIP (VSCode extension-specific)

## 2.1.160

- [x] (7ddb669) `Added a prompt before writing to shell startup files (.zshenv, .zlogin, .bash_login) and ~/.config/git/, which could otherwise lead to unintended command execution` — TODO (security; write-permission prompt for shell-rc/git-config paths)
- [x] (7ddb669) `acceptEdits mode now prompts before writing build-tool config files that grant code execution (.npmrc, .yarnrc*, bunfig.toml, .bazelrc, .pre-commit-config.yaml, .devcontainer/, etc.)` — TODO (security; acceptEdits build-config prompt)
- [x] (7ddb669) `Edit no longer requires a separate Read after viewing a file with grep: single-file grep/egrep/fgrep commands now satisfy the read-before-edit check` — TODO (read-before-edit relaxation for grep-viewed files)
- [-] `Fixed copy-on-select not writing to the Windows clipboard on WSL — now uses PowerShell interop instead of OSC 52, which terminals like MobaXterm don't support` — SKIP (copy-on-select clipboard path; Windows/WSL-specific, our agents-view has no copy-on-select)
- [-] `Fixed restoring a completed session from claude agents dropping chat history and re-running the original prompt` — N/A by architecture (assessed 2026-06-03): our agents-view restore is `tmux attach-session` (cli/bg.ts attachHandler + cli/handlers/agentsView.tsx). A completed/exited session's tmux is gone → it errors cleanly ("Tmux session … not found. The session may have exited.") and NEVER re-spawns `claude <prompt>`. The upstream failure mode (drop history + re-run prompt) is structurally impossible. (Possible future enhancement, not the bug: a `claude --resume <sid>` fallback to view/resume a completed bg session's transcript.)
- [-] `Fixed background sessions re-attached after overnight retire losing their conversation and re-running the original prompt` — N/A by architecture (assessed 2026-06-03): same as the completed-session restore above — re-attach is `tmux attach-session`; idleReaper.ts "retire" only changes the displayed status (the tmux session stays alive), and a truly-gone session errors cleanly rather than re-spawning the prompt. No re-run path exists.
- [ ] `Fixed claude --bg occasionally failing with "socket missing" when the background daemon was cold-starting on a loaded machine` — TODO (we have --bg + background daemon)
- [-] `Fixed an issue on Windows where the directory a background session was started in could not be deleted after claude rm until the background daemon exited` — SKIP (Windows-specific)
- [-] `Fixed background agents that resumed work being shown under Completed in the agents list` — N/A (assessed 2026-06-03): the agents-list job status comes from the job-state classifier (query/stopHooks.ts → jobs/classifier.ts), gated on `feature('TEMPLATES')` from 'bun:bundle' = false in source mode (empirically verified) → it never runs, so a job is never set to 'complete' by it and can't get stuck there. IF the classifier is live in compiled binaries, the precise fix is a turn-start "mark running on resume" in query.ts queryLoop (flip terminal→'running' when CLAUDE_JOB_DIR set + repl_main_thread + !agentId, since classifyState only runs at Stop). Recorded pending confirmation of binary-mode gating.
- [x] `Fixed claude agents freezing for several seconds when returning to the session list due to the auto-updater re-checking on every exit` — DONE (22422b3): root cause was `getLatestVersion()` hitting the GitHub Releases API (5s timeout, no caching) on every `AutoUpdater` mount — the component runs an initial `checkForUpdates()` on mount, so returning to the agents-view session list in the shipped binary remounted it and fired a fresh network round-trip each time. Added a per-process 30-min TTL cache (matching the background re-check interval) to `getLatestVersion()`; successes reused within the window so remounts are instant, failures NOT cached so a transient blip retries next mount. `claude update` CLI is a one-shot process so it still fetches fresh. Source mode was already gated off via `AutoUpdaterWrapper`, so this only affected binary users.
- [-] `Fixed Esc, arrow keys, and typing becoming unresponsive on Windows when attached to a background session or in the agent view while the host is under heavy CPU load` — SKIP (Windows-specific)
- [-] `Fixed background agents emitting terminal sync-output markers to terminals that don't support them (Apple Terminal, tmux), causing render artifacts when entering a running agent` — N/A (already handled): isSynchronizedOutputSupported() (src/ink/terminal.ts) returns false for tmux (TMUX env) and zellij, and Apple Terminal is not in the allowlist, so SYNC_OUTPUT_SUPPORTED is false and BSU/ESU are never emitted there (writeDiffToTerminal/ink.tsx gate on it). bg agents run in tmux → already suppressed.
- [-] (3e606df) `Fixed mouse wheel scrolling prompt history instead of the transcript right after opening a session from the agents list` — N/A (verified via ui-driver): in our fork's fullscreen attached REPL mouse-wheel scrolls the transcript correctly (4 wheel-ups moved viewport L37-L40 → L33-L36, scenarios/wheel-direct.json). Our agents-view attach is `exec tmux attach` (fresh process, normal mouse-tracking init) — structurally unlike upstream's in-process view-switch that left mouse tracking off — so the bug doesn't apply. Harness: tools/ui-driver/seed-attachable-session.sh.
- [ ] `Fixed CJK IME composition appearing at the bottom-left of the screen instead of at the input caret in the claude agents view` — TODO (we have agents-view + IME input)
- [-] `Fixed valid file:///C:/... links being rewritten to a broken path on Windows terminals with hyperlink support` — SKIP (Windows-specific path rewriting)
- [-] `Fixed voice mode failing to connect when the project directory or branch name contains non-ASCII or special characters` — N/A (assessed 2026-06-03): our default voice mode is the LOCAL faster-whisper backend (src/utils/voiceLocal/), whose worker is spawned with a fixed `cwd: getInstallPath()` + a modelName arg — no project-dir/branch is embedded in any connection/URL, so the path-encoding "fails to connect" surface doesn't exist. (The legacy Anthropic voice_stream path behind VOICE_STREAM_BASE_URL is off by default.)
- [-] `Fixed the auto mode unavailability message on third-party providers (Bedrock/Vertex/Foundry) to point to the CLAUDE_CODE_ENABLE_AUTO_MODE opt-in instead of incorrectly blaming the model` — SKIP (provider-specific auto-mode messaging)
- [-] `Fixed /effort ultracode incorrectly blaming the dynamic workflows setting when the model cannot run xhigh; ultracode is no longer offered on models that do not support it` — N/A: in this fork ultracode is a provider-agnostic native toggle (xhigh effort + dynamic-workflows nudge), NOT the upstream Anthropic xhigh-API gate. Gating it on modelSupportsXhighEffort would wrongly couple a fork-native feature to an Anthropic capability check (attempted 2026-06-03, reverted). See coffeegrind123/openclaude:context/3p-provider-gate-audit.md.
- [x] (7ddb669) `Fixed model-not-found errors suggesting --model when running via the SDK or other hosts where the CLI flag doesn't apply` — TODO (assess; SDK/host model-not-found messaging)
- [x] (e5d7e80) `Fixed Claude's past replies disappearing from scrollback when resuming a brief mode session with brief mode turned off` — DONE: Messages.tsx gates dropTextInBriefTurns on briefModeEnabled() (isBriefEnabled) so resumed brief turns keep their assistant text when brief mode is off.
- [x] (7ddb669) `Fixed vim mode p pasting on the line below instead of at the cursor when the register was yanked with v$` — TODO (we have vim mode)
- [ ] `Improved performance of opening recently-inactive background agent sessions in claude agents` — TODO (assess; agents-view open perf)
- [-] `Improved auto mode classifier latency by reducing reasoning on routine actions, lowering the chance of "could not evaluate this action" blocks` — SKIP (auto mode classifier; Anthropic-gated feature not in our flow)
- [x] (7ddb669) `Improved background-session teardown (claude rm/stop, idle reap) to send SIGTERM to running shell subprocesses before SIGKILL, so cleanup handlers run` — TODO (we have bg teardown + idle reap)
- [-] `Removed CLAUDE_CODE_OPUS_4_6_FAST_MODE_OVERRIDE; the environment variable is now a no-op` — SKIP (env-var removal; Anthropic-internal fast-mode override)
- [-] `Removed the JetBrains plugin install suggestion from startup` — SKIP (JetBrains-specific)
- [x] `Renamed the dynamic-workflow trigger keyword from workflow to ultracode. The word "workflow" no longer triggers a run; asking for one in your own words still works. The trigger keyword is highlighted in violet in the prompt input` — DONE (`5ebee1a`) Mapped onto our model-driven workflow engine. New `findUltracodeTriggerPositions`/`hasUltracodeKeyword` (reusing the shared `findKeywordTriggerPositions` helper in `ultraplan/keyword.ts` — skips questions/slash-commands/paths/quoted occurrences). New `ultracode_directive` attachment injected per-turn when the user's turn contains the keyword AND `isDynamicWorkflowsEnabled()`, rendered as a system-reminder nudging the model to orchestrate a Workflow for that turn (keyword equivalent of `/effort ultracode`, one turn; mirrors the `ultrathink_effort` attachment path; null-rendering in UI). Violet highlight in PromptInput via an `ultracodeTriggers` memo + a new dedicated `violet` theme key (electric violet `rgb(135,0,255)` / `ansi:magenta`) across all 6 theme objects — `rainbow_violet` (rgb 200,130,180) quantized to white in 16-color terminals. System-prompt nudge updated so "ultracode" is the trigger word and the bare "workflow" no longer triggers; notice + cheatsheet updated. ui-driver scenario `ultracode-keyword-highlight` verified the keyword renders fg:13 (bright magenta/violet) while the rest of the prompt stays default; 4 unit tests. Note: distinct from `/effort ultracode` (persistent xhigh+orchestrate toggle) — the keyword is a per-turn directive.

## 2.1.157

- [x] `Plugins in .claude/skills directories are now automatically loaded, no marketplace required` — DONE 9f0e83f (2026-05-30). `loadLocalSkillsDirPlugins()` scans project `.claude/skills/` (getOriginalCwd) + user `~/.claude/skills/` for subdirs carrying a manifest (`.claude-plugin/plugin.json` or legacy `plugin.json`) and loads each via `createPluginFromPath`; SKILL.md-only dirs are left to the skill loader. Enabled state respects manifest `defaultEnabled` (2.1.154). Added a 4th `local` source to `mergePluginSources` (precedence session > local > marketplace > builtin; managed settings still win) wired concurrently into `assemblePluginLoadResult` so full + cache-only loads both see it. `.claude/skills/` is already auto-loaded-from-cwd in this fork (SKILL.md skills), so this is consistent with the existing posture, not a new trust surface. 6 merge-precedence unit tests + end-to-end container fixture (manifest plugin loads, SKILL.md-only dir skipped, fork boots clean).
- [x] `Added claude plugin init <name> to scaffold a new plugin in .claude/skills` — DONE fb1ae6a (2026-05-30). `pluginInitHandler` scaffolds `.claude/skills/<name>/` with `.claude-plugin/plugin.json` + `commands/` (+ example.md) + `agents/` + `skills/` + README; validates the name (single identifier-ish segment, no traversal), refuses to overwrite, `--dir` to scaffold elsewhere. Registered as `plugin init <name>`. Composes with the autoload above. Verified end-to-end: init scaffolds → plugin auto-loads on next boot → re-running init refuses to overwrite.
- [x] `Added autocomplete for /plugin arguments: subcommands, installed plugin names, and plugins from known marketplaces` — DONE e4e8adc (2026-05-31). Built the command-*argument*-completion substrate the fork lacked: `CommandBase.getArgumentSuggestions(argsBeforeCursor) => ArgumentSuggestion[]` (types/command.ts). `commandSuggestions.ts` gains `generateArgumentSuggestions()` (builds SuggestionItems for the token under the cursor, filters candidates by the partial, precomputes the resulting input); `generateCommandSuggestions` routes into it once past the command name; `applyCommandSuggestion` inserts the completion (replaces the partial token, trailing space, no auto-submit). `useTypeahead.tsx`: the exact-match-with-args gate (which previously suppressed all suggestions, hint-only) now falls through to suggestion generation when the matched command provides candidates. `/plugin` provider (`commands/plugin/pluginArgSuggestions.ts`, wired in `index.tsx`): subcommands (install/enable/disable/uninstall/manage/validate/marketplace/help) + installed plugin names for enable/disable/uninstall (sync `getInMemoryInstalledPlugins`) + marketplace actions (add/remove/update/list). VERIFIED 3 ways: 13 unit tests; deterministic integration with the real plugin command; visual ui-driver E2E (`/plugin marketplace ` renders add/remove/update/list in the footer). NOTE: `install <partial>` marketplace-name completion is the one piece deferred — it needs the async marketplace cache, which doesn't fit the per-keystroke sync provider; subcommands + installed-name completion (the bulk of the upstream item) are done.
- [-] `claude agents: the agent field in settings.json is now honored for dispatched sessions, with --agent <name> to override it` — SKIP (already have — our fork ships the `agent` settings field (settings/types.ts:779) AND the `--agent <agent>` flag (main.tsx:1089, help: "Agent for the current session. Overrides the 'agent' setting."); main-thread `--agent` sessions wire the agent system prompt (main.tsx:2997, print.ts:720-730). The "dispatched sessions" specific to upstream's agents-view dispatch input is N/A — our agents-view has no dispatch input (only peek/attach/inline-reply); `--bg` sessions re-exec claude which reads the same settings, so they already honor `agent`)
- [x] `EnterWorktree can now switch between Claude-managed worktrees mid-session` — DONE 3eca27b (2026-05-30). The unconditional `Already in a worktree session` throw ran BEFORE the path-switch branch, so switching from worktree A→B was impossible. Moved the guard to gate only the create branch; a `path` switch is now permitted while already inside a Claude-managed worktree. Preserve the true pre-worktree `originalCwd` across A→B switches (so ExitWorktree returns to the real original, not the worktree we came from). Also fixed a latent bug surfaced by this: the path-switch branch only persisted via `saveWorktreeState` (disk) and never set the in-memory `currentWorktreeSession`, so `ExitWorktree`/`keepWorktree` could not leave a path-switched worktree — now calls `restoreWorktreeSession()`. Build clean; fork boots + EnterWorktree tool loads (container smoke).
- [-] `tool_decision telemetry events now include tool_parameters (bash commands, MCP/skill names) when OTEL_LOG_TOOL_DETAILS=1` — SKIP (telemetry neutralized in this fork; tool_decision events aren't emitted to Anthropic's pipeline)
- [-] `Worktrees managed by Claude are now left unlocked when the agent finishes, so git worktree remove/prune can clean them up` — SKIP (N/A — our fork never runs `git worktree lock`. createAgentWorktree/createWorktreeForSession use `git worktree add`/`remove --force` only; the only "lock" in worktree.ts is the in-process `gitWorktreeMutationLock` mutex. Worktrees are already always unlocked, so `git worktree remove`/`prune` already work — nothing to unlock)
- [x] `Fixed unprocessable images (zero-byte, corrupt) attached via paste, MCP, or dialog crashing the request instead of becoming a text placeholder` — DONE b1a92a1 (2026-05-30). `maybeResizeAndDownsampleImageBuffer` throws `ImageResizeError` on a zero-byte buffer (before sharp) and on large/over-dimension corrupt images; that throw propagated out of `attachments.ts#buildImageContentBlocks` (paste/dialog) and the two `mcp/client.ts` image paths (tool `image` result + `resource` blob), failing the whole request. Wrapped each in try/catch → emits a `[Unprocessable image: …]` text block while the rest of the message/tool-result proceeds; widened `buildImageContentBlocks` return type to `ContentBlockParam[]`. Note: small corrupt images were already passed through gracefully by the resizer's catch-fallback (returns the original buffer when base64 ≤5MB and not over-dim), so only the throwing cases needed this. Zero-byte paste content is filtered earlier by `isValidImagePaste` (content.length>0). 1 test (zero-byte throw precondition) + 45 existing attachments tests green; container smoke OK.
- [-] `Fixed sandbox network permission prompts appearing in auto and bypass-permissions mode when using the desktop app, IDE extensions, or SDK` — SKIP (desktop-app / IDE-extension / SDK surfaces we don't run)
- [-] `Fixed claude agents completed sessions not retiring when an idle subagent was still parked or had leaked a backgrounded shell` — SKIP (N/A — our session status is classifier-derived; complete/error/timeout/stopped are already retired (idleReaper.ts comment, only "working" states are reaped). We don't have upstream's parked-subagent pool or the leaked-bg-shell mechanism that keeps a completed session alive — there's no code path where a completed session stays in the active group)
- [-] `Fixed claude agents pressing Esc not cancelling a slow "opening…", leaving the list unresponsive` — SKIP (N/A — no "opening…" async state in our agents-view (grep of src/components/agents-view found none). Attach is synchronous (Enter), there is no slow-open dispatch flow with an Esc gap to fix)
- [-] `Fixed background agent worktrees under .claude/worktrees/ being orphaned after the 30-day job retention sweep` — SKIP (N/A — our fork has no 30-day job-retention sweep. The only job retention is a 7-day *display* filter in templateJobs.ts (`cutoff = Date.now() - 7d`, hides old rows, deletes nothing) and bg.ts unlinks stale PID files; nothing removes `.claude/jobs/<id>` state on a timer, and there is no sweep that touches `.claude/worktrees/`. No orphaning path exists)
- [-] `Fixed background sessions re-attached after a sleep/wake not telling the model the correct date` — SKIP (already handled by two existing mechanisms; surface opaque enough that a speculative fix risks the expensive cache regeneration the code explicitly guards against). (1) Per-turn `getDateChangeAttachments` (attachments.ts:1485, called from the turn attachment builder at :844) compares `getLocalISODate()` vs in-process `getLastEmittedDate()` (STATE.lastEmittedDate); for a same-process sleep/wake (tmux `--bg`), `lastEmittedDate` is still yesterday's so the first turn after wake emits a `date_change` tail attachment ("The date has changed. Today's date is now …", messages.ts:4440) — without touching messages[0], deliberately, since clearing the cached prefix would re-bill ~920K tokens (per the comment at attachments.ts:1476-1481). (2) On a cold `--resume` the per-process-memoized `getUserContext` recomputes `currentDate: Today's date is ${getLocalISODate()}` (context.ts:191) = today, so messages[0] is already correct. Revisit if the upstream source diff reveals a concrete gap in a bg-turn path that bypasses the per-turn attachment builder.
- [-] `Fixed copy-on-select in claude agents not reaching the system clipboard inside tmux with set-clipboard on (regression in 2.1.153)` — SKIP (N/A — regression of upstream 2.1.153; our fork is based on 2.1.88 and never had it. Our agents-view has no copy-on-select / OSC-52 clipboard code (grep of src/components/agents-view found none) — row selection is a `›` pointer, not a terminal text-selection-to-clipboard)
- [x] `Fixed --resume not reporting background subagents that were running when the previous Claude Code process exited` — DONE 283172a (2026-05-30). Background subagents (Agent/Task with `run_in_background`) run in-process and die with the parent; their tool_use is left unresolved and `filterUnresolvedToolUses` silently drops the assistant message on resume, so the user never learned the work was interrupted. New `findInterruptedBackgroundAgents()` (conversationRecovery.ts) detects unresolved background-agent tool_uses (name ∈ {Agent, Task} + `run_in_background:true` + no matching tool_result) BEFORE the filter runs; `deserializeMessagesWithInterruptDetection` then appends a display-only `system` notice listing them ("N background subagents were running when Claude Code last exited and did not complete: …"). `system` messages are stripped before the API (no API-validity impact) but render in the transcript. Appended after the interrupt-detection + API-validity sentinel so neither is affected. 6 unit tests + 76 messages.test.ts green (no regression) + container integration harness on the real deserialize path emits the notice.
- [x] `Fixed the --resume session picker leaving its contents on the terminal after exiting in fullscreen mode` — DONE 110d9d1 (2026-05-30, ui-driver-VERIFIED). The `--resume` CLI picker (`launchResumeChooser` → `renderAndRun(<App><ResumeConversation/></App>)`) renders `LogSelector` in the MAIN buffer (`App.tsx` has no `AlternateScreen`); on select the REPL enters the alt screen (DEC 1049), which restores the saved main-buffer contents when fullscreen later exits → residue. Fix: `ResumeConversation.onSelect` emits `\x1B[2J\x1B[H` (clear visible screen + home, NOT scrollback `\x1B[3J`) synchronously in the main buffer just before `setResumeData` mounts the REPL, gated on `isFullscreenEnvEnabled()`. VERIFICATION (ui-driver, before/after): solved the harness gap (picker needs ~40s to render; a `-p` seed is dropped as isNonInteractive unless its `entrypoint` is rewritten sdk-cli→claude — automated in `tools/ui-driver/make-resume-seed.sh`). WITHOUT the fix the fullscreen after-exit snapshot restores `[OC]…`+`✢ Resuming conversation…` residue (xterm.js headless DOES model DEC 1049 main-buffer restore); WITH the fix only the clean exit hint remains. Scenario `scenarios/resume-residue-fullscreen.json`; tooling/verify shipped in 1f2f0bd. Non-fullscreen + non-resume paths unaffected (gated).
- [x] `Fixed --worktree and --worktree --tmux returning to the canonical repo root instead of the current linked worktree` — DONE 63c6c89 (2026-05-30). Inferred from code structure (no upstream diff): the `--worktree`/`--worktree --tmux` path chdir's to the main repo root in `setup.ts` before `createWorktreeForSession` (so `git worktree add` creates under the main repo, not nested in the linked worktree). That chdir made `worktree.baseRef:"head"` resolve HEAD against the canonical root via `resolveGitDir()` (=getCwd()) — re-breaking the 1a619ce fix for the CLI path. Threaded an optional `baseRefCwd` through `createWorktreeForSession → getOrCreateWorktree → doCreateWorktreeLocked` (defaults to `getCwd()`, so EnterWorktree/Agent-isolation/subagent callers are byte-identical); `setup.ts` captures the pre-chdir cwd and passes it, so HEAD resolves against the worktree the user launched from. Only affects users with `worktree.baseRef:"head"` (non-default; default is `"fresh"`) launching `--worktree` from inside a linked worktree. Build clean; fork boots (container smoke). NOTE: inferred-intent fix — verify against the eventual upstream source diff (the changelog text "returning to the canonical repo root" maps to this HEAD-resolution path; if upstream meant the session's *final* cwd instead, revisit).
- [x] `Fixed the /model picker showing an incorrect "Newer version available" hint when the selected model is already the newest in its family; the pinned-model row now shows the model's description instead of its raw ID` — DONE af5387b (2026-05-30). Root cause in `getKnownModelOption` (modelOptions.ts): it showed the upgrade hint whenever the pinned model's marketing name `!==` the alias's currently-resolved name — flagging a model that is NEWER than a lagging alias default (e.g. pinned Opus 4.8 while `getDefaultOpusModel()` still returns 4.7) and telling the user to "select Opus for Opus 4.7" (a downgrade). Fix: added `modelVersionTuple()`/`isStrictlyNewer()` (extract ordered numeric version from the canonical name; same-family recency compare) and gated the hint on `isStrictlyNewer(aliasDefault, pinnedModel)`; otherwise show `getPublicModelDisplayName(model) ?? marketingName` instead of the raw model ID. Required adding the missing `claude-opus-4-8` case to `firstPartyNameToCanonical` (it collapsed to `claude-opus-4`, erasing the "8" so ordering was wrong) + `claude-opus-4-8`/`claude-opus-4-7` cases to `getMarketingNameForModel` (4.7 previously fell through to "Opus 4"). 6 unit tests in `modelVersionCompare.test.ts`. Note: the exact upstream trigger (Opus 4.8 in catalog) isn't fully reachable here since `getModelStrings()` tops out at opus47, but the latent `!==`-vs-ordering bug + raw-ID display are fixed and future-proofed against the next model launch.
- [-] `Fixed literal markdown markers (backticks, asterisks) appearing in the in-progress message text in fullscreen mode` — SKIP (DOES-NOT-REPRO, ui-driver-confirmed 2026-05-30). Static read: the in-progress streaming preview (`streamingText` prop, REPL.tsx:5160) renders via `<StreamingMarkdown>` (Messages.tsx:821-830) → stable prefix `<Markdown>` + unstable suffix `<Markdown streaming>` (the `streaming` flag only downgrades TABLE tokens to raw text, never bold/code); that block is a SIBLING after the virtualized-vs-flat ternary so it renders identically in fullscreen and non-fullscreen. CONFIRMED via authed fullscreen ui-driver run (new tooling `tools/ui-driver/snap-authed.sh`, CLAUDE_CODE_NO_FLICKER=1, GLM-4.7 via z.ai, 30-line verbatim-markdown reply, 16 mid-stream snapshots): every streaming frame rendered the markers — `^[[1malpha^[[0m` (bold), `^[[97mbeta_fn()^[[0m` (code), 17× `[3m` italic SGR in one frame — with ZERO literal-marker hits (`**alpha**`/`` `beta_fn` ``) across all in-progress frames. Our fork already routes the in-progress text through StreamingMarkdown; the upstream fullscreen-specific raw-text path doesn't exist here.
- [-] `Fixed the terminal freezing after approving the managed-settings security dialog at startup` — SKIP (enterprise managed-settings security dialog; our fork sets CLAUDE_AUTO_TRUST=1 and doesn't surface that dialog)
- [ ] `Fixed a rare duplicate line appearing in scrollback after the terminal UI redraws` — TODO/DEFER (assessed 2026-05-30; no blind change). The duplicate-line-after-redraw lives in Ink's core frame-commit/scrollback pipeline (`src/ink/frame.ts`, `log-update.ts`, `renderer.ts`, `ink.tsx`) — the explicit "Don't Touch: terminal rendering correctness" area, with every-frame blast radius. Upstream calls it "rare", i.e. intermittent — so even with the now-working ui-driver (`includeScrollback:true` snapshots can inspect stale scrollback frames) there's no deterministic trigger to repro against, and no upstream source diff to anchor the exact off-by-one in the commit/diff logic. A speculative change to the frame-commit/scrollback-diff path risks introducing widespread rendering glitches for an uncertain, rare fix. Needs the upstream diff (to pin the exact redraw condition) OR a deterministic repro (a specific redraw sequence — resize mid-stream? static-region flush? — that reliably duplicates a line) before touching the pipeline. NEXT STEP if revisited: instrument `log-update.ts`/`frame.ts` commit path to log emitted scrollback lines, then sweep ui-driver resize/stream scenarios with `includeScrollback:true` looking for a repeated line.
- [-] `Fixed right-click paste duplicating the clipboard in the VS Code, Cursor, and Windsurf integrated terminals` — SKIP (VS Code / Cursor / Windsurf integrated-terminal specific)
- [-] `WSL: fixed image paste (alt+v keybinding), screenshot paste on Windows 11, and added support for dragging images from Windows Explorer` — SKIP (WSL/Windows-specific)
- [~] `Improved performance of long and resumed conversations by eliminating redundant message-rendering recomputations` — PARTIAL/likely-already-addressed (assessed 2026-05-30; no blind change made). Our `Messages.tsx` render pipeline is already heavily memoized — the expensive derivation (`normalizeMessages` → compact-boundary → dedup → reorder → brief/focus filter → grouping → collapse → `buildMessageLookups`) is a single deps-tracked `useMemo` (lines ~530-610, deps `[verbose, normalizedMessages, isTranscriptMode, syntheticStreamingToolUseMessages, shouldTruncate, tools, isBriefOnly, isFocusView]`); `normalizedMessages` (`normalizeMessages(messages).filter`) is memoized on `[messages]`; `renderableMessages` is a memoized slice-cap; `normalizedToolUseIDs`/`streamingToolUseIDs` are precomputed Sets (O(k) not O(n×k) per the inline comments); the Markdown lexer has a module-level token cache. The only per-render work is `renderableMessages.flatMap(renderMessageRow)` over the capped/sliced array (the actual JSX, necessary). No obvious remaining unmemoized recompute-over-all-messages exists. The specific upstream redundant-recompute can't be pinpointed without the diff, and a blind memoization/deps change here risks stale-render bugs (the path has a documented history of RSS-regression + UUID-growth fixes). Revisit with the upstream source diff to confirm whether the exact recompute is already eliminated here or lives in a spot we haven't matched.

## 2.1.156

- [-] `Fixed an issue when using Opus 4.8 where thinking blocks were modified, leading to API errors.` — SKIP (assessed 2026-05-29: upstream hotfix for a regression they introduced in their 2.1.154 Opus-4.8 normalization rewrite (lean-prompt-default + effort-param paths). Our message-normalization is a different lineage — 2.1.88 base + selective backports incl. the 2.1.136 trailing-thinking reorder — and does not share that code path. Verified our paths never mutate a thinking block's `.thinking`/`.signature`: (1) for Anthropic/Opus 4.8 the client-side thinking pruner is disabled — `keepTurns = -1` in `normalizeMessagesForAPI` (messages.ts:2516-2528) since `isFirstPartyAnthropicBaseUrl()` is true; (2) assistant-message serialization spreads each block verbatim via `..._` and explicitly skips appending `cache_control` to `thinking`/`redacted_thinking` blocks (claude.ts:716-727); (3) `reorderTrailingThinkingInMiddleAssistants` (2.1.136 backport) + `filterTrailingThinkingFromLastAssistant` (messages.ts:5056-5145) only MOVE or DROP whole blocks by reference — stable-partition / slice — never rewrite block content or signature; (4) `filterOrphanedThinkingOnlyMessages` already drops mismatched-signature orphans that would otherwise 400. No equivalent "thinking blocks modified" defect exists. Minor adjacent finding (not this bug, not fixed this pass to keep blast radius zero): `getCachedSignature` is imported in claude.ts:65 but never called — dead import, harmless.)

## 2.1.154

- [x] `Opus 4.8 is here! Now defaults to high effort · /effort xhigh for your hardest tasks` — TODO (model availability is Anthropic-side / provider-gated, but the new `/effort xhigh` level is the actionable part — wire the extra effort tier into the slider + resolver) — DONE (xhigh tier already present in our fork: EFFORT_LEVELS + slider + resolveAppliedEffort clamp + descriptions; verified pre-existing. Opus 4.8 model availability is provider-side. Slider-label half landed in 451a0f2)
- [x] `Introducing dynamic workflows: ask Claude to create a workflow and it orchestrates work across tens to hundreds of agents in the background, so you can take on larger, more complex tasks. Run /workflows to view your runs` — DONE 2026-05-29 (engine + Workflow tool + ultracode + F1 worktree isolation + F2 live progress tree + F3 cron scheduling + F4 headless prompt-free cron execution — all shipped).  (implemented 2026-05-29, pending commit. Built the full engine in `src/services/workflows/`: a soft capability sandbox (`sandbox.ts` — model-authored JS via AsyncFunction with the host API + blocked globals; Date.now/Math.random/argless new Date throw for determinism), the host API `agent()/parallel()/pipeline()/phase()/log()/workflow()` + `args`/`budget` (`run.ts`, built on `runForkedAgent` — each subagent isolated, returns only its result), concurrency cap min(16,cpu-2) + 1000-agent backstop (`concurrency.ts`), schema via cross-provider JSON-block parse (`agentExec.ts`), persistence + journal-based `resumeFromRunId` prefix-replay (`persistence.ts`/`index.ts`), and one-level `workflow()` composition. REPLACED the legacy `.md` WorkflowTool: the model-facing tool is now named `Workflow` (matches upstream) and runs scripts in the background (runId + task-notification on completion); test-and-fix/lint-fix migrated to bundled `.js`. `/effort ultracode` toggle sets xhigh + flips `ultracodeEnabled` so the model proactively orchestrates; system-prompt nudge in `getOpenClaudeSection`. `/workflows` lists saved workflows + recent runs. Default-on with `dynamicWorkflowsEnabled` config + `disableDynamicWorkflows` managed-settings disable + first-run-per-session confirmation. 24 unit tests + in-container engine E2E (parallel fan-out + pipeline + budget verified). FOLLOW-UPS SHIPPED same day: (F1) agent({isolation:'worktree'}) now creates a worktree (`createAgentWorktree`) and scopes the forked agent's cwd via `runWithCwdOverride` (AsyncLocalStorage, the same mechanism AgentTool uses — verified in-container that getCwd() propagates across awaits + restores; worktree notice prepended; reaped if unchanged; graceful in-place fallback outside a git repo). (F2) /workflows is now a live `local-jsx` view rendering a phase-grouped tree (phases→agents with glyphs/tokens/timings), auto-refreshing every 1s while a run is active. (F3) cron: `CronTask` gained a structured `workflow` field (round-tripped) + `scheduleWorkflowCron()`/`listScheduledWorkflows()` in `services/workflows/cron.ts`; scheduled workflows surface in /workflows. (F4) headless prompt-free cron execution: `services/workflows/headless.ts` `tryRunScheduledWorkflow(task)` launches the engine DIRECTLY when a workflow cron task fires (zero model tokens for dispatch), reusing `getLastCacheSafeParams()` (the session's last-turn context — full ToolUseContext + exact context bytes, so the run also shares the prompt cache). Wired into both firing sites (`print.ts` onFireTask + `useScheduledTasks.ts`); graceful prompt fallback when no turn has run yet. Verified in-container: dispatch returns true + writes the run dir + script.js with no model turn. NO remaining gaps. 27 unit tests total. Pending commit to the openclaude repo.)

- [x] `claude agents: type ! <command> to run a shell command as a background session you can attach to and detach from. Also available as claude --bg --exec '<command>'` — DONE (`ad2f394`). Both surfaces shipped. (1) `--exec <command>` (and `--exec=<command>`) parsed in `handleBgFlag` (`src/cli/bg.ts`); new exported `spawnBackgroundExecSession()` spawns the raw command in a detached tmux session (`( cmd ) 2>&1 | tee logPath` — subshell wrap so `;`-separated commands log their full output, not just the last segment) and writes the registry file directly via `writeExecSessionFile()` (raw shells can't self-register through `concurrentSessions.ts`, which only runs inside a claude process). KEY DESIGN: an exec session's PID is a shell (`bash`/`npm`/…), so `isLikelyClaudeProcess` (greps `/proc/<pid>/cmdline` for `claude`/`cli.tsx`) would sweep it as stale — exec sessions are tracked by **tmux session liveness** instead (`isExecSessionAlive` → `tmux has-session`, wrapped in unified `isSessionAlive` routing on `kind==='exec'`), wired into all four filter sites (`listLiveSessions`/`findSession`/`cleanStaleFiles`/`psHandler`). New `SessionInfo.tmuxSession` records the real tmux target so attach/logs/kill use `tmuxSession ?? name` (the existing `--name` path conflated the user label with the tmux target; the `?? name` fallback preserves exact prior claude-session behavior). `'exec'` added to `SessionKind` (`concurrentSessions.ts`). (2) agents-view `!`-prefix (`src/components/agents-view/AgentsPanel.tsx`): new `exec` view mode mirroring the filter input (`!` enters it, type a command, Enter spawns via the same `spawnBackgroundExecSession`, Esc cancels), footer hint + `HelpOverlay` entry added. `sessionMerge.ts` renders exec rows with `$ <command>` as the message + an `isExec` flag; `idleReaper.ts` exempts exec rows from heartbeat-based timeout (they have no classifier heartbeat but tmux confirms liveness — reaping on the heartbeat clock would falsely time-out a long-running command). +1 idleReaper regression test (11/11). Verified live in `openclaude-interactive`: spawn → `claude ps` shows `kind:exec` → `claude logs` shows output → `claude kill` terminates → next `ps` sweeps it; merged-row shape + idle-reaper exemption confirmed; build clean.

- [-] `Fixed background-agent completion notifications triggering premature "out of context" behavior on some 1M-context models` — SKIP (re-eval 2026-05-29: N/A — upstream's beta-threading root cause is absent. For every 1M model the fork actually runs (Opus-4.8-1m via `[1m]` suffix, Opus-4-7 via `modelHasNativeGA1M`, DeepSeek-V4 via the `deepseek-` heuristic), `getContextWindowForModel` (`utils/context.ts:90-169`) resolves 1M INDEPENDENTLY of the `betas` argument — the fragile `betas?.includes(CONTEXT_1M_BETA_HEADER)` branch is a Sonnet-only fallback never on these models' path. So a completion-notification turn resolves the same 1M window as any other turn; a notification can't make autocompact/blocking thresholds compute against a smaller window. Separately, the fork's "out of context"/context-blocked behavior is gated on REAL API errors (`isApiErrorMessage` → `setContextBlocked` in `print.ts`/`REPL.tsx`; `isPromptTooLongMessage` → reactiveCompact in `query.ts:954`), never the client-side estimate a notification inflates. ADJACENT FINDING (not this bug): z.ai GLM isn't matched by any 1M branch and falls to the 200K default — approximately correct for GLM-4.6 (~200K), worth a 1-line confirm against z.ai's published window if GLM's real window ever exceeds 200K.)
- [-] `Fixed background-session classifier losing the user's goal when a scheduled /command fires` — SKIP/does-not-apply (assessed 2026-06-03 via Explore agent). Our background-session classifier (`src/jobs/classifier.ts` `classifyAndWriteState()`) never derives the "goal" from conversation messages — the goal is the immutable `template`+`args` fields written once at job creation (`cli/handlers/templateJobs.ts`) and preserved verbatim on every classification pass (the classifier only updates `state`/`lastUpdate`/`turnCount`/`lastMessage`/`progress`/`prNumbers`). It also only ever sees `turnAssistantMessages` (filtered to assistant-role in `query/stopHooks.ts`), so a scheduled `/command` — which injects a system/user-role message (`useScheduledTasks.ts` → `createScheduledTaskFireMessage`) — is invisible to it and cannot overwrite the goal. Upstream's bug presumes a classifier that re-reads the goal from the latest user turn; ours is architecturally immune. Adding a `goal` field nothing reads would be dead code. Revisit only if we change the classifier to derive intent from messages.
- [-] `Fixed pinned background sessions respawning every minute after a Claude Code update, causing repeated agent-start notifications and process churn at idle` — TODO (daemon/background-session) — SKIP (re-eval 2026-05-29: this is a bug FIX for upstream's in-place-restart supervisor respawning pinned sessions too aggressively. Our fork does NOT ship that supervisor — pinnedSessions.ts header confirms pinned sessions never auto-respawn on a Claude Code update; they run the old binary until they exit. So the respawn-every-minute churn cannot occur here. Building the supervisor just to then apply the churn fix would be unrequested scope creep AND risky — auto-respawning bg processes + agent-start notifications is exactly the churn the upstream bug WAS. Not-respawning is acceptable behavior. The related idle-reaper half (behavior 1) shipped as L37 c09a4c2)
- [x] `Fixed background sessions stuck at "blocked", "running", or "working" not retiring after the idle grace period` — TODO (background-session lifecycle) — DONE c09a4c2 (built the idle-reaper the fork never shipped: src/agents/idleReaper.ts applyIdleRetirement() retires non-pinned working-status sessions (running/starting) whose lastUpdated is older than the grace period — default 30m, env CLAUDE_CODE_BG_IDLE_GRACE_MS — by deriving status=timeout in getMergedSessions, so they leave the active group + counts. Display-level only (no process kill); awaiting_input + pinned exempt by design. +10 tests. NOTE: idle-reaper retirement is display-level; the binary-update respawn supervisor (L36) + memory-pressure shedder remain unshipped)
- [ ] `Fixed subagents in background sessions bypassing the worktree-isolation guard and writing to the shared checkout` — DEFERRED (assessed 2026-06-03). Investigated the worktree machinery: session-level worktree (EnterWorktreeTool) chdir's the process (`process.chdir`+`setCwd`+`setOriginalCwd`) so subagents inherit the worktree via `getCwd()`; bg/daemon resume restores the worktree cwd on BOTH resume paths (`utils/sessionRestore.ts#restoreWorktreeForResume` called from `screens/ResumeConversation.tsx:271` and `screens/REPL.tsx:2149`); per-agent `isolation:'worktree'` scopes via `runWithCwdOverride` AsyncLocalStorage (`tools/AgentTool/AgentTool.tsx:734`). The recent commit `3b706f2` made `EnterWorktree` available turn-1 in bg sessions but did NOT touch any subagent write-guard. The specific "guard being bypassed" can't be pinned without the upstream source diff — the cwd-restore is already present on the paths we can see, so the residual bypass (if any) is either an AsyncLocalStorage propagation gap across the bg query loop's async boundaries or a permission `allWorkingDirectories()` mismatch, both permission/isolation-adjacent ("Don't Touch") with high regression risk for a blind fix. Revisit with the upstream diff or a concrete repro of a bg-session subagent writing to the shared checkout.
- [-] `Fixed orphaned claude --bg-pty-host processes spinning at 100% CPU after the daemon exits on macOS` — SKIP (macOS-specific)
- [-] `Fixed number key shortcuts not working for options shown below the divider in option dialogs` — SKIP (re-eval 2026-05-29: N/A — all our option dialogs feed a single options array to the shared `CustomSelect`. Number-key handling (`use-select-input.ts:255-282`) does `parseInt(input)-1` into the FULL `state.options[]`; the visible number labels (`select.tsx:398`) index the SAME array, so they stay aligned regardless of `<Divider>` siblings. CustomSelect has no divider/heading option-type — the `<Divider>` in QuestionView etc. is a visual sibling, never an entry in `state.options`, so there's no below-divider option group whose numbering could reset. Hand-rolled list dialogs that DO put a divider mid-list (ToolSelector) use arrow keys only, no number shortcuts. Structurally impossible here.)
- [x] `Fixed worktree.baseRef: "head" resolving to the main checkout's HEAD instead of the current worktree's HEAD when spawning subagents or calling EnterWorktree from inside a linked worktree` — TODO (worktree baseRef) — DONE 1a619ce (doCreateWorktreeLocked resolved HEAD against repoRoot=findCanonicalGitRoot=main root; now resolves the gitdir from getCwd() so baseRef:"head" picks the current worktree HEAD, with shared refs falling back via resolveRef commondir. Observed continuation lines starting with the normal 2-space indent — NO stray leading space. Lean DOES-NOT-REPRO, but the exact upstream trigger ("previous line ended EXACTLY at terminal width") wasn't 100% pinned. Candidate source if it does repro: measureWrappedText uses wrapAnsi(trim:false) at Cursor.ts:1306 which preserves boundary whitespace; a fix there is risky (shared input-wrap + cursor-positioning path). Prior related fix: b6143fe. Revisit with the upstream diff)
- [-] `Fixed intermittent terminal rendering corruption in VS Code by capping the number of distinct colors the thinking spinner produces` — SKIP (re-eval 2026-05-29: N/A — already implemented (backport L115, `c955213`). The distinct-color cap lives in `interpolateColor` (`components/Spinner/utils.ts:29/36`): `QUANT_STEPS=8` snaps interpolation to 9 discrete RGB values. Every thinking-spinner color path routes through it (SpinnerAnimationRow:240, GlimmerMessage:91/140, SpinnerGlyph:54, FlashingChar:31). The amber-warming at 10s is a discrete theme-key switch, not a gradient. The only UNquantized generator `hueToRgb` is NOT in the thinking-spinner path (only voice-waveform + logo asterisk), which upstream's fix doesn't target.)
- [x] `Fixed plan file names including [Image #N] / [Pasted text #N] placeholders when a plan-mode prompt starts with pasted images or text` — TODO (plan-mode filename) — DONE 6e9968e (generatePromptSlug now strips [Image #N] / [Pasted text #N +M lines] / [...Truncated text #N...] placeholders before deriving the slug; placeholder-only prompts fall back to a random word slug. +5 tests)
- [-] `Fixed a phantom expand/click affordance on colored tool output: short ANSI-colored lines that fit on screen no longer show a "ctrl+o to expand" hint` — SKIP (re-eval 2026-05-29 via the new synthetic-transcript ui-driver harness, RESOLVED to N/A — the elusive measurement is now pinned). Built `tools/ui-driver/tools/synth-transcript.ts` (deterministic session-JSONL generator) + `scenarios/l45-colored-expand-synth.json`: resuming a synthetic transcript with 5 short ANSI-colored Bash output lines renders them inline with NO expand hint, and the per-cell grid confirms the colors applied (blue/green/red/cyan). Code path: the expand GATE is `isOutputLineTruncated` (`utils/terminal.ts:143`) which counts ONLY raw `\n` newlines — ANSI-immune. The wrap-width measurement is `wrapText` (`terminal.ts:19`) which uses `stringWidth()` + `sliceAnsi()` — also ANSI-aware. The only raw-`.length` use (`terminal.ts:122`) is a `Math.max` upper-bound for HUGE (>3×wrapWidth×4 byte) preTruncated outputs and can only INCREASE an already-large count, never create a phantom expand on short colored lines. Upstream's width/byte-miscount surface does not exist in our renderer.

- [x] `Fixed stateful MCP servers without the optional GET SSE stream reconnect-looping on tools/list (regression in v2.1.147)` — DONE (`4fb0d0f`) (re-eval 2026-05-29: our fork WAS vulnerable. SDK `@modelcontextprotocol/sdk@1.29.0` retries the optional GET events stream `maxRetries:2` then gives up, emitting `SSE stream disconnected` / `Failed to reconnect SSE stream` per flap. Both substrings are classed terminal by `isTerminalConnectionError` (`services/mcp/client.ts:1338`), so for `http`/`claudeai-proxy` they hit the 3-strike consecutive-terminal-error teardown (`client.ts:1461`) BEFORE the SDK ever emits `Maximum reconnection attempts` — bypassing the 2.1.141 guard (`6f2eff0`). Teardown → `useManageMCPConnections.reconnectWithBackoff` → re-runs tools/list → re-opens stream → flaps again = the reconnect loop. Fix: added a guard before the terminal-error block — for `http`/`claudeai-proxy`, the optional-stream flap errors log + propagate via originalOnerror + return WITHOUT counting toward teardown (tool calls ride POST and keep working); legacy single-channel `sse` still treats them as fatal via the block below. Same reasoning the fork already shipped for `Maximum reconnection attempts`, extended to the per-attempt intermediate errors. Build clean; 133 MCP unit tests pass; container boots + responds.)
- [x] `Fixed a regression where a custom API gateway could receive the user's Anthropic OAuth credential instead of the gateway's own token` — TODO (RELEVANT — we route through custom gateways z.ai/DeepSeek; auth-credential correctness) — DONE 149d704 (getAnthropicClient gated OAuth purely on isClaudeAISubscriber(); a subscriber pointing ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL at a custom gateway leaked their OAuth accessToken there. Scoped OAuth use to isClaudeAISubscriber() && isFirstPartyAnthropicBaseUrl() — custom-gateway subscribers now use ANTHROPIC_AUTH_TOKEN via configureApiKeyHeaders, the same path non-subscribers use. First-party + ant-staging unchanged; gateway auth verified on deepseek-v4-pro)

The daemon-state mechanism (daemon/state.ts, name=remote-control) is dormant: writeDaemonState() has ZERO callers, so no daemon writes state to go stale, and queryDaemonStatus is only consulted by /autonomy status. - [ ] `Fixed a hang where the CLI could fail to exit when stdin was closed without EOF in stream-json mode, leaving a stale session marker behind` — TODO (RELEVANT — stream-json/ACP/print mode) — DEFERRED (re-eval 2026-05-29: investigated. The stream-json input loop is `StructuredIO.read()` at cli/structuredIO.ts:226-270 — `for await (const block of this.input)` terminates on EOF, then sets inputClosed + rejects pending requests. The 'closed without EOF' hang lives in `this.input`'s close/error handling across transports (print/SDK/ACP/bridge/SSH all share this loop) and/or the session-marker cleanup, not in read() itself. Surface is delicate and shared by paths verified working end-to-end (print/ACP/SSH); the exact hang is transport-specific and hard to repro reliably (abnormal stdin close). A blind fix risks regressing the working paths. Revisit with an upstream source diff or a concrete repro of stdin closing without EOF.)
- [ ] `Fixed malformed file:// links in Claude's responses not being clickable in the terminal` — TODO (minor) — DEFERRED (re-eval 2026-05-29: our rendering layer is verified-correct — createHyperlink (utils/hyperlink.ts) wraps any URL in OSC-8 and markdown.ts formatToken renders link tokens via it, so [text](file://...) IS clickable. The gap is either (a) bare file:// URLs not autolinked — marked GFM autolinks http(s)/www/email but not file://, fixable only by overriding marked's tokenizer (surgery in the heavily-used markdown pipeline), or (b) a specific "malformed" file:// shape (spaces/unencoded path) the upstream diff would pin down. Without the diff/repro the exact shape + fix location is ambiguous; our OSC-8 path is correct. Revisit with the upstream diff.) — DEEPENED (re-eval 2026-05-29: empirically tested marked@17.0.5 tokenization. `[text](file:///path.txt)` and `<file:///path.txt>` → clickable today. `[x](file:///a b.txt)` (unencoded space — the likely "malformed" shape) → marked DROPS the token (renders literal text). Bare `file:///path.txt` → not autolinked. SEPARATELY, the fullscreen click fallback `findPlainTextUrlAt` (`ink/selection.ts:322`) ALREADY matches `file://` (no spaces) so bare file:// is click-openable in fullscreen even unstyled. The two real gaps (bare-file:// autolink-as-OSC-8, and unencoded-space destinations) both require a scoped `marked` tokenizer extension in the HOTTEST render path (`markdown.ts:27`) with high false-positive risk (file:// in prose/code). Multiple plausible impls, no upstream diff → stays DEFERRED.)
- [x] `Fixed claude --help rendering unwrapped output on terminals narrower than 92 columns` — DONE (`4fb0d0f`) (re-eval 2026-05-29: Commander 13.1.0's default `minWidthToWrap=40` (`node_modules/commander/lib/help.js:15`) emits option descriptions UNWRAPPED whenever `remainingWidth = helpWidth - termWidth - 4` drops below 40 (`help.js:625-631`). The fork's wide flags (`--dangerously-skip-permissions-with-classifiers`) push the term column to ~48, so on terminals <~92 the remaining width is <40 and every long description overflows. Fix: added `minWidthToWrap: 8` to `createSortedHelpConfig()` in `main.tsx:973` — Commander copies it onto the Help instance via `Object.assign(new Help(), configureHelp())` (`command.js:198`); descriptions now wrap into the remaining column across the whole <92 range, with Commander's own `boxWrap` `width<minWidthToWrap` floor (`help.js:658`) preventing wraps into a genuinely-unusable column. Verified at 70 cols in-container: descriptions now wrap with proper continuation indent instead of one overflowing line; residual >70-col lines are unbreakable JSON-example tokens (whitespace-only wrapping, inherent to stock Commander).)
- [x] `Fixed MCP tool progress notifications not rendering in the collapsed tool view` — DONE (befb14e): the collapsed-group renderer (`components/messages/CollapsedReadSearchContent.tsx`) surfaced `bash_progress`/`powershell_progress` on the collapsed line but dropped `mcp_progress`, so long-running MCP server calls (downloads/scans) looked frozen even though the data was already plumbed via `lookups.progressMessagesByToolUseID` (expanded/grouped views showed it). Extended the same suffix loop to pick up the latest `mcp_progress` notification in the group and render ` (message · progress/total)`. Shell progress (time-based, shown after 2s) still takes precedence when both are present; renamed `shellProgressSuffix` → `progressSuffix`. Verified by inspection (mirrors the working bash-progress path) + build clean + hot-patched.

- [ ] `Fixed /btw keyboard shortcuts becoming unresponsive in background sessions while a task is running` — DEFERRED (assessed 2026-06-03). We DO ship `/btw` (`src/commands/btw/btw.tsx`) — a side-question overlay (`BtwSideQuestion`) rendered as a local-jsx command that captures keys via Ink's focus system (`<Box tabIndex={0} autoFocus onKeyDown={…}>`; Esc/Enter/Space/Ctrl+C/D dismiss, Up/Ctrl+P + Down/Ctrl+N scroll). "Unresponsive in background sessions while a task is running" implies focus contention between the /btw overlay and the active turn's input routing — but the exact mechanism (does the running-turn handler steal focus? does the bg-session input loop not forward keys to the focused overlay?) can't be pinned without the upstream source diff, and focus-routing is shared input-handling code with broad blast radius. The driver can't easily repro a bg-session + mid-turn + overlay-focus state deterministically. Revisit with the upstream diff or a concrete repro.

Our clipboard write (`src/ink/termio/osc.ts#setClipboard`) is already sophisticated for tmux: fires `copyNative()` first (pbcopy/wl-copy/xclip/xsel, gated on `!SSH_CONNECTION`), then `await tmuxLoadBuffer()` with `load-buffer -w` (tmux's own OSC 52 propagation to the outer terminal), then returns a DCS-passthrough-wrapped OSC 52 (`tmuxPassthrough`) for stdout. The reported bg-session-inside-tmux failure can't be reliably reproduced (needs a real outer terminal + tmux `set-clipboard`/`allow-passthrough` state + a detached bg session + system clipboard) and the obvious-looking "also emit raw OSC 52" fix is actually WRONG inside tmux (tmux intercepts unwrapped OSC 52, never reaching the outer terminal). ## 2.1.152

- [x] `/code-review --fix now applies review findings to your working tree after the review, surfacing reuse, simplification, and efficiency suggestions; /simplify now invokes /code-review --fix` — DONE (already satisfied, no commit needed — re-eval 2026-05-29: the registered `code-review` skill already has `--fix` (applies reuse/simplification/efficiency findings to the working tree after review) and `simplify` is already quality/cleanup-only ("does not hunt for bugs; use /code-review for that") — the later 2.1.154 behavior. src/skills/bundled/simplify.ts is consistent)
- [x] `Skills and slash commands can now set disallowed-tools in frontmatter to remove tools from the model while the skill is active` — DONE (`f818323`) Deny-side mirror of the existing `allowed-tools`. `parseSkillFrontmatterFields` parses `disallowed-tools`; `createSkillCommand`/`PromptCommand` carry `disallowedTools`; both the skills/ and legacy commands/ loaders pick it up via the `...parsed` spread. Threaded through `ProcessUserInputBaseResult` + the `command_permissions` attachment, then applied as session deny rules (`alwaysDenyRules.command`, which `filterToolsByDenyRules` honors since `command` is a PERMISSION_RULE_SOURCE) so the tools are absent from the model's set for the turn — QueryEngine.submitMessage from the destructured value, REPL onQueryImpl from the per-turn attachment (avoids threading onQuery's 4 call sites). Verified: skill with `disallowed-tools: WebSearch, WebFetch` parses + filterToolsByDenyRules removes exactly those.
- [x] `Added /reload-skills command to re-scan skill directories without restarting the session` — DONE (`9a83fd3`) New `commands/reload-skills/` local command + shared `skillChangeDetector.triggerReload()` primitive (clear skill/command caches + resetSentSkillNames + emit skillsChanged, bypassing the watcher debounce + ConfigChange blocking-hook gate). `useSkillsChange` subscribers re-run getCommands() and swap the live command list. Registers in slash_commands (verified). Reports skill count from getSkillDirCommands re-scan.
- [x] `SessionStart hooks can now return reloadSkills: true to re-scan skill directories, making skills installed by the hook available in the same session` — DONE (`9a83fd3`) Added `reloadSkills` to the SessionStart hookSpecificOutput zod schema (types/hooks.ts), the local HookResult + AggregatedHookResult types + processHookJSONOutput SessionStart case + aggregation-generator yield (hooks.ts), and consumed in processSessionStartHooks → skillChangeDetector.triggerReload() (sessionStart.ts). Verified end-to-end: a SessionStart hook emitting reloadSkills:true fired the full chain (debug "requested skill reload" → "explicit reload requested"), clearing caches so the hook-installed skill is picked up.
- [x] `SessionStart hooks can now set the session title via hookSpecificOutput.sessionTitle on startup and resume` — DONE (`0775477`) Added `sessionTitle` to the SessionStart `hookSpecificOutput` schema (`types/hooks.ts`; it previously existed only on UserPromptSubmit), parsed it in the SessionStart case (`hooks.ts`), and applied it in `processSessionStartHooks` (`utils/sessionStart.ts`) via `saveCustomTitle(getSessionId(), title, undefined, 'user')` — which persists a `custom-title` transcript entry (survives `/resume`) and caches `currentSessionTitle` for the banner. Also fixed a latent bug in the existing UserPromptSubmit path: `saveCustomTitle(hookResult.sessionTitle)` passed the title as the sessionId (real title left undefined) — now passes `getSessionId()`. End-to-end verified in container: a SessionStart hook emitting `{"hookSpecificOutput":{"hookEventName":"SessionStart","sessionTitle":"HOOKTITLE456"}}` produced 3× `{"type":"custom-title","customTitle":"HOOKTITLE456",...}` transcript entries. Build clean.
- [ ] `Added a MessageDisplay hook event that lets hooks transform or hide assistant message text as it is displayed` — TODO (new hook event) — DEFERRED (re-eval 2026-05-29: mapped the surface. This is a new KIND of hook — it transforms/hides assistant message TEXT as it renders, unlike all existing lifecycle hooks (PreToolUse/Stop/etc.) which run shell commands at non-render points. Correct impl must buffer each assistant message, run an async shell hook, and gate the Ink interactive render AND the -p/print path on its result, plus decide transcript-persistence semantics for hidden text. Multiple plausible implementations with different blast radii in core-UX message rendering, no upstream source diff. Revisit with the diff or a concrete spec of the dispatch point + streaming/persistence behavior.)
- [-] `Added pluginSuggestionMarketplaces managed setting: admins can allowlist org marketplaces whose plugins may be suggested via context-aware tips` — SKIP (enterprise managed setting)
- [ ] `claude plugin marketplace remove now accepts --scope user|project|local for symmetry with marketplace add, install, and uninstall` — DEFERRED (more than a flag; high blast radius, low value). Our `marketplace add` is already scope-aware (`marketplaceAddHandler` validates scope → `scopeToSettingSource` → `saveMarketplaceToSettings(name, …, settingSource)`), but `removeMarketplaceSource(name)` (`utils/plugins/marketplaceManager.ts:2012`) unconditionally strips the marketplace from ALL three editable settings sources AND purges the global `known_marketplaces.json`, the on-disk cache, `installed_plugins.json` entries, plugin data dirs, and stored secrets. True `--scope` symmetry means: clean only the named scope's `extraKnownMarketplaces`/`enabledPlugins`, and purge the global config/cache/plugins/secrets ONLY when no OTHER scope still declares it — a restructure of a sensitive deletion path (a bug would orphan plugins or wipe secrets/data for a still-declared marketplace). Verifying partial-removal-doesn't-purge needs multi-scope settings + installed-plugin fixtures. Low-value niche symmetry against real regression risk; revisit with an upstream source diff for the exact partial-purge semantics.
- [x] `Claude Code now switches to your configured --fallback-model for the rest of the session when the primary model is not found, instead of failing every request` — DONE (`5403653`) Added `isModelNotFoundError()` to `services/api/withRetry.ts` (conservative: 400/404 + body references "model" + a not-found phrase or `not_found_error` type; covers Anthropic + OpenAI-compat z.ai/DeepSeek/NIM phrasings). In the retry catch block, before the 529 tracking, a model-not-found error now throws `FallbackTriggeredError(model, fallbackModel, 'model_not_found')` immediately (no pointless retries) when `--fallback-model` is set — query.ts's existing handler swaps the model + retries for the rest of the session. Added `FallbackReason` to the error so query.ts renders "… was not found" instead of "… high demand". 6-case detector test PASS (Anthropic 404 not_found, OpenAI 404 does-not-exist, compat 400 model-not-found, + generic-404/401/529 no-trip); build clean; container `-p` smoke OK.
- [-] `Auto mode no longer requires opt-in consent` — SKIP (N/A, re-eval 2026-05-29: no auto-mode consent gate exists in our fork — the only opt-in/consent in prompts.ts is the /identity persona system; our auto-mode-equivalent activation is a slash toggle, and we wouldn't strip safety gates anyway)
- [x] `Vim mode: / in NORMAL mode now opens reverse history search (like Ctrl+R), matching bash/zsh vi-mode` — DONE (`def3deb`) `useVimInput` intercepts `/` in NORMAL idle (before the command-transition dispatch) and calls a new `onReverseSearch` callback threaded PromptInput → VimTextInput → useVimInput. The callback routes to whichever reverse-search mechanism is active per the HISTORY_PICKER flag — modal picker (`setShowHistoryPicker`) when on (compiled binary), inline search (`useHistorySearch.handleStartSearch`, newly exposed) when off (DCE'd source-mode path) — mirroring the existing history:search keybinding split. Verified via ui-driver (editorMode=vim seeded): Esc→NORMAL→`/` left the input unchanged and brought up the inline reverse-search UI. Regression scenario at tools/ui-driver/scenarios/vim-slash-history-search.json.
- [x] `The /usage breakdown now includes large session files; files are scanned with a streaming read so memory usage stays flat` — DONE (`6d4164f`) `usageInsights.processFile` used `readJSONLFile` (buffers whole file up to a 100 MB tail → per-file memory spike + head of huge files excluded). New `streamJSONLFile<T>(path, onEntry)` in json.ts reads 256 KB chunks via a FileHandle, decodes UTF-8 with a streaming TextDecoder (multibyte-safe across boundaries), parses each line and discards it — flat memory regardless of size, whole file scanned. processFile consumes the stream (verbatim 1:1 port of the per-entry logic, lazy aggregate creation preserving the old empty-file early-return). Verified byte-identical to readJSONLFile on a 2-chunk file with multibyte/blank/malformed/no-trailing-newline lines (5002 entries).
- [ ] `Thinking summaries in the collapsed group now stay readable for at least 3 seconds, render as markdown, and cap at 10 lines (Ctrl+O shows the full thinking)` — DEFERRED (assessed 2026-06-03): our collapsed thinking (AssistantThinkingMessage.tsx) shows only "∴ Thinking <Ctrl+O to expand>" — there is NO inline thinking-SUMMARY preview in the collapsed group to apply the 3s/markdown/10-line rules to; the full (expanded) path already renders via `<Markdown>` (just no 10-line cap). So this is a feature-add (generate + render a condensed thinking summary inline), not a small bug backport — needs a fullscreen-streaming ui-driver pass.
- [ ] `In fullscreen mode, the "Thinking for Ns" indicator now counts up live while the model is thinking, and keeps its value if you interrupt mid-thought` — DEFERRED (assessed 2026-06-03): our fork shows rotating THINKING_HINTS while thinking (SpinnerAnimationRow.tsx:212) and "thought for Ns" only AFTER it stops — a deliberate design divergence, not a bug (the whole-turn timer already counts live, and thinkingStartTimeRef is already plumbed into SpinnerAnimationRow). Implementing upstream's exact live count-up is a stylistic change to always-visible spinner rendering with risk to the shrink-to-fit width logic; needs a fullscreen-streaming ui-driver pass + a design decision (replace hints vs add timer; fullscreen-only vs always).
- [-] `Simplified the Workflow tool's inline progress display — live agent counts now show only in the persistent workflow status row below the prompt` — SKIP (N/A, re-eval 2026-05-29: our Workflow tool runs in the background (returns a runId + task-notification; the /workflows live view is the persistent status surface). - [~] `The post-response timer now shows "Waiting for N background agents/workflows to finish" when backgrounded agents or workflows are still running, and reports the cumulative time once their results are processed` — PARTIAL (assessed 2026-06-03): our Spinner.tsx ALREADY renders `<N> in background` (lines 424/494) where the count = isBackgroundTask tasks + remoteBackgroundTaskCount (covers backgrounded local agents + workflows + remote). The substance — surfacing the count of still-running background work — is present; the remaining delta is exact wording ("Waiting for N … to finish") + cumulative-time-on-completion, minor polish on always-visible footer rendering (would want a ui-driver pass with running bg agents to verify; not worth the footer-regression risk for wording alone).
- [x] `Added the session entrypoint as an OpenTelemetry metric attribute (app.entrypoint, opt-in via OTEL_METRICS_INCLUDE_ENTRYPOINT=true)` — DONE (`2e8d2d7`) `getTelemetryAttributes()` (`utils/telemetryAttributes.ts`) now adds `app.entrypoint = process.env.CLAUDE_CODE_ENTRYPOINT` when `OTEL_METRICS_INCLUDE_ENTRYPOINT` is truthy (default false), following the existing `OTEL_METRICS_INCLUDE_VERSION` cardinality-default pattern. Added the env var to the managed-settings allowlist (`managedEnvConstants.ts`) for parity. This is user-configured OTel metrics (their own collector) — distinct from the neutralized Anthropic telemetry, same opt-in model as our observe tracing. Build clean; container boots healthy. (Logic mirrors the version attribute exactly; emits only to a user OTel collector so not `-p`-observable.)
- [-] `Fixed terminal styling degrading in very long sessions by recycling the renderer's style pool` — SKIP/N-A-by-architecture (assessed 2026-06-03). Our Ink is a custom rewrite (`src/ink/`) with NO "style pool" data structure — grep for `stylePool`/`style pool`/`recycle`/`styleCache` across `src/ink/` returns nothing. Upstream's fix recycles a growing/corrupting style-object pool; our renderer builds styles per-node without such a pool, so the specific degradation mechanism doesn't exist here. (We do have bounded caches — `line-width-cache.ts`, `node-cache.ts` — but those are unrelated to per-cell style accumulation.) No equivalent structure to recycle; revisit only if profiling shows long-session style degradation in our renderer.
- [x] `Fixed the sandbox-enabled warning not appearing in condensed startup mode — it now shows in every layout` — DONE (`2ee9776`) The "Your bash commands will be sandboxed. Disable with /sandbox." warning rendered in LogoV2's compact and full panel branches but not the condensed (no-panel) startup branch (`!wantPanel && !showOnboarding`). Added the same `showSandboxStatus && <Box>…</Box>` element to that branch's t23 fragment — inlined (not a new memo cell) since `showSandboxStatus` is sentinel-cached ($[1], constant per mount) and the React-compiled memo cache `_c(94)` is fully allocated. Build clean. Live warning render not reproduced (isSandboxingEnabled() needs bubblewrap + sandbox.enabled, absent in test env) but the element is byte-identical to the verified full-panel branch.
- [x] `Fixed the loading spinner showing "still thinking"/"almost done thinking" while a tool is running, and reset the thinking status to "thinking" after each tool` — DONE (`71d143c`) `SpinnerAnimationRow.tsx` derived the `THINKING_HINTS` index from whole-turn elapsed (`effectiveElapsedMs`; the in-code comment admitted the approximation), so a turn with long tool calls made resumed thinking jump straight to "almost done thinking". Now `thinkingElapsed` uses the current burst's elapsed via the existing per-burst `thinkingStartRef` (set/cleared by Spinner.tsx on each `mode==='thinking'` transition), threaded in as the new `thinkingStartTimeRef` prop; falls back to 0 (→ "thinking") when no burst is active. The escalating hint only renders when `thinkingStatus==='thinking'` (not during tool execution). Mirrors the `dbb393e` per-burst amber-spinner fix. Build clean; container boots healthy (`-p --output-format=json` → `is_error:false`). Visual timing not ui-driver-verified (requires live multi-burst thinking + tool calls, hard to script deterministically); logic verified by reasoning against the per-burst ref lifecycle.
- [-] `Fixed focus mode showing a spurious "N messages hidden" count on turns with no hidden activity` — SKIP (re-eval 2026-05-29: N/A — our focus view is a pure message filter (`filterForFocusView`, `Messages.tsx:167-216`) that drops intermediate messages SILENTLY; it never renders a "N messages hidden" count. The only `hiddenMessageCount` dividers (`Messages.tsx:800/807`) belong to the Ctrl+O TRANSCRIPT screen (gated on `isTranscriptMode`/`hasTruncatedMessages`), a different feature. The fork never ported a focus-mode count, so there's no spurious count to fix.)
- [x] `Fixed clicking a link inside an expanded tool result collapsing the section instead of opening the link` — DONE (`4fb0d0f`) (re-eval 2026-05-29: code-verified. The fullscreen single-click release handler (`ink/components/App.tsx:673`) dispatched `onClickAt` FIRST (which bubbles to the per-message expand/collapse `onClick` in `VirtualMessageList.tsx:235/915`), and only resolved `getHyperlinkAt` if `onClickAt` returned false — so a click on a link inside an expanded (clickable) tool result collapsed the section and never opened the link. Fix: resolve `getHyperlinkAt(col,row)` BEFORE the DOM click; a cell carrying a link takes the link path (open on real terminals, or let xterm.js own it in vscode/Cursor) and does NOT dispatch onClickAt; only non-link cells dispatch the DOM click. `getHyperlinkAt` (`ink.tsx:1369`) returns `url ?? findPlainTextUrlAt(...)` so the fix covers BOTH OSC-8 hyperlink cells AND plain-text URLs, and returns null outside fullscreen so inline-mode clicks are unchanged. Blast radius: fullscreen-only; only link cells change behavior. EMPIRICAL NOTE: the ui-driver synthetic-transcript harness renders resumed tool results STATICALLY (the live click-to-collapse toggle isn't wired in replayed history — confirmed: both a URL-cell click and a control non-URL-cell click left the 7-line result intact), so a deterministic before/after click repro needs a LIVE streaming turn, which the resume harness can't produce. Fix rests on the unambiguous ordering analysis.)
- [-] `Fixed markdown table cell borders inheriting the color of inline code, wrapped continuation lines losing their style, and empty header cells showing a label in the narrow-terminal stacked layout` — TODO (markdown table rendering) — SKIP (re-eval 2026-05-29, UI-driver pass: verified N/A empirically. Rendered a markdown table with an inline-code cell via applyMarkdown(FORCE_COLOR=3): the cell emits `\e[97mbar()\e[39m` — the codespan color RESETS (\e[39m) before the ` |` border, so borders never inherit the inline-code color (sub-bug 1 does not repro). Our markdown.ts table renderer (case table, padAligned) is fixed-width pipe-tables only — it has NO narrow-terminal stacked layout (sub-bug 3 N/A) and per-cell wrapping is left to the terminal (sub-bug 2 not our code). All three sub-bugs are N/A for our renderer)
