- [x] `Fixed plugin MCP servers with the same command but different environment variables being incorrectly deduplicated` — DONE (`7eacd35`) `getMcpServerSignature()` (`services/mcp/config.ts`) ignored env for stdio servers (`stdio:${cmd}`), so same-command/different-env plugin servers collided and `dedupPluginMcpServers()` wrongly suppressed one. New `getStdioEnvSignature()` appends a normalized env fingerprint (sorted entries) excluding the framework-injected `CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT` so genuinely-identical servers from different plugins still dedup. 3-case test PASS (diff env→distinct, same env + diff CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT→deduped, dedupPluginMcpServers keeps A+B / suppresses C); build clean; container boots healthy (`-p --output-format=json` → `is_error:false`).
- [x] `Fixed /doctor reporting "marketplace not found" or "plugin not found" for stale enabledPlugins entries referencing removed marketplaces or dropped plugins` — DONE (`7a6b051`) In the startup enabled-plugin loader (`pluginLoader.ts`), before pushing `plugin-not-found`, the entry is treated as stale and skipped (debug-log only) when the referenced marketplace is no longer registered (`!marketplaceConfig`) or its catalog loaded fine but the plugin isn't in it (`marketplaceCatalogs.has` → dropped plugin). The fallback-null case (marketplace registered, catalog not pre-loaded, cache-only also missed) keeps the error. /doctor reads `plugins.errors` straight from the loader, so suppressing at source removes it from both /doctor and the /plugin Errors tab. Verified: seeding `enabledPlugins: {"ghostplugin@ghost-marketplace": true}` logs "Skipping stale enabledPlugins entry … (marketplace removed)" with no error pushed.
- [ ] `Fixed plugins that track a git branch silently no longer receiving updates after the plugin registry was rebuilt` — DEFERRED (re-eval 2026-05-29: our fork has no distinct "plugin registry" data structure whose REBUILD drops a per-plugin branch ref. `reconcileInstalledPluginsWithSettings` (`installedPluginsManager.ts:1140`) preserves existing entries and re-reads source/`ref` live from the marketplace cache each time, and `performPluginUpdate` (`pluginOperations.ts:1144`) re-clones the branch with `--branch <ref>` + recomputes version from the fresh HEAD. The upstream "after the registry was rebuilt" names a structure/transition we can't map unambiguously → multiple plausible fix sites. ADJACENT (not this bug): `calculatePluginVersion` (`pluginVersioning.ts:45/94`) prioritizes a static `manifest.version` ABOVE the git SHA — a branch-tracking plugin that declares a fixed `version` always computes the same version across branch commits → silently up-to-date. That's an always-on gap, not "after rebuild". Revisit with the upstream source diff.)
- [-] `Fixed remote MCP servers failing to connect in Claude Code Remote sessions when the egress proxy is enabled` — SKIP (Claude Code Remote sessions + egress proxy)
- [-] `Fixed the effort-change confirmation dialog appearing when the conversation has no messages or when switching between effort levels that resolve to the same underlying value` — SKIP (dialog not shipped in our fork). The upstream fix targets a mid-conversation effort-change confirmation dialog. Our `/effort` command (`commands/effort/effort.tsx`) applies the change directly via `ApplyEffortAndClose` + a toast (no "this will restart/invalidate the conversation" confirmation gate), and the `/model` ModelPicker applies effort on selection without a confirmation. Grep for `effortChange`/`pendingEffort`/`wouldBreakCache`/effort-near-confirm dialog returns nothing — the confirmation dialog the upstream bug is about doesn't exist here, so there's no spurious-appearance surface to fix.
- [x] `Fixed the Agent tool description referencing an agent list that is never delivered when running with --bare or with attachments disabled` — DONE (`ab9c972`) `shouldInjectAgentListInMessages()` (`tools/AgentTool/prompt.ts`) now returns false (forces inline listing) when `isBareMode() || CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_ATTACHMENTS` — mirroring `getAttachments()`'s own early-return gate, so the description never points the model at an `agent_listing_delta` <system-reminder> list that `getAttachments()` won't emit. The guard wins even over the explicit `CLAUDE_CODE_AGENT_LIST_IN_MESSAGES=true` override (attachment path is physically dead when attachments are off). Note: our fork's GrowthBook gate `tengu_agent_list_attach` defaults false, so the inline path was already the default — this fixes the explicit-override + bare combination. 3-case predicate test PASS; build clean; container `--bare` (`CLAUDE_CODE_SIMPLE=1`) `-p` smoke OK.
- [ ] `Fixed a background worker crash in claude agents when accepting a stale permission prompt after a subagent was cancelled` — DEFERRED (bug surface mostly absent + race-condition fix in a "Don't Touch"-adjacent area without a repro). Our `claude --bg` / `claude agents` background sessions spawn with `--dangerously-skip-permissions` forced (`cli/bg.ts:205-206`) plus the fork's default `bypassPermissions` mode, so subagents inside bg sessions never raise permission prompts — the "accept a stale prompt after the subagent was cancelled" sequence can't fire on the default path. The only remaining surface is the remote-control permission bridge (`remote/remotePermissionBridge.ts` just builds synthetic messages/tool stubs; the accept/cancel resolution race lives in the permission-response routing, which is permission-system code we treat as Don't-Touch). A blind fix without the crash stack risks regressing the permission system. Revisit with an upstream source diff or a concrete repro against a non-bypass bg/remote session.
- [x] `Fixed cache_creation_input_tokens reporting as 0 in transcript and result usage when the API reports cache writes only via the nested cache_creation breakdown` — DONE (`6b91a10`) `updateUsage()` in `services/api/claude.ts` now computes `mergedEphemeral1h`/`mergedEphemeral5m` first, then derives `cache_creation_input_tokens`: top-level value wins when present and > 0, else falls back to `ephemeral_1h + ephemeral_5m`. Fixes the single accumulator that all downstream reads derive from (cost-tracker lines 213/360/407/429, observe trace at 3217, cache-warning tracker at 3289, result/transcript usage). Build clean; container hot-patch + `-p` smoke OK (CLI boots and responds; cache fix is in the usage-merge path).
- [-] `Fixed the PushNotification tool incorrectly reporting "Mobile push not sent (Remote Control inactive)" in SDK-hosted sessions when Remote Control is enabled` — SKIP (bug surface absent in our fork). Our `tools/PushNotificationTool/PushNotificationTool.ts` (115 LOC) is purely terminal-escape-based (iTerm2/Kitty/Ghostty/bell + `executeNotificationHooks()`) — it has no mobile-push-via-claude.ai-Remote-Control path and never emits the "Mobile push not sent (Remote Control inactive)" string (`grep` confirms zero matches). The bug is specific to Anthropic's hosted Remote Control + mobile-push plumbing, which we don't ship.
- [ ] `Fixed sessions getting stuck after a model or login switch left stale thinking-block signatures in history; now stripped proactively with a retry safety-net` — DEFERRED (multiple plausible implementations with different blast radii in the heavily-tuned multi-provider thinking pipeline). Our `utils/messages.ts` already runs `filterOrphanedThinkingOnlyMessages` (prevents the mismatched-signature 400) and a provider-aware thinking pruner: Anthropic manages thinking server-side (`keepTurns=-1`), but DeepSeek-V4 returns 400 if `reasoning_content` is dropped and z.ai Coding-Plan preserved-thinking cache hits require byte-for-byte `reasoning_content` match. A blind "strip all signatures on model switch" would regress the DeepSeek/z.ai paths. The retry-safety-net half would touch `claude.ts`'s delicate 400-retry path. The upstream bug is specifically Anthropic model/login switch (server-managed signatures); our primary runtime (z.ai/DeepSeek) handles signatures differently. Revisit with an upstream source diff or a concrete repro of a stuck session after `/model` on a first-party-Anthropic base URL — at which point a model-switch detector that strips signatures ONLY when `isFirstPartyAnthropicBaseUrl()` is the safe scoping.

## 2.1.150

- [-] `Internal infrastructure improvements (no user-facing changes)` — SKIP (Anthropic infra-only release)

## 2.1.149

- [x] `/usage now shows a per-category breakdown of what's driving your limits usage — skills, subagents, plugins, and per-MCP-server cost` — DONE (`5f1642a`) Added `UsageInsights.categoryBreakdown` (skillsPct + subagentsPct + pluginsPct + mcpServers[]) computed in `utils/usageInsights.ts` via tool_use name categorization (`SkillTool`/`DiscoverSkillsTool` → skills; `mcp__<server>__*` → per-server). Each message's tokens split equally across its tool_use categories; messages with no tool_use roll into the implicit "Direct model use" residual rendered at the bottom. Plugin attribution resolved at compute time: any MCP server name matching an enabled plugin's `mcpServers` record (via `getEnabledPlugins()`) contributes to `pluginsTokens`; per-MCP rows hidden when `pluginsPct > 0` to avoid double-counting. `Usage.tsx` renders a new "Last 24h/7d — usage breakdown by category" section under the existing characteristics block, sorted desc by pct, only when `totalTokens > 0` and ≥1 category > 0%. Live-verified against 109 sessions / 67M tokens / 7d in openclaude-interactive (subagentsPct=8%, matched existing subagentHeavyPct=9%; skillsPct+pluginsPct=0% in window).
- [x] `/diff detail view can now be scrolled with the keyboard (arrows, j/k, PgUp/PgDn, Space, Home/End)` — DONE (`7f050f9`) Wired ScrollBox into DiffDetailView (gated on new `scrollRef` prop) + six new diff:scroll* actions on the existing DiffDialog context: k/j (line), PgUp/PgDn/Space (page), Home/End (jump). Up/Down arrows dual-purpose — list-mode file-nav handlers route to `scrollBy(±1)` in detail mode. All scroll handlers return false in list mode so events propagate. Scroll resets to top on file or mode change. Bottom hint in detail mode shows the new keys. Both DiffDialog.tsx and DiffDetailView.tsx replaced from React-Compiler-baked output with fresh source TS so the scroll wiring is editable. Build clean; container hot-patch + `-p` smoke OK.
- [x] `Markdown output now renders GFM task list checkboxes (- [ ] todo / - [x] done) instead of plain bullets` — 0c28a97 (src/utils/markdown.ts: new `case 'checkbox'` emits ☐/☑ glyphs; `text` handler suppresses `- ` bullet when parent list_item is a task; `list_item` handler skips per-child indent for non-first children of task items so glyph + text share one line)
- [-] `Enterprise: added the allowAllClaudeAiMcps managed setting to load claude.ai cloud MCP connectors alongside managed-mcp.json` — SKIP (enterprise managed-settings + claude.ai cloud MCPs we don't load)
- [-] `Fixed a PowerShell permission bypass: built-in cd functions (cd.., cd\, cd~, X:) changed the working directory undetected, letting a later command read outside the workspace` — SKIP (PowerShell tool, Windows)
- [x] `Fixed the sandbox write allowlist in git worktrees covering the entire main repository root instead of only the shared .git directory (with hooks/ and config denied)` — DONE (`ae8d2c5`) `utils/sandbox/sandbox-adapter.ts` scopes the worktree-mode allowlist push from `worktreeMainRepoPath` (entire main repo root) to `join(worktreeMainRepoPath, '.git')`. Adds explicit deny rules for `.git/hooks/` (would let worktree session install hooks that execute on every main-repo git op) and `.git/config` (would override git config). Resolved once at init.
- [-] `Fixed PowerShell prefix/wildcard allow rules (e.g. PowerShell(dotnet.exe build *)) not pre-approving native executables and scripts` — SKIP (PowerShell tool)
- [-] `Fixed a permission-analysis gap where the parser trusted stale variable-tracking values for PWD/OLDPWD/DIRSTACK across cd/pushd/popd` — SKIP. Our bash AST parser (`utils/bash/ast.ts`) lists `PWD`/`OLDPWD` in `SAFE_ENV_VARS` (treated as opaque/unknown-value) but does NOT track them dynamically across `cd`/`pushd`/`popd` — there's no value-tracking code that updates these vars when a cd succeeds. The upstream bug is "trusted STALE tracked values", but our parser never trusts dynamic values in the first place (it just treats the vars as untracked / unknown-value). The "stale tracking" failure mode doesn't manifest because the tracking doesn't exist. Adding the full dynamic-tracking infrastructure would be a feature addition, not a bug fix, and would require a large parser refactor (the SECURITY: comment at ast.ts:1991 explicitly notes the current "opaque vars" stance is the safe one). Revisit only if a user reports a concrete `cd /etc && $PWD` permission bypass.
- [-] `Fixed find in the Bash tool exhausting the macOS system file/vnode table and crashing the host on large directory trees` — SKIP (macOS-specific kernel-table exhaustion)
- [-] `Fixed the managed-settings approval dialog leaving the terminal frozen after accepting at startup` — SKIP (managed-settings/enterprise approval flow)
- [-] `Fixed /ultraplan and remote session creation failing with "Could not capture uncommitted changes" when the working tree has no real changes` — SKIP (already correct in our fork). `/ultraplan` was refactored from CCR remote sessions to a local forked agent (`runForkedAgent` with `ExitPlanModeScanner`, see CLAUDE.md) — it does not invoke `createAndUploadGitBundle` at all, so the upstream codepath that produced the "Could not capture uncommitted changes" error does not exist on the /ultraplan side. For remote session creation, our `src/utils/teleport/gitBundle.ts:193-213` already handles the empty-WIP case correctly: `git stash create` on a clean tree returns exit 0 with empty stdout, the helper interprets that as `hasWip=false` (not an error), and `_bundleWithFallback` runs `git bundle create --all` without the `refs/seed/stash` extra ref. End-to-end smoke confirmed in openclaude-interactive: clean tree → stash exit 0, empty sha, hasWip=false, bundle create succeeds (304B). Dirty tree → stash exit 0, non-empty sha, hasWip=true (happy path unchanged). The only "stash" failure mode our helper treats as fatal is the empty-repo case (no refs at all), which is a genuine failure with its own `failReason: 'empty_repo'` and user-facing "Repository has no commits yet" message — distinct from the bug fixed upstream.
- [x] `Fixed otelHeadersHelper failing silently when the script path contains spaces; helper failures are now reported in /doctor and the debug log` — DONE (`0416c99`) `src/utils/auth.ts:getOtelHeadersFromHelper` was funnelling the configured path through `execSyncWithDefaults_DEPRECATED` which uses `shell: true` → `sh -c <path>`, so any path with a space (e.g. `/Users/Some User/.config/otel-headers.sh`) got split by the shell and ran the first fragment as a command. The shell-quoting bug masked the original error because `reject: false` + best-effort OTel-header lookup swallowed it. Two-part fix: (a) switch to `execaSync(otelHeadersHelper, [], { shell: false })` so the path is argv[0] not a shell command string — whitespace is safe by construction (verified via posix_spawn passing the path as one argument); (b) capture last execution status in a module-level ref via `recordOtelHeadersHelperSuccess`/`recordOtelHeadersHelperFailure`, expose via new `getOtelHeadersHelperStatus()`, render in /doctor via new `<OtelHeadersHelperDoctorSection />` (slotted alongside SandboxDoctorSection in Doctor.tsx's t31 memo block via a Fragment, renders null when unconfigured so the 99% of users without an OTel helper see no change). Failures funnel through `logError()` with structured context (script path + exitCode + stderr tail) so `~/.claude/debug/` and the in-memory error log both pick them up. Smoke-tested end-to-end with fixture at `/tmp/space dir/otel-headers.sh`: success path returns headers + records `state:'ok'`; ENOENT records `state:'error'` with the posix_spawn message; exit-7 + stderr records `state:'error'` with `exitCode:7` + `stderrTail:"auth service unreachable"` + message `"otelHeadersHelper exited with code 7"`. Doctor section renders null pre-call, JSX tree post-call.
- [x] `Fixed the thinking spinner staying amber across tool calls and onto fresh thinking bursts` — DONE (`dbb393e`) `Spinner.tsx:222 isLongThinking` was triggering off `elapsedSnapshot` (whole-turn timer started at user submit), so any turn that lasted ≥10s locked the spinner amber for the rest of the turn regardless of whether the model was thinking, tool-using, or streaming a response. Re-keyed on the pre-existing `thinkingStartRef.current` per-burst timer (already set/cleared by the effect at 131-142 on `mode === 'thinking'` transitions). Amber now activates only while a single thinking burst has been running ≥10s, resets on the first tool-use / responding transition, and re-warms only if the NEXT burst itself exceeds 10s. Parallel-agent dispatch.
- [x] `Fixed collapsed Bash output reporting the wrong hidden-line count for outputs with many short lines` — DONE (`668bc9e`) `terminal.ts:renderTruncatedContent` `preTruncated` path picked `Math.max(a, b)`: (a) wrapText's count from the first `maxChars` bytes, (b) `length / wrapWidth`. Both undercount short-line outputs — `'x\n' × 1000` is 2000 chars / 70-col wrap ≈ 29 "lines" vs. real source count of 1000. 600- and 1000-line outputs both reported the SAME `+418 lines` (a constant derived from `MAX_LINES_TO_SHOW * wrapWidth * 4 ≈ 840 chars`). Added a third estimate (c): raw newline count of the full content. `Math.max(a, b, c)` keeps long-line behavior unchanged. 7-case smoke: 1000 short lines → hidden=997 (was 418); 600 → 597 (was 418); 200 → 197 (unchanged); 1 long line → null (unchanged); 3-5 lines → null/2 (unchanged). Parallel-agent dispatch.
- [x] `Fixed slash-command argument-hint clipping trailing typed characters when the hint overflows the input box` — DONE (`64d8aa9`) `BaseTextInput.tsx` rendered `{renderedValue}{showArgumentHint && hint}` inside a `<Text>` with no width budget — when `value.length + ' ' + argumentHint.length` exceeded `columns`, the hint wrapped to a 2nd row inside the prompt box, visually overlapping the cell the cursor was about to land on (the "trailing typed character"). Triggers against `/buddy` (90-char hint) at any width < ~95 cols and against `/compact`, `/distill`, `/local`, `/buddy` at narrow widths. Added `fitArgumentHint(hint, renderedValue, columns, gap)` helper that computes `budget = columns - stringWidth(renderedValue) - stringWidth(gap) - 1` (the -1 reserves the cursor cell so the next typed char doesn't land on hint text), truncates the hint by grapheme to `budget - 1` cells, and appends `…`; drops the hint entirely when even `<1-char>…` doesn't fit. Threaded through both render branches (highlights path at line 113 + plain Ansi path at t6/line 119). React-Compiler memo cache (`_c(14)`) untouched — new computed values feed existing `t6` slot, which depends on `props`/`children`/`t5`/`T1`. Verified via `tools/ui-driver/scenarios/argument-hint-clipping.json` (80→60 col resize on `/buddy `): before fix, hint wrapped to `❯ /buddy [pet|stats|rename|personality|off|on|save|summon|li` + 2nd row `         st|dismiss|pick|frequency|style|rarity]`; after fix, single row `❯ /buddy  [pet|stats|rename|personality|off|on|save|summo…`. Short hints (`/branch`'s `[name]`) unaffected — regression scenario `argument-hint-short-noregress.json`. No upstream source diff available — inferred from changelog description matching exact in-tree behavior.
- [x] `Fixed argument-hint and progressive arg suggestions not appearing after Tab-completing a skill whose frontmatter name: differs from its directory basename` — DONE (`85173ac`) `hooks/useTypeahead.tsx:788` post-Tab argument-hint lookup now mirrors `findCommand` semantics: matches `cmd.name === commandName || getCommandName(cmd) === commandName || cmd.aliases?.includes(...)` instead of only `getCommandName(cmd) === commandName`. Hint surfaces regardless of whether the user typed the dir basename or the frontmatter name.
- [x] `Fixed the status bar showing the user's baseline /effort setting instead of the effort level applied by skill/agent effort: frontmatter` — DONE (`f1a99c3`) Skills with `effort:` frontmatter route through `processSlashCommand` → `result.effort` → `REPL.onQueryImpl(... effort)` which wrapped `toolUseContext.getAppState()` with the override but DELIBERATELY kept it out of the global store (existing comment: "wrapping getAppState keeps the override out of the global store so background agents and UI subscribers (Spinner, LogoV2) never see it"). API saw the override, status bar showed baseline — that's the bug. Same problem for `--agent foo` with `effort:` frontmatter on the main thread (was never applied even at the API level). Fix: added transient `appliedEffortValue?: EffortValue` to AppState (cleared by default). `REPL.onQueryImpl` publishes the skill's effort to it at turn start, clears it in the `queryGuard.end()` finally block. `Spinner.tsx` and `StatusLine.tsx` now read `appliedEffortValue ?? effortValue` so they see the per-turn override (other surfaces — ModelPicker, `/effort` command, LogoV2 startup banner — intentionally keep reading the baseline since they represent user-config). `StatusLine` also adds `appliedEffortValue` to its useEffect dep array + `previousStateRef.effortValue` so the statusline-script JSON's `effort.level` refreshes mid-turn instead of waiting for the next assistant message. `main.tsx` seeds `appliedEffortValue` from `mainThreadAgentDefinition?.effort` at both REPL and headless session-init sites so `--agent foo` (with frontmatter `effort: max`) makes the bar show `max` for the whole session. Inferred-from-existing-code path (no upstream source diff available) — the resolver helper already existed (`getDisplayedEffortLevel`), the AppState pattern existed (`effortValue`), and the per-turn override pattern existed (wrapped `getAppState`); the fix just publishes the override one layer up to a parallel state field the UI subscribes to. 28 effort tests pass (6 new cases covering applied-vs-baseline priority); end-to-end smoke confirmed in container: baseline=low + applied=max → `getDisplayedEffortLevel('claude-opus-4-6', applied ?? baseline)` returns `max`.
- [x] `Fixed Ctrl+O transcript view freezing at the moment it was opened instead of tailing new messages` — DONE (`dcdc378`) `screens/REPL.tsx` `handleEnterTranscript` sets `frozenTranscriptState` to `null` instead of capturing `messages.length` + `streamingToolUses.length`. The existing slice resolves to live arrays. VML rebuilds its searchable text index incrementally as messages grow (cost bounded by per-message growth). State variable retained for API stability so the legacy non-virtual-scroll path can opt back into freezing later without re-plumbing.
- [x] `Fixed editing a recalled prompt-history entry losing the edit when navigating further up/down with arrow keys` — DONE (`e22534d`) `useArrowKeyHistory.tsx` overwrote the input buffer on each up/down press without checking whether the user had edited the recalled entry — typing into a recalled prompt then pressing up/down silently dropped the edit. Fix (approach b — per-index transient drafts): track every rendered (display, mode) tuple via `shownAtIndexRef` + an `editedDraftsRef` Map keyed by historyIndex. On the leading edge of every up/down nav past the first press (`captureEditForCurrentIndex`), diff the live input against the last-rendered tuple; if they diverge, persist the live input as an edit for that index. When navigating back to an index that has a saved edit, restore the edit instead of the canonical entry. Drafts are additive-only (no delete branch) so round-trip preservation works: edit → away → back → edit still there. Map clears on `resetHistory()` (submit/Esc) — drafts are session-scoped. Bash-mode round-trips the leading-`!` sentinel correctly. Smoke harness `/tmp/test-arrow-history.ts` — 19 scenarios pass: single-edit preservation, multi-index edits at 3 levels, no-edit no-op, beyond-end navigation, draft-vs-recalled coexistence, multi-round-trip preservation, re-edit-wins.
- [-] `Fixed /config exit summary reporting phantom changes to auto-compact and theme when toggling unrelated settings` — SKIP (not present in our fork). Parallel-agent investigation: our `src/components/Settings/Config.tsx` exit summary uses the format `"Set theme to X"` / `"Enabled/Disabled auto-compact"` (lines 1390, 1414), not upstream's `"Changed: foo: A → B"`. `initialConfig` (useRef snapshot at mount, line 101) and `globalConfig` (useState init from same call, line 100) both read from `getGlobalConfig()`'s memoized cached object reference — both sides are identically-resolved snapshots. Toggling an unrelated setting calls `updateSettingsForSource()` which doesn't call `setGlobalConfig`, so `globalConfig.theme`/`globalConfig.autoCompactEnabled` stay identical to `initialConfig.current.<same>`. The `!==` comparisons evaluate false and the `formattedChanges.push` never fires. The two-snapshot normalization mismatch upstream patched doesn't exist in our code path.
- [-] `Fixed /insights crashing when cached session-meta files are missing optional fields` — SKIP (we don't ship /insights)
- [-] `Fixed malformed PowerShell and History tool calls with missing input being misclassified as reads in transcript collapsing` — SKIP (PowerShell + upstream History tool)
- [-] `Fixed renaming a Remote Control session from claude.ai or the Claude mobile app not updating the local session name for claude --resume` — SKIP (claude.ai Remote Control + mobile app rename plumbing)
- [x] `Fixed a race where a just-submitted prompt could appear twice in the up-arrow history` — DONE (`594d804`) The 2.1.147 in-memory dedup (`addToPromptHistory` short-circuit on `lastAddedEntry`) doesn't cover this case — the dup comes from the READ side of `makeLogEntryReader`, not from a missed write-side dedup. Sequence: addToHistory pushes entry into `pendingEntries` and kicks off the async flush; a concurrent up-arrow press starts `getHistory()` and the reader's first phase yields the entry from pendingEntries; the yield suspends the generator, the in-flight flush completes (entry now on disk, `pendingEntries = []`); the reader resumes into its disk pass and yields the same entry again from `history.jsonl`. Fix: snapshot pendingEntries at reader start, record `(timestamp + display)` keys of every entry yielded from memory, filter the same-session disk pass against the set. Same-session scoping matches the existing `skippedTimestamps` precedent and avoids dropping legitimate parallel-session entries with the same display. Smoke: 3-scenario harness (basic flush+read, race during flush, slow-consumer race repro). Reverted fix → slow-consumer scenario yields `slow-consumer × 2`. With fix → single entry; tests 1/3 confirm no false-positive dedup of distinct entries.
- [x] `Fixed tapping the "Jump to bottom" pill in fullscreen mode not dismissing it immediately` — DONE (`2027bdf`) Pill visibility is computed by a `useSyncExternalStore` snapshot at `FullscreenLayout.tsx` reading `scrollTop + viewportHeight < dividerY`. On click, `jumpToNew()` calls `scrollToBottom()` which fires `notify()` synchronously, but `scrollTop` hasn't been pinned to `maxScroll` yet (that happens in the next render pass via `render-node-to-output.ts`'s stickyScroll handling) — so the immediate snapshot re-read still sees the OLD scrollTop and the pill stays visible until the next scroll event. Fix: synchronously clear `dividerYRef.current = null` in the `onPillClick` handler BEFORE calling `jumpToNew()`. The snapshot returns `false` when `dividerY == null`, so the listener fired by `scrollToBottom()`'s `notify()` sees the cleared ref and the pill unmounts on the immediate React commit. `dividerIndex` is left alone so the in-transcript "N new messages" divider line still marks where new content started, matching `jumpToNew()`'s existing documented behavior of clearing the pill anchor without clearing the divider line. The ref-only mutation (no setState) means no extra render is forced. One-line addition to REPL.tsx's existing `onPillClick` arrow.
- [-] `Improved /feedback reports to include the conversation that happened before context compaction, making issues from earlier in long sessions easier to triage` — SKIP (/feedback ships transcripts to Anthropic; not our channel)

## 2.1.148

- [-] `Fixed the Bash tool returning exit code 127 on every command for some users (a regression introduced in 2.1.147)` — SKIP (regression in 2.1.147 we never shipped)

## 2.1.147

- [~] `Pinned background sessions (Ctrl+T in claude agents) now stay alive when idle, are restarted in place to apply Claude Code updates, and are shed under memory pressure only after non-pinned sessions` — PARTIAL (`0e2cced`) User-visible parts of the feature land: new `agents/pinnedSessions.ts` (~120 LOC) flat-JSON store at `~/.claude/pinned-sessions.json` keyed by SessionInfo.name; `Ctrl+T` in agents-view toggles pin for focused row; SessionRow renders a `●` glyph in 'permission' color when pinned; `getMergedSessions` annotates rows with `pinned?: boolean` and sorts pinned-first across all status groups; `pruneStalePinned` drops entries whose session no longer exists so the file doesn't accumulate. HelpOverlay + bottom hint updated. The lifecycle behaviors are no-ops in our fork — we don't ship an idle-session reaper (so "stay alive when idle" holds trivially), an in-place-restart supervisor, or a memory-pressure shedder. The pin marker + file format mirror what those subsystems would read, so when/if we add the lifecycle infrastructure they can read the existing JSON directly with no migration.
- [-] `Renamed /simplify to /code-review. It now reports correctness bugs at a chosen effort level (e.g., /code-review high); pass --comment to post findings as inline GitHub PR comments. The old cleanup-and-fix behavior has been removed` — SKIP (we already ship `/code-review` as a bundled skill via a different lineage — `src/skills/bundled/index.ts` plus the `code-review` allowlist entry; we never had `/simplify` to rename. The new behavior overlaps with our `/bug-hunter` 4-agent pipeline and `/ultrareview` parallel review — comparable surface, separate implementation.)
- [-] `Improved auto-updater: retries transient network failures, reports specific error categories and OS error codes on failure, and shows the current version when an update fails` — SKIP (our auto-updater uses GitHub Releases API, is gated off in source mode per `feedback_no_autoupdate_in_source.md`, and reports failures through the GitHub-flavored path — upstream's npm/GCS-targeted retry logic doesn't apply)
- [x] `Improved diff rendering performance for large file edits` — DONE (`97f7692`) `StructuredDiffFallback#generateWordDiffElements` calls `calculateWordDiffs(removedLineText, addedLineText)` once per line in a paired add/remove diff; both sides of a pair call it with IDENTICAL inputs, so large file edits with hundreds of paired lines paid the `diffWordsWithSpace` cost twice per pair every render. Fix: compute the word diff ONCE per pair during `processLinesForWordDiff` (where the pair is known) and share the result on both members via a new `cachedWordDiffs?: DiffPart[]` field. `generateWordDiffElements` prefers the cached value, falling back to a fresh call only for callers that bypass the processing step (defensive — no live caller does this). Halves diffWordsWithSpace calls on the paired-line path.
- [x] `Prompt history no longer records consecutive duplicate entries — recalling a prompt with arrow-up and submitting it again won't add another copy` — DONE (`70d728f`) `addToPromptHistory` short-circuits when the candidate entry's `display` + `pastedContents` match the in-memory `lastAddedEntry` (already tracked for `removeLastFromHistory`). Pasted-content comparison: shallow key-set + per-id `type` + inline `content` OR `Bun.hash` recompute against stored `contentHash`. Same-session only; cross-session dedup deliberately not done (would require disk read per submit). Smoke: 6 calls in pattern HHH-AA-H produced 3 entries (HHH→1 H, AA→1 A, final H added since no longer consecutive).
- [-] `Fixed enterprise login restrictions (forceLoginOrgUUID and forceLoginMethod managed-settings) not being enforced against third-party-provider and API-key sessions` — SKIP (enterprise managed-settings; not our auth path)
- [x] `Fixed & in ! command output displaying as &amp;, which broke copy-pasting URLs from commands like gcloud auth login on headless machines` — DONE (`80f49c0`) `processBashCommand` wraps `!`-command stdout/stderr in `<bash-stdout>`/`<bash-stderr>` tags via `escapeXml` so the model can parse the XML — the UI was rendering the still-escaped text. Added `unescapeXml` to `src/utils/xml.ts` (replacement order `&lt;` → `&gt;` → `&amp;` last so pre-escaped literals round-trip correctly) and wired it into `UserBashOutputMessage.tsx` after the `extractTag` calls for `bash-stdout` + `persisted-output` + `bash-stderr`. Also rewrote the component as plain JSX, dropping the React-Compiler `_c(10)` memoization scaffold — simpler source easier to maintain. Round-trip smoke: 6 inputs incl. URLs with `&`, `<` text, and a pre-escaped `&amp;` literal all decode byte-for-byte back to original.
- [x] `Fixed unknown slash commands silently doing nothing in headless/SDK mode — they now show an error message` — DONE-BY-EXISTING-IMPL. Our `QueryEngine.ts:447` calls `processUserInput` → `processSlashCommand` for BOTH interactive and headless paths (upstream's bug surface was an SDK path that bypassed processSlashCommand). The `Unknown skill: <name>` message becomes `resultText` (`processSlashCommand.tsx:373`), flows into the `type: 'result'` emission at `QueryEngine.ts:677`. Verified live in `openclaude-interactive` against `claude -p "/totally-fake-command"`: text mode prints `Unknown skill: totally-fake-command`; `--output-format json` and `--output-format stream-json --verbose` both carry the same string in the `result` field of the trailing result event. Exit code stays 0 (`is_error: false`) — upstream's wording "show an error message" doesn't promise a non-zero exit, and flipping it could break SDK consumers that treat the message as informational. If we later want exit=1 we can revisit, but the user-visible "silent failure" bug is already absent.
- [x] `Fixed /help rendering a broken tab header and showing only one command per page on small terminals when not in fullscreen mode` — f7ba057 (HelpV2 maxHeight now uses useModalOrTerminalSize() instead of floor(rows/2); on 24-row terminal visibleCount goes from 1 → 7. Also matches upstream: title "Help" instead of "Claude Code vX.Y.Z" so the tab row does not wrap; footer flexShrink=0; feedback tip gated on rows >= 44)
- [x] `Fixed shell snapshot dropping user functions whose names start with a single underscore, which broke aliases referencing them` — DONE (`67b6ed7`) `ShellSnapshot.ts:getUserSnapshotContent` was filtering functions matching `^_[^_]` (single-underscore prefix) as a noise heuristic for "auto-generated completion functions"; the heuristic was wrong — developers commonly use `_foo` for private-by-convention helpers (Python convention), and aliases referencing them broke after snapshot. Removed the filter from both the zsh `typeset +f` and bash `declare -F` branches. Snapshot files grow modestly (~20-50 functions on a typical Linux box via bash-completion) but no longer silently break aliases. 4-function fixture verified: old filter captured 2/4, new path captures all 4.
- [x] `Fixed plugin agents that declare multiple Agent(...) types in tools: frontmatter dropping all but the last entry` — DONE (`17374e6`) `agentToolUtils.ts:204 resolveAgentTools` was assigning `allowedAgentTypes = ruleContent.split(',')` on each iteration of the tools loop — meaning a manifest like `tools: ['Agent(researcher)', 'Agent(reviewer)', 'Read']` ended with `allowedAgentTypes = ['reviewer']`, researcher silently dropped. Replaced with an accumulating loop that appends + dedupes across entries. Handles 3 legitimate shapes: multiple separate `Agent()` entries, comma-separated inside one entry (`Agent(a, b)`), and mixed forms with overlapping types. 7-case smoke against the isolated merger.
- [-] `Fixed hook if conditions like PowerShell(git push*) never matching — only PowerShell(*) worked` — SKIP (PowerShell tool)
- [-] `Fixed PowerShell tool dropping output for commands that rely on the default formatter` — SKIP (PowerShell tool)
- [-] `Fixed: on Windows, "Yes, and don't ask again" for a PowerShell script invocation now writes a rule that actually matches on subsequent runs` — SKIP (Windows + PowerShell)
- [-] `Fixed PowerShell tool failing on Windows with exit code 1 when pwsh is installed via winget or the Microsoft Store` — SKIP (Windows + PowerShell)
- [x] `Fixed /effort opening with the slider on the wrong level — it now starts at your current effort` — DONE-BY-EXISTING-IMPL. Our `effort.tsx:176-179 EffortSlider` already uses the lazy `useState(() => EFFORT_LEVELS.indexOf(currentLevel))` initializer keyed on `getDisplayedEffortLevel(model, effortValue)` — same resolver as the status bar, so the slider opens at "your current effort" by construction. Verified via 9-case smoke covering opus-4-6/sonnet-4-6/non-effort-supporting models × low/medium/high/xhigh/max/undefined effortValue: every case returns the matching `EFFORT_LEVELS.indexOf` for the displayed level. Upstream's bug was a hardcoded initial-state default that we don't have.
- [x] `Fixed paginating MCP servers dropping resources, templates, and prompts past page 1` — DONE (`2ac946d`) `services/mcp/client.ts` called `tools/list`, `resources/list`, `prompts/list` exactly once each and ignored any `nextCursor` in the response; servers like Atlassian or larger CMS bridges paginate at ~50 items per page, and everything past page 1 was silently dropped. Added a `drainPaginatedList<R, Item>` helper that follows the cursor chain (passing `{cursor}` on subsequent requests) until `nextCursor` is absent; hard cap at 100 pages so a misbehaving server can't pin the client in an infinite loop. Wired all three list endpoints through the helper (preserving the upstream 2.1.132 retry-once envelope around `tools/list`). 4-case smoke against a mock client: 1-page / 3-page / empty / mixed-page-sizes all drain to the expected total.
- [-] `Fixed full-screen strobing in attached background sessions on Windows Terminal while Claude is streaming` — SKIP (Windows Terminal)
- [-] `Fixed: on Windows, removing a background-job worktree no longer follows NTFS junctions into the main repo` — SKIP (Windows NTFS junctions)
- [x] `Fixed /background refusing sessions whose only typed input was a skill or custom slash command` — DONE (`0eb3d6d`) Our equivalent is `claude --bg` + the `/bg` slash. `handleBgFlag` had two latent bugs that combined to break slash-only (and any multi-word) prompts: (1) **shell-quoting** — `filteredArgs.join(' ')` produced a tmux command line that the shell re-tokenized, so `claude --bg "/super on"` became `claude /super on …` and commander errored with "too many arguments"; (2) **`--proactive` over-injection** — the auto-`--proactive` gate only suppressed itself for `-p`/`--print`, not for positional prompts, so a user-supplied slash/skill got `--proactive` appended, overriding their intent. Fix: added a `shellQuoteArg()` helper that single-quotes any arg containing whitespace/metacharacters before joining, and extended the gate to detect any non-flag positional and skip the proactive default. Verified end-to-end against `openclaude-interactive`: `claude --bg "/super on"` and `claude --bg "/buddy hatch"` both spawn cleanly and the spawned session dispatches the slash through normal slash-command routing (proved by `/buddy hatch` producing the actual `/buddy` help banner — the slash arrived as a single positional, not two split tokens); the latent multi-word non-slash regression (`claude --bg "hello world"`) is also fixed by the same quoting change.
- [-] `Fixed auto mode suppressing AskUserQuestion when the user or a skill explicitly relies on it; the auto-mode classifier now sees the user's answers as intent signal` — SKIP. Our /super (proactive/auto-mode equivalent) doesn't ship a code-level AskUserQuestion suppressor — the only "suppression" is a system-prompt nudge in `super-impl.ts:15` telling the model "AskUserQuestion auto-answers under /super". The model can still call AskUserQuestion if it judges the call genuinely necessary; nothing in our code blocks it. The upstream bug is "the classifier was suppressing the tool entirely AND not learning from user answers", but our classifier (`yoloClassifier.ts`) is a permission-classifier for tool calls, not a meta-classifier that learns from AskUserQuestion answers. The fix would need a classifier-feedback subsystem we don't ship.
- [-] `Fixed /theme "New custom theme" and color editor dialogs not responding to Esc` — SKIP (UI flow doesn't exist in our fork). Our `/theme` only renders `ThemePicker` (`src/components/ThemePicker.tsx`) which is a single `<Select>` over built-in themes + any disk-based custom themes discovered at `~/.claude/themes/*.json` (the 2.1.118 feature, backported in `f4a2df4` + `8ed9177`). There is no "New custom theme" entry in the picker, no interactive create/name dialog, and no in-CLI color editor — users author custom themes by hand-editing JSON files. The `<Select>` already cancels cleanly on Esc via the existing `onCancel` wiring in `theme.tsx`. Upstream's 2.1.146/2.1.147-ish "create theme from /theme" + per-color editor UI was never backported (the related parent-feature backlog entry on line 734 was DONE only for the file-based half). Flipping a `useInput` Esc handler in a dialog we don't have is a no-op; the fix doesn't have a target surface. To revisit: port the create-theme + color-editor dialogs first, then this Esc fix becomes meaningful. Verified via `grep -rn "New custom\|Create custom\|themeName\|writeCustomTheme\|saveCustomTheme\|ThemeEditor\|ColorEditor" src/` returning no theme-UI hits (the `ColorPicker` matches are for sub-agent icon-color picking, unrelated).
- [-] `Fixed an uncaught exception at the end of streaming sessions when running via the Agent SDK` — SKIP (Agent SDK exit path; we don't ship the SDK entry surface)
- [-] `Fixed a rare hang when waiting for scroll to settle on Windows` — SKIP (Windows)
- [-] `Fixed stale and doubled rows in the agent view list on Windows when background session results contain wide (CJK) characters` — SKIP (Windows + CJK width tables)
- [x] `Fixed pasted text being delivered to agents as an unreadable [Pasted text #N] placeholder instead of the actual content` — DONE (`71fdf6d`) Both `AskUserQuestionPermissionRequest` (notes field, per-question paste store) and `ExitPlanModePermissionRequest` (on-accept feedback field, dialog-local paste store) now call `expandPastedTextRefs(value, store)` at the submit boundary. AskUserQuestion uses a `pastedContentsByQuestionRef` re-synced every render so the React-Compiler-memoized submitAnswers callback (whose memo-cache key doesn't include the paste store) reads the live map at submit time. ExitPlanMode expands `planFeedback` before threading `acceptFeedback` into the plan-implementation prompt or the tool-result feedback string. Subagent now sees the actual pasted content instead of the placeholder.
- [x] `Fixed plugin component counts in claude plugin details and /plugin being doubled when a plugin's manifest listed paths overlapping its default directories` — DONE (`7ae3b4c`) `pluginComponents.ts collectPluginComponents` was iterating `plugin.commandsPath` + `plugin.commandsPaths` (same for agents/skills) without dedup. When a manifest's path overlapped a default-directory convention, both pointed at the same on-disk directory and every file was counted twice. Added `dedupePathsByRealpath` helper using `fs.realpath` (with `path.resolve` fallback on ENOENT) — mirrors the loader's safeResolvePath/isDuplicatePath key so symlinked checkouts fold together correctly. Second-layer dedup at the basename level (`Array.from(new Set(...))`) covers the rarer case of distinct dirs containing files with the same stem (runtime loader already silently shadows the second). Both `claude plugin details` CLI surface and the interactive `/plugin` pane consume the same arrays via `join(', ')` so this fixes both surfaces (and `--json` output) in one shot. End-to-end smoke: 3 identical dir refs producing 6 entries collapse to 2. Parallel-agent dispatch.
- [-] `Fixed backgrounded sessions re-prompting for tool permissions you already granted with "don't ask again"` — SKIP (upstream daemon-mode tmux-respawn cycle; our tmux-based `--bg` keeps the same process so granted permissions persist naturally — verified no re-prompt path exists in `cli/bg.ts`)
- [x] `Fixed GNOME Terminal right-click and middle-click paste not inserting text` — DONE (`135adea`) Root cause: `parse-keypress.ts#parseMultipleKeypresses` text-token branch detected mouse escape sequences anywhere in a token and swallowed the ENTIRE token. GNOME Terminal's middle-click paste (PRIMARY selection) and right-click → context-menu Paste emit `\x1b[<button;col;rowM<paste-content>` as one stdin chunk when mouse tracking is on (fullscreen mode); the swallow-all branch silently discarded the paste payload. Fix: split the token at the mouse-sequence boundary — emit `before` + `seq` (mouse event) + `after` (paste content) as separate keypresses. Defensive fallback preserves the original swallow path if `.match()` diverges from `.test()` after the predicate succeeds. Doesn't affect non-fullscreen mode (no mouse tracking → no mouse sequences in text tokens), bracketed paste (consumed by the inPaste accumulator above), or pure mouse events (before/after empty, swallowed as before).
- [-] `Fixed CLAUDE_CODE_SUBAGENT_MODEL not applying to teammate processes spawned by agent teams` — SKIP (Agent Teams — Anthropic-specific multi-process orchestration; we use `/coordinator` + `runForkedAgent` instead, which already honor the same env var via `getDefaultModel()` → `process.env.CLAUDE_CODE_SUBAGENT_MODEL`)
- [x] `Fixed slash commands followed by a tab or newline being treated as an unknown command` — DONE (`7c12c38`) Three slash-parse call sites split on SPACE only (`slashCommandParsing.ts:50` via `split(' ')`, `handlePromptSubmit.ts:234` via `indexOf(' ')`, `REPL.tsx:3455` via `indexOf(' ')`), gluing tab/newline-separated args to the command-name token. Switched all three to first-whitespace search (`/\s/`) and preserved args verbatim past the boundary so quoted strings + multi-spaces + inner-tabs survive. 7-case smoke verifies `/help\targ`, `/help\narg` now route to /help (were UNKNOWN); `/help   foo  bar` preserves the inner double-space.
- [-] `Fixed several spacing and layout glitches in the /plugin, /status, /mobile, /sandbox, and /permissions menus` — SKIP. We don't ship `/mobile` or `/sandbox` commands; only `/plugin`, `/status`, `/permissions` apply. Inspected the three: `/status` (`Settings/Status.tsx`) has minimal spacing (one `paddingX={1}` on diagnostic rows); `/plugin` and `/permissions` already render through the same `Dialog` component that enforces consistent paddings via the design system. Without the upstream source diff or a specific glitch screenshot, can't tell which paddingX/marginTop the upstream tweak changed without risking visual regression. Existing layouts have been live-verified across the L497 narrow-terminal pass + the L56 (`/help`) row-count pass we shipped, so the dialogs are in a working state for our user-visible widths.
- [x] `Fixed stripped images prompting the model to repeatedly re-read media that was no longer present` — DONE (`77d73f0`) `stripImagesFromMessages` was replacing image/document blocks with bare `[image]` / `[document]` markers, which the model read as "media is present, please look at it" cues and called Read on the remembered file path — each re-read got stripped on the next compaction, producing the same marker, ad infinitum. Changed placeholders to `[image removed]` / `[document removed]` (past-tense signal). Covers all three call sites of the export (`streamCompactSummary` pre-API trim, `reactiveCompact.ts:171` media-size error recovery, direct compaction pipeline). Other literal `[image]` references in `BriefTool/UI.tsx:103` (compose-time attachment label) and `openaiBridge/requestTranslator.ts:166/178/206` (document fallback shapes) untouched — separate semantics from summarization. Verified across all three block shapes (top-level image, image nested in tool_result.content, top-level document).

## 2.1.145

- [x] `Added claude agents --json to list live Claude sessions as JSON for scripting (tmux-resurrect, status bars, session pickers)` — DONE (`c459e46`) `cli/handlers/agentsView.tsx` short-circuits BEFORE the Ink mount when `opts.json` is set — reuses `getMergedSessions(cwdFilter)` from `agents/sessionMerge.ts`, maps each `MergedAgentRow` to a public JSON shape (id/pid/name/cwd/status/startedAt/lastUpdated/lastMessage/turnCount + optional jobId/prUrl), emits via `console.log(JSON.stringify(out, null, 2))`, exits 0. `main.tsx` commander registration extended with `.option('--json', ...)`. Composes with `--cwd` (2.1.141) so callers can scope. The MergedAgentRow shape was already a clean public schema (no internal-only fields), so the mapping is straight passthrough — only stripping the type discriminator. Live-verified in container: `claude agents --json | jq 'type, length'` → `"array", 0` (well-formed, empty in container with no live sessions). Cheatsheet updated.
- [x] `Added agent_id and parent_agent_id attributes to claude_code.tool OTEL spans, and fixed trace parenting so background subagent spans nest under the dispatching Agent tool span` — DONE (`7d91426`) `recordToolObservation` was exported but never invoked in our fork (the legacy `sessionTracing.ts#startToolSpan`/`endToolSpan` paths are noops). Wired it into `services/tools/toolExecution.ts` success + error branches, emitting a span per tool call with the new `agentId`/`parentAgentId`/`endTime` params (`endTime` lets the dashboard render realistic durations for long-running tools — without it, `span.end()` defaults to Date.now() at the post-tool tick and collapses long tools to near-zero duration). `createSubagentTrace` gains `parent: ObserveTrace | null` + `parentAgentId: string` params; `AgentTool#dispatch` passes `toolUseContext.observeTrace` (agent-run root or dispatching subagent span) + `toolUseContext.agentId` so the subagent span nests correctly. Attributes emitted in both dotless (`agent_id`) and dotted (`agent.id`) forms — the exporter reads the dotted form, legacy dashboards key on dotless. Telemetry errors swallowed so they can't block tool execution.
- [x] `Status line JSON input now includes GitHub repo and PR information when detected` — DONE (`afff457`) New `src/types/statusLine.ts` formalizes the JSON shape (was inline only) and adds optional `gh.repo` (`{owner, name, full_name, host}`) + `gh.pr` (`{number, state, title?, base?, head?, url?, review_state?}`) subfields. `src/utils/ghStatusCache.ts` is a sync module-level cache mirroring `getRawUtilization()` so `buildStatusLineCommandInput` (runs every ~300ms in a non-async callback) can include the new fields without IO. `fetchPrStatus()` extended to request `baseRefName,title` from `gh pr view --json` and carry them on `PrStatus`. `usePrStatus` mirrors fetched PR data into `setCachedGhPr` on every poll; a sister effect calls `detectCurrentRepositoryWithHost()` once on mount and pushes into `setCachedGhRepo` (NOT gated on `enabled` — repo detection only reads `.git/config`, no `gh` shell-out, so the user's `prStatusFooterEnabled: false` opt-out doesn't apply). `StatusLine.tsx` subscribes via `subscribeCachedGhStatus` and schedules a status-line refresh on cache change so the first frame's JSON includes `gh.*` instead of waiting for user input. Smoke-test in openclaude-interactive against a fresh repo with `origin = github.com/coffeegrind123/openclaude.git` captured stdin JSON with `gh.repo: {owner: "coffeegrind123", name: "openclaude", full_name: "coffeegrind123/openclaude", host: "github.com"}` — `gh.pr` absent (no open PR on main, correct).
- [x] `/plugin Discover and Browse screens now show a plugin's commands, agents, skills, hooks, and MCP/LSP servers before installation` — DONE (`3c2989a`) New `commands/plugin/pluginInstallSummary.ts` (~170 LOC) is the single source of truth: given a `PluginMarketplaceEntry`, returns an `InstallSummary` covering all 6 categories (commands/agents/skills/hooks/mcpServers/lspServers) with counts + truncated label lists. Handles inline object/record (keys), inline array (prettified basenames), and bare-string path shapes. `BrowseMarketplace.tsx` replaces its inline "Will install" block (which only handled 4 of 6 categories) with `summarizePluginInstall(entry)` + `formatCategoryLine`. `DiscoverPlugins.tsx` adds the same section in parity (didn't exist before). Both list-row paths get a compact `formatInstallBadge` line ("3 commands · 2 MCP servers") under the description. Pre-install attribution only works when marketplace entry ships inline contents (which `PluginManifestSchema().partial()` supports); plugins that only declare `source: github` fall through to "Component summary not available for this remote plugin (full contents will be loaded after install)". Future enhancement: pre-install manifest fetch via raw GitHub URL.
- [x] `claude agents terminal tab title now shows the awaiting-input count so an alt-tabbed window tells you when an agent needs attention` — DONE (`e45794f`) `AgentsPanel.tsx` wires the existing `useTerminalTitle` hook (`src/ink/hooks/use-terminal-title.ts`) with text `Agents · N awaiting` when `counts.awaiting > 0` and plain `Agents` otherwise. Routes through OSC 0 on POSIX and `process.title` on Windows. We don't snapshot the prior title (no portable way to read it back) so the title persists after the dashboard closes — generally fine because the typical exit path is `Enter` → tmux-attach which rewrites the title immediately.
- [x] `Slash command and @-mention suggestion list now supports mouse hover and click in fullscreen mode` — f7ba057 (useTypeahead exposes acceptSuggestionAt + setSelectedSuggestionIndex; PromptOverlayData carries onSelectAt + onHoverAt; PromptInputFooterSuggestions wraps each row in a Box with onClick + onMouseEnter; FullscreenLayout SuggestionsOverlay forwards both callbacks. Inline path also wired for AlternateScreen mouse capture even though only fullscreen has it enabled today)
- [x] `Stop and SubagentStop hook input now includes background_tasks and session_crons fields` — DONE (`a501275`) Added `StopHookBackgroundTaskSchema` `{id, type, status, description?}` + `StopHookSessionCronSchema` `{id, cron, prompt, recurring?, lastFiredAt?}` to `coreSchemas.ts`; both `StopHookInputSchema` and `SubagentStopHookInputSchema` gain optional `background_tasks` + `session_crons` arrays. `executeStopHooks` populates via two best-effort collectors: `collectStopHookBackgroundTasks` enumerates `getAppState().tasks` filtered to `running`/`starting` (and by `task.agentId === subagentId` for SubagentStop scope); `collectStopHookSessionCrons` lazy-imports `listAllCronTasks` and applies the same agentId filter. Conditional-spread so the existing payload shape is unchanged when neither array exists. Both collectors swallow errors.
- [x] `Fixed a permission-prompt bypass where bare variable assignments to non-allowlisted environment variables in Bash commands were auto-approved` — DONE (`8798a76`) `ast.ts` threads a `bareAssignments` accumulator alongside `commands[]` through `collectCommands` + `walkRedirectedStatement`; statement-level `variable_assignment` nodes now record `{name, value}` before returning null. Substitution subshells get a throwaway accumulator so `echo $(FOO=bar)` doesn't trigger an outer ask. `bashPermissions.ts` adds an early-return when `astResult.commands.length === 0 && bareAssignments.length > 0` — any name not in `SAFE_ENV_VARS` (or `ANT_ONLY_SAFE_ENV_VARS` for ant users) → ask. Allowlisted names (NO_COLOR/LANG/CI/NODE_ENV/etc) keep falling through to vacuous allow. Chained `FOO=bar && ls` and command env-prefix `NODE_ENV=prod npm run build` unaffected (cmds>0). Verified via parser smoke: `LD_PRELOAD=/evil/path` → bare=[LD_PRELOAD] → ASK; `NO_COLOR=1` → bare=[NO_COLOR] → allow; `VAR=$(rm -rf /)` → cmds=[rm -rf /] (inner extracted, outer bare ignored).
- [x] `Fixed MCP prompt slash commands showing raw server validation errors when a required argument is omitted — the error now names the missing argument and shows expected usage` — DONE (`3fe9495`) `getPromptForCommand` in `services/mcp/client.ts` pre-validates required args from `prompt.arguments[*].required` BEFORE the `prompts/get` RPC. Missing required args produce a structured error naming every missing field + a slash-command usage template with `<required>`/`[optional]` placeholders. Optional args still propagate as undefined to the server (no shape change).
- [x] `Fixed the spinner and elapsed-time display freezing until a keypress after the terminal was resized or refocused` — DONE (`550850b`) Root cause in `src/ink/hooks/use-terminal-viewport.ts`: `useTerminalViewport`'s layout effect updated `entryRef.current.isVisible` without triggering a re-render (the comment explicitly cited cascading-re-render avoidance). That's fine while ticks are firing — each tick re-renders the consumer which reads the fresh ref — but once `useAnimationFrame`'s effect cleaned up because `isVisible` flipped false (transient layout reflow during resize can push the spinner past `viewportBottom`; same for refocus when the alt-screen redraw repaints fewer rows momentarily), there's no tick channel left. The layout effect's next run computes `isVisible: true` again and updates the ref, but nothing reads it → animation stays dead until an unrelated re-render (keypress, parent state change) wakes the component up. Fix: (1) add a `useState` wake counter whose setter is invoked from the layout effect ONLY on the `false → true` visibility edge — gives consumers a re-render channel exactly when the bug otherwise leaves them frozen; the reverse edge (`true → false`) keeps the old ref-only behavior so we don't risk feedback loops with sibling layout effects whose layout depends on whether the animated child is visible; (2) subscribe to `TerminalFocusContext` alongside `TerminalSizeContext` so refocus events trigger a re-render of every viewport consumer, giving the layout effect a chance to re-evaluate against the post-refocus layout. UI-driver scenario `spinner-resize-freeze.json` ships the harness shell; a deterministic in-driver repro requires a fixture that reliably pushes the spinner past `viewportBottom` during the resize transient (host bare-REPL setup keeps it in bounds at 120x40 → 100x30) so the scenario is left in tree for the next session that sees the bug live. Build clean; smoke `-p "say hi"` OK in container; existing OffscreenFreeze + Ratchet callers benefit from the same wake-up edge (they cache children based on isVisible).
- [-] `Fixed the cross-project resume hint failing in default Windows PowerShell 5.1 — Windows now uses ; as the command separator` — SKIP (Windows PowerShell 5.1)
- [-] `Fixed voice push-to-talk not working in the agent view's reply pane` — SKIP. Our agents-view dashboard's reply pane (`components/agents-view/InlineReplyInput.tsx`) is a plain text-input wrapping our standard `TextInput`. Voice push-to-talk (`voice:pushToTalk` keybinding) is bound globally at the input level by `VoiceProvider` (`state/AppState.tsx`), so the inline reply pane DOES route Space → pushToTalk when the voice subsystem is enabled (our `voiceLocal` STT). Verified by reading `InlineReplyInput.tsx` — no input wrapper that would intercept the space key. Upstream's bug was a missing event handler in their reply pane component; ours doesn't have that surface. Revisit only if a user reports voice keybindings not firing in the pane.
- [x] `Fixed task lists rendering in random order when several tasks are created at once` — DONE (`b865796`) `TaskCreateTool.isConcurrencySafe()` was returning `true`, so when the model emitted multiple `TaskCreate` blocks in one assistant turn, `toolOrchestration.partitionToolCalls` batched them and dispatched via `runToolsConcurrently → all()`. `createTask` serializes ID allocation through a lockfile (5-100ms exponential backoff), but the order in which parallel promises win the lock is non-deterministic — so `subj-2` could get id 1 while `subj-1` got id 5. `TaskListV2` sorts by numeric id, producing the random visible order. Flipping `isConcurrencySafe()` to `false` forces serial dispatch via `runToolsSerially` which iterates the input array in order, so id N → Nth tool_use block. Smoke-tested 10× direct against `createTask`: pre-fix parallel produced 0/10 stable orderings; post-fix serial produces 10/10 stable orderings.
- [-] `Fixed stale "Failed to install Anthropic marketplace" banner showing when the marketplace is already installed` — SKIP (Anthropic-owned marketplace)
- [x] `Fixed the PR badge in the footer not updating immediately after gh pr create and other PR-state-changing commands run in-session` — DONE (`1fc73d1`) Added a lightweight pub-sub at `src/utils/prStatusRefresh.ts`: anchored regex matches `gh pr <verb>` for the 11 state-changing verbs (create/close/reopen/merge/ready/edit/review/comment/update-branch/lock/unlock; read-only verbs view/list/diff/status/checks excluded), module-level listener set with throw isolation. `usePrStatus` subscribes via a second `useEffect` keyed on `enabled` (independent of polling effect so subscription survives isLoading toggles between turns) that calls a mutable ref set by the polling effect — the ref clears any pending timer, bumps idle-stop activity timestamp, and kicks off an immediate poll. Polling fn gains a single-flight `inflight` guard so a refresh signal landing during a scheduled poll doesn't double-fire `gh pr view`. `runPostToolUseHooks` (services/tools/toolHooks.ts) fires the refresh at the top of the success path when tool is `Bash` and command matches. Failures skipped intentionally — failed `gh pr create` didn't change server state. 19/19 regex cases verified, pub/sub isolation verified, build clean, container hot-patch + `-p` smoke OK.
- [-] `Fixed Agent Teams teammates with non-ASCII names failing every API call due to invalid header encoding` — SKIP (Agent Teams; see 2.1.147 entry above)
- [-] `Fixed /review using a deprecated projectCards GraphQL query that errored on repos with Classic Projects` — SKIP (not applicable). Our `/review` (`src/commands/review.ts`) is a plain-prompt command that shells out to `gh pr view/diff/list` only — no GraphQL. The bundled `/code-review` plugin skill (`work/.claude/plugins/marketplaces/claude-plugins-official/plugins/code-review/commands/code-review.md`) likewise uses only `gh pr view/diff/comment/list` — no GraphQL. Exhaustive grep across `src/` for `projectCards` / `projectsV2` / `gh api graphql` returned zero matches. Nothing to fix in our fork.
- [x] `Fixed claude plugin validate not flagging skills: entries that point at a file instead of a directory — the error now suggests the parent directory` — e716a4d. Added a filesystem-stat pass over `manifest.skills` in `validatePluginManifest()` (src/utils/plugins/validatePlugin.ts) that resolves each entry relative to the plugin root (parent of `.claude-plugin/`), stat()s the result, and on `!isDirectory()` emits a targeted error naming the field, the offending path, and (when the path ends in SKILL.md) the parent-directory suggestion. Missing paths still defer to the existing load-time path-not-found pipeline to avoid duplicating error surface.
- [x] `Fixed an infinite loop where a skill using context: fork could repeatedly re-invoke itself instead of running` — DONE (`7ea3e0f`) Added `QueryChainTracking.forkedSkillStack?: readonly string[]` to `Tool.ts`. `executeForkedSkill` checks `parentStack.includes(commandName)` before spawning and throws a structured error naming the chain (`A → B → my-skill → my-skill`) when re-entry is detected. Caps overall nesting at `MAX_SKILL_FORK_DEPTH = 8` for unbounded A→B→A-style mutual recursion. Stack propagated to sub-agent's queryTracking so deeper forks see the chain.
- [x] `Improved the Read tool to return a truncated first page with a "PARTIAL view" notice instead of a hard error when a whole-file read exceeds the token limit` — DONE (`b181945`) `FileReadTool.callInner` wraps `validateContentTokens` in try/catch — on `MaxFileReadTokenExceededError` AND `limit === undefined`, re-reads with `readFileInRange(..., { truncateOnByteLimit: true })` using `maxTokens * 3` as the byte budget (matches the existing 4× fast-reject ratio with headroom; lineCount stays exact rather than slicing in JS). `readFileState` gets `isPartialView: true` so Edit/Write require a fresh Read before mutating partial-view files. The existing post-data truncation nudge gains a `PARTIAL view:` prefix + `(the whole file was too large to fit in one read — paginate with offset to see the rest)` suffix on this path. Build clean; container hot-patch + `-p` smoke OK.

## 2.1.144

- [x] `Added /resume support for background sessions — sessions started via claude --bg or agent view now appear alongside interactive ones, marked with bg` — DONE (`588da5b`) bg sessions already wrote to the normal session JSONL (they're regular CLI processes running in tmux) so they were ALREADY appearing in /resume — only the visual marker was missing. New `agents/liveBackground.ts#getLiveBackgroundSessionIds()` cross-references `listLiveSessions()` (the same source `claude agents` uses) and returns the Set of sessionIds bound to a currently-running `kind: 'bg'` process. `commands/resume/resume.tsx#loadLogs` tags matching rows with `LogOption.isLiveBackground=true`. `components/LogSelector.tsx#buildLogLabel` adds a ` (bg)` suffix next to the existing ` (sidechain)` suffix in both list and search-results paths (sidechain wins on collision — structural fact beats launch mode). Foreground REPLs (`kind: 'interactive'`) and daemon kinds skipped. Errors swallowed → empty set, picker still works without the marker.
- [x] `Added elapsed duration to background subagent completion notifications (e.g. "Agent completed · 3h 2m 5s")` — DONE (`b181945`) `enqueueAgentNotification` in `LocalAgentTask.tsx` now appends `· <duration>` (via existing `formatDuration` helper) to completion / failure / stopped summaries when `usage.durationMs` is set. Existing usage plumbing already carries durationMs; one-line summary string change matches upstream wording verbatim.
- [x] `The /plugin browse and discover panes now show when a plugin was last updated` — DONE (`f4beb19`) Earlier DEFERRED rationale ("significant new infra") was overestimated: implementation came in at ~315 LOC across one new module + Browse + Discover wiring, no schema changes needed. New `utils/plugins/pluginLastUpdated.ts` (~190 LOC): `getCachedPluginLastUpdated` (sync TTL'd read of `~/.claude/plugins/last-updated-cache.json`, 6h TTL), `refreshPluginLastUpdated` (async — runs `git -C <marketplaceInstallLocation> log -1 --format=%cI -- <entry.source>` for github/git source-type marketplaces, 5s timeout, persists null results to avoid re-running git log on stale lookups), `getMarketplaceInstallLocation` (sync read of `known_marketplaces.json` so callers don't have to plumb installLocation through props), `formatLastUpdated` (humanized "3h ago"/"5 days ago"/"2 months ago" with absolute-date fallback for >12-month-old entries). `BrowseMarketplace.tsx` + `DiscoverPlugins.tsx` mount a small `PluginLastUpdatedLine` component in the plugin-details pane right after Version. Reads cached value synchronously on mount, fires refresh-and-cache in parallel, re-renders when ready. Hides itself on null. Non-git sources (npm/pip/url) return null cleanly — no flicker, no "—" placeholder. Container hot-patch + `-p` smoke OK.
- [x] `/model now changes the model for the current session only; press d in the model picker to set a default for new sessions` — DONE (`a016f5b`) Half already true in our fork — `commands/model/model.tsx#handleSelect` only mutates `setAppState` (no userSettings.model write), so Enter is already session-only. New `modelPicker:setDefault` keybinding (schema.ts + defaultBindings.ts, bound to `d`) calls `updateSettingsForSource('userSettings', { model: <focused> })` and shows a transient success-colored confirmation. `ModelPicker.tsx` rewritten from React-Compiler-baked output to plain source TS (precedent: DiffDialog/DiffDetailView per L16 backport) so the new useKeybindings handler can be added cleanly. Header text updated + new "Default for new sessions: <X>" line + `d` shortcut surfaced in the bottom byline. NO_PREFERENCE → undefined clears the saved default.
- [-] `Renamed "extra usage" to "usage credits" across CLI copy; /extra-usage is now /usage-credits (old name still works)` — SKIP (we don't ship /extra-usage; claude.ai billing surface)
- [x] `Fixed startup hanging up to 75s when api.anthropic.com is unreachable (captive portal, firewall, VPN issues) — side-channel API calls now time out after 15s` — DONE (`83f514e`) Added explicit `timeout: 15000` (axios first-class field) to 8 side-channel API call sites across 4 files: `fastMode.ts` (1 site — fastMode status prefetch), `grove.ts` (3 sites — getGroveSettings GET, markGroveNoticeViewed POST, updateGroveSettings PATCH), `overageCreditGrant.ts` (1 site — fetchOverageCreditGrant GET), `adminRequests.ts` (3 sites — createAdminRequest/getMyAdminRequests/checkAdminRequestEligibility). Preserved all existing try/catch + log + cache-skip/null-return fallback paths. No wrapper helper introduced (axios native timeout is the right tool). Other startup paths (bootstrap, metricsOptOut, referral, usage, getOauthProfile, settingsSync, zaiQuota, deepseekBalance, openrouterCredits, etc.) already had timeouts via earlier fork work — verified with grep. Parallel-agent dispatch.
- [x] `Fixed garbled terminal output after a missed window-resize event (e.g. dragging a VS Code split-pane divider) — now self-heals on the next frame instead of requiring Ctrl+L` — f7ba057 (per-frame self-heal in onRender(): if stdout.columns/rows differ from cached terminalColumns/Rows, route through handleResize() before painting. handleResize already dedupes same-dim events to zero work)
- [-] `Fixed progressive terminal display corruption (stale/garbled glyphs) that could appear in very long sessions and only cleared on terminal resize or restart` — SKIP. Distinct from L113 (resize-event-loss self-heal, which we shipped in `f7ba057`) — that one fires when dimensions diverge; this one fires WITHOUT a dimension change. Most likely root cause: Ink's cell-buffer diff-render reuses stale cells when a long-running session accumulates enough state that a width-cached calculation goes stale (string-width memoization key collision, ANSI segmentation cache eviction, etc.). Without a concrete repro path or the upstream source diff we can't identify the right cache to invalidate. Adding speculative "clear cell buffer every N frames" would mask the bug AND tax rendering perf. Revisit if a user reports the garbled-glyph state with a recipe.
- [x] `Reduced terminal rendering glitches in VS Code by reducing spinner animation color count` — DONE (`c955213`) `components/Spinner/utils.ts#interpolateColor` quantizes `t` to 8 discrete steps (snap to nearest 1/8). Without quantization the smooth lerp from messageColor → ERROR_RED during a stalled-state transition emitted up to ~30 distinct RGB values per second (50ms frame cadence × stalled-intensity ramp); VS Code's xterm.js doesn't coalesce per-frame SGR sequences with novel RGB values as efficiently as iTerm2/Kitty/Ghostty, so the rapid churn surfaced as visible flicker. Quantizing to 8 buckets caps the emitted color set at 9 unique values across the whole transition. Native terminals render the quantized output indistinguishably from the smooth-lerp version (50ms × 8 steps = 400ms full traversal — under the eye's stepping-detection threshold at that cadence).
- [-] `Fixed macOS background sessions crashing with "exit 1 before init" when the project lives under a Full Disk Access-protected folder (regression in 2.1.143)` — SKIP (macOS Full Disk Access)
- [x] `Fixed an unrecoverable conversation when reading a file whose image extension doesn't match its contents (e.g. HTML saved as .png) — now falls back to text` — DONE (`f4c2993`) `FileReadTool.callInner()` IMAGE_EXTENSIONS branch now sniffs the first 12 bytes against PNG/JPEG/GIF/WebP/BMP magic-byte signatures BEFORE committing to the image pipeline. On mismatch, emits `[FileRead] <path> is not a <EXT>; read as text instead.` to stderr + `tengu_file_read_image_extension_mismatch` analytics event, then falls through to the text-read path. Added companion `detectImageFormatFromBufferStrict()` in `imageResizer.ts` that returns `null` on no-match instead of the existing `detectImageFormatFromBuffer()` which silently defaults to PNG. Smoke-tested: real PNG → `type: image, media_type: image/png`; HTML-as-.png → text with full `<html>...</html>` content + stderr warning; plain-text-as-.jpg → text + stderr warning. Magic bytes checked: PNG `89 50 4E 47 0D 0A 1A 0A`, JPEG `FF D8 FF`, GIF `47 49 46 38 (37|39) 61`, WebP `52 49 46 46 .. .. .. .. 57 45 42 50`, BMP `42 4D`.
- [x] `Fewer spurious tool errors during search: head/tail file views now satisfy the read-before-edit check, and a "no matches" result (exit code 1) from egrep, fgrep, git grep, or git diff is no longer reported as a command failure` — DONE (`10b80e2`) for the grep/diff half; the head/tail half was already in our fork. `commandSemantics.ts`: extracted shared `GREP_SEMANTIC` + `DIFF_SEMANTIC` helpers and registered `egrep`, `fgrep`, `git grep`, `git diff` (existing `grep`/`rg`/`find`/`diff`/`test`/`[` unchanged). To make `git grep` distinct from `git status` (which keeps default semantics), `extractBaseCommand` now returns the two-token `git <sub>` form when the second token is a lowercase identifier. 13-case smoke: 5 new no-error-on-1 (egrep/fgrep/git grep/git diff/git diff --quiet), 2 regression (grep/rg unchanged), 6 still-errors-on-1 (ls/cat/git status/bare-git), 2 still-errors-on-2 (grep+bad path, git grep+bad flag) — all pass. head/tail half: `BashTool.tsx:908-926` already calls `extractViewedFilePath` (handles cat/head/tail/sed-n at `BashTool.tsx:114-149`) and writes a full-read entry into `readFileState` so `FileEditTool`'s read-before-edit gate accepts the file. No work needed there.
- [x] `Fixed /branch failing with "No conversation to branch" after entering a worktree or in some background sessions` — DONE (`41c7987`) `commands/branch/branch.ts createFork` had read/write asymmetry on the project-dir resolver. READS via `getTranscriptPath()` honored the pin-aware fallback `getSessionProjectDir() ?? getProjectDir(getOriginalCwd())`. WRITES (`projectDir = getProjectDir(getOriginalCwd())`, `forkSessionPath = getTranscriptPathForSession(forkSessionId)`) ignored the pin — `getTranscriptPathForSession` only honors the pin when the requested id matches `getSessionId()` which a fresh fork UUID never satisfies. After `EnterWorktreeTool` mid-session, reads landed at the pre-worktree pinned dir while writes landed at the worktree dir, splitting the fork across two locations. Fix: compute `transcriptProjectDir` once with the pin-aware fallback and use it for both mkdir + `join(transcriptProjectDir, \`\${forkSessionId}.jsonl\`)`. Background sessions hit the same shape when the spawning parent had a pinned dir different from getOriginalCwd(). Parallel-agent dispatch.
- [x] `Fixed pressing Escape in the AskUserQuestion notes field aborting the turn instead of returning to answer selection` — DONE (`71fdf6d`) Pass `disableEscapeDoublePress={true}` on the notes TextInput (so `useTextInput`'s double-press "Esc again to clear" doesn't fire) + add a raw Ink `useInput` listener at the PreviewQuestionView level gated on `isInNotesInput` that calls `handleNotesExit()` and `event.stopImmediatePropagation()`. Stops escape from propagating to outer chat:cancel / dialog confirm:no handlers that would tear the AskUserQuestion dialog down.
- [-] `Fixed model selection not applying when changed via the IDE model picker or applyFlagSettings after startup` — SKIP (N/A for our fork) We don't ship the upstream IDE model picker (no `mcp__ide__setModel` channel) and we don't have an `applyFlagSettings` function — our settings flow goes through `applySettingsChange` which deliberately doesn't sync `settings.model` to `AppState.mainLoopModel` because our `sessionPinned.ts` actively protects each session's `/model` choice from cross-session disk-watcher poisoning (see CLAUDE.md "session-model" feature). Both surfaces of the upstream bug don't exist in this fork.
- [x] `Resumed sessions now keep the model they were using instead of picking up another session's /model choice` — 095ce73 — persisted as new `session-model` JSONL entry per session; saveSessionModel at session start + onChangeAppState diff hook; applied on --resume/--continue + interactive /resume + ResumeConversation picker via mainLoopModelOverride + sessionPinned.markLocalModelSelection unless --model passed explicitly
- [-] `Fixed Bedrock and Vertex users unable to select "Opus (1M context)" from the /model picker (regression in v2.1.129)` — SKIP (Bedrock/Vertex auth path)
- [-] `Fixed remote-session login failing with "Can't access this organization" for users with forceLoginMethod and forceLoginOrgUUID set` — SKIP (enterprise managed-settings)
- [x] `Fixed MCP servers with paginated tools/list responses only returning the first page, silently dropping tools` — DONE (`2ac946d`) Already shipped earlier — `drainPaginatedList` helper in `services/mcp/client.ts` follows `nextCursor` up to 100 pages for `tools/list`, `resources/list`, and `prompts/list`. Hard cap prevents pin-loops on misbehaving servers; cap-hit logged via `logMCPError` so operators can see when truncation fired. BACKLOG marker flipped during 2026-05-23 audit pass.
- [x] `Fixed MCP images with unsupported MIME types (e.g. SVG) breaking the conversation — now saved to disk and referenced in the tool result` — DONE (`b181945`) `transformResultContent`'s `image` block in `services/mcp/client.ts` now checks `IMAGE_MIME_TYPES.has(resultContent.mimeType)` first; on miss, routes through `persistBlobToTextBlock` instead of feeding the buffer to `maybeResizeAndDownsampleImageBuffer`. Mirrors the existing `resource`-with-image-blob path's `isImage` gate (which already handled SVG correctly via the same `IMAGE_MIME_TYPES.has()` check). SVG/etc. now save to disk and return a text block with the file path, no longer break the conversation.
- [x] `Fixed file descriptor exhaustion when a build runs inside a skill directory — non-.md files no longer trigger skill reloads` — DONE (`a4c1386`) `skillChangeDetector.ts` chokidar watcher fired on every file change in a skill dir including build artifacts / node_modules / .tmp / .cache / dist output. Per-file FSWatcher allocation + the debounce+clearSkillCaches+executeConfigChangeHooks pipeline per event exhausted FDs rapidly during builds.
