Two-layer filter: chokidar `ignored:` excludes regular files not ending in `.md` (root-cause: no per-file FSWatcher allocated for build noise); `handleChange()` defensively short-circuits on non-`.md` paths to skip the debounce+cache-clear storm if a stats-less event slips through (e.g. unlink). Pre-existing .git exclusion + special-file guard preserved. Skills are only ever .md, so no real skill change gets missed. Parallel-agent dispatch.
- [x] `Fixed session title being generated from plugin monitor output instead of the user's first prompt` — DONE (`26fa041`) Already shipped — `extractTitleText` in `bridge/bridgeMessaging.ts` rejects content tags `task-notification`, `background-bash-notification`, `plugin-monitor`, `plugin-monitor-notification`, `monitor-notification`, `mcp-notification`, `mcp-monitor`, `system-notification` (filtered by `isNotificationContent`), and skips non-human origins. BACKLOG marker flipped during 2026-05-24 audit pass.
- [x] `Fixed Skill tool failing with permission error in headless mode (regression in v2.1.141)` — DONE (`e94f61f`) `SAFE_SKILL_PROPERTIES` in `tools/SkillTool/SkillTool.ts:928` was missing the metadata-only CommandBase fields `kind`, `availability`, and `isSensitive`. The regression hit workflow commands hardest: `tools/WorkflowTool/createWorkflowCommand.ts:79` sets `kind: 'workflow'` on every workflow command it produces, and that single non-allowlist property tripped `skillHasOnlySafeProperties` → `checkPermissions` returned `behavior: 'ask'` → headless (`-p` / `--print`) treats `ask` as deny because there's no UI. Added the three keys — they're purely informational (workflow-badge marker / claude-ai-console auth scoping / args-redaction flag) and don't grant new tool capability, so they belong in the same allowlist tier as `argumentHint`/`whenToUse`/`loadedFrom`. The deliberate gates `allowedTools` (extra tool perms) and `hooks` (registers hooks) stay OUT. 4/4 predicate smoke cases pass; real `-p` smoke clean.
- [x] `Fixed plugins enabled in your own settings showing "not cached" errors after first load on a fresh machine; plugins enabled only by a project's .claude/settings.json now show an actionable claude plugin install hint` — DONE (`2039f1e`) `getErrorGuidance` for `plugin-cache-miss` in `commands/plugin/PluginErrors.tsx` now mentions both paths: project-settings-enabled (`claude plugin install <plugin>`) + user-settings-on-fresh-machine (`/plugins` refresh). Upstream branched on plumbed settings-source tracking — we don't have that plumbing yet, so dual-message guidance with the plugin name baked into the install command (paste-ready) is the minimal-risk fix.
- [x] `Fixed claude mcp list silently reporting no servers when .mcp.json can't be parsed (e.g. using VS Code's "servers" key instead of "mcpServers") — now shows configuration errors` — DONE (`7685914`) `getMcpConfigsByScope('project')` already returned a `errors[]` array for malformed files, but the upstream destructuring at `getClaudeCodeMcpConfigs:1118` only kept `.servers` — dropped on the floor. Added an explicit scope sweep at the top of `mcpListHandler` (project/user/local) that prints any ValidationError messages to stderr with the offending scope + a recovery hint before the normal listing. Well-formed configs unaffected — sweep only emits when errors[] is non-empty. Live-verified: trailing-comma JSON → `"MCP config is not a valid JSON"`; VS Code `"servers"` key → schema error naming the missing `mcpServers` field; well-formed → no error block.
- [x] `Fixed background side-queries on custom ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL setups and Bedrock Mantle not using Haiku — now falls back correctly when a first-party API key is configured or no Haiku model is set` — DONE (already covered by 2.1.139 backport in `getSmallFastModel`) Our `src/utils/model/model.ts:42-69 getSmallFastModel()` already falls back to `getDefaultMainLoopModel()` when `getAPIProvider() !== 'firstParty'` AND no Haiku override (`ANTHROPIC_SMALL_FAST_MODEL` / `ANTHROPIC_DEFAULT_HAIKU_MODEL`) is configured. Third-party gateways (z.ai/DeepSeek/NIM) — our primary use case — work correctly. The upstream fix's surface for Bedrock Mantle + separately-configured first-party API keys doesn't apply to our fork (we don't have Bedrock infra and we don't have a separate first-party API key config for side queries — same `ANTHROPIC_AUTH_TOKEN` drives all paths). BACKLOG marker flipped during 2026-05-23 audit pass.
- [-] `Fixed scrolling in attached background sessions on Windows — PgUp/PgDn, mouse wheel, and Ctrl+O transcript navigation now work` — SKIP (Windows)
- [x] `Fixed a crash when closing the terminal while attached to a background session` — DONE (`79d394b`) `attachHandler` in `cli/bg.ts` installs a no-op SIGHUP handler scoped to the duration of `spawnSync('tmux attach-session', ...)`, restoring previous handlers via try/finally. Default Node behavior on SIGHUP is to terminate with status 129 + dump unhandled-signal stack — now cleanly exits with status 0. Also tolerates `EPIPE`/`EIO`/etc. spawnSync errors by logging "Detached from tmux session …" instead of letting them bubble.
- [-] `Fixed on Windows, pressing ← in claude agents leaving the list unresponsive to keyboard input` — SKIP (Windows)
- [-] `Fixed ghost characters at the left edge when switching panes in Agent View on Windows Terminal with CJK content` — SKIP (Windows Terminal CJK)
- [-] `/bg and ←-detach now preserve directories added via /add-dir` — SKIP (no retire/wake or respawn in our tmux-based `--bg`; flags persist for tmux-session lifetime — same family as the 7 2.1.143 retire/wake SKIPs)
- [-] `Fixed Edit/Write refusing with "background session hasn't isolated its changes yet" right after detaching a session that was already editing in place` — SKIP (we don't track per-session worktree isolation state in `--bg`; the bug surface doesn't exist for us)
- [-] `Fixed claude respawn <id> on a stopped background session showing "stopped" instead of running` — SKIP (no `claude respawn` subcommand in our fork)
- [x] `Fixed /resume picker not showing sessions forked from a background session` — DONE (`ee6fa45`) After the worktree+added-dirs scan in `loadSameRepoMessageLogsProgressive`, also enumerate sessions from the project dirs of currently-running bg sessions (via `listLiveSessions()`). Covers three real scenarios: (a) bg launched from a sibling cwd; (b) bg predates the current `git worktree list` layout; (c) user forked a bg session and the fork inherited the bg's cwd, landing the new transcript in the bg's project dir. Dedup by sessionId against the worktree scan; skip dirs already covered; getProjectDir() is memoized so repeat lookups are free. Errors swallowed — picker still works without the merge. Side effect: surfaces the LIVE bg session itself (with the L107 `(bg)` badge), matching upstream behavior — user can /resume into the bg's transcript even from a sibling cwd.
- [x] `Fixed opening a session from claude agents or running claude logs <id> hanging when the background service is unresponsive — now times out after 10s with a recovery hint` — 4be21da (10s `spawnSync` timeout on `tmux capture-pane` in `logsHandler`, on `tmux has-session` pre-flight in `attachHandler`, and on the `has-session` pre-flight added to `agentsViewHandler` before `attach-session` takes over the TTY; error message includes `tmux kill-server` recovery hint plus pointers to log + supervisor log)
- [-] `Fixed background Bash tasks spawned by subagents staying "Running" in SDK task panels after the process exits` — SKIP (Agent SDK task-panel surface)
- [-] `Fixed completed or stopped background sessions briefly failing to wake being permanently marked as a startup crash` — SKIP (no wake-from-idle cycle in our `--bg`)
- [x] `Fixed markdown links in claude agents attached sessions rendering as plain text instead of clickable hyperlinks` — DONE (`79d394b`) `PeekPane.tsx` pipes `row.lastMessage` through `applyMarkdown(message, themeName)` so `[label](https://example.com)` becomes an OSC 8 clickable hyperlink (in supports-hyperlinks terminals) instead of the raw markdown source. Empty / undefined messages still take the "No classifier message yet" branch.
- [x] `Fixed custom spinnerVerbs applying to the post-turn duration message — past-tense built-ins like "Worked for 5s" are restored there` — DONE (`2181860`) `getTurnCompletionVerbs()` in `constants/turnCompletionVerbs.ts` now ALWAYS returns `TURN_COMPLETION_VERBS` regardless of `spinnerVerbs`. The active spinner still honors customization via `getSpinnerVerbs()` — this function only governs the post-turn duration line. Users overwhelmingly customize live-spinner verbs (gerunds), not the past-tense duration line, so blending caused a verb-form mismatch.
- [x] `claude agents / --bg rejection messages now name the specific gate (non-TTY, env var, or setting) instead of a generic message` — DONE (`2181860`) `handleBgFlag` adds two pre-tmux gates with specific copy: `CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_BACKGROUND_TASKS=1` (env var named, unset suggested) and non-TTY stdin (`!process.stdin.isTTY` covers both `false` and the Node/Bun `undefined` for piped stdin — suggests Bash `run_in_background` or raw `tmux new-session -d` alternatives). Tmux gate now lists apt/brew/dnf install commands. Verified live in openclaude-interactive against both new branches. We don't ship a separate "background tasks setting" so that branch is N/A for our fork.
- [x] `claude --bg --name <label> now echoes the name in the post-spawn confirmation` — DONE (`746e781`) `handleBgFlag` parses `--name <value>` (and `--name=<value>`) out of filteredArgs before tmux dispatch. Label propagates through `CLAUDE_CODE_SESSION_NAME` which `concurrentSessions.ts` already writes into the PID-registry JSON as `SessionInfo.name`, so `claude attach <label>` works immediately. Confirmation line appends `(name: <label>)`; example commands switch to the label form. Verified live in openclaude-interactive: `claude --bg --name testlabel "echo hi"` printed `Background session started: claude-bg-e3f85fb1 (name: testlabel)` + `claude attach/logs/kill testlabel`.
- [-] `claude agents: renaming a background session with Ctrl+R now updates the attached session's banner immediately` — SKIP. Our agents-view dashboard doesn't ship a Ctrl+R rename shortcut. Adding rename would require: (a) a rename UI input layer in `AgentsPanel.tsx` (we already bind Ctrl+T for pin per L48; Ctrl+R is available); (b) `concurrentSessions.ts#updatePidFile({name})` to persist the new name to `~/.claude/sessions/<pid>.json`; (c) a banner subsystem in the running session's REPL that re-reads its PID file on a tick. (c) is the upstream-specific surface — our session doesn't ship a banner that reads from the PID file. Without (c), Ctrl+R rename would land changes the attached session never sees. Revisit once we add a banner subsystem.
- [-] `Background session worktree isolation guard now applies for non-git VCS users with WorktreeCreate hooks configured` — SKIP. Our `claude --bg` (`cli/bg.ts`) doesn't ship a worktree-isolation guard at all — it spawns the tmux session in the user's current cwd without checking for or creating a worktree. Adding the non-git-VCS branch upstream fixed would first require porting upstream's git-based isolation guard, then extending it through `hasWorktreeCreateHook()` (which we DO ship at `utils/hooks.ts`). Significant new infra; defer until the broader bg-isolation feature lands.
- [x] `Plugin marketplace add/update now respects CLAUDE_CODE_PLUGIN_PREFER_HTTPS` — DONE-BY-EXISTING-IMPL (2.1.141 backport `4d72a34` already wired `shouldPreferHttpsForPlugins()` into both `pluginLoader.ts` and `marketplaceManager.ts` paths — marketplace add/update goes through `marketplaceManager.ts` and inherits the HTTPS preference automatically)
- [x] `/plugin now returns to the Installed list after enabling, disabling, or uninstalling a plugin` — DONE (`2039f1e`) `handleSingleOperation` in `commands/plugin/ManagePlugins.tsx` sets local `viewState` back to `'plugin-list'` after a successful enable/disable/uninstall instead of exiting via `setParentViewState({type: 'menu'})`. Clears `pendingToggles` + `processError` so the list renders live state. Update path's already-up-to-date short-circuit still exits to the menu (no list to return to). `setIsProcessing(false)` hoisted before the `onManageComplete` await so the spinner clears immediately.
- [x] `/doctor now shows an exec-form example when a command hook is missing the command field` — DONE (`746e781`) New TIP_MATCHERS entry in `utils/settings/validationTips.ts` targets `ctx.path.endsWith('.command') && ctx.code === 'invalid_type'` under the hooks tree and fires BEFORE the existing generic hook matcher so users see both shell form (`{"type": "command", "command": "echo Done"}`) and exec form (`{"type": "command", "command": "${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/scripts/run.sh", "args": ["--flag", "value"]}`) explicitly when their hook is missing the command body. Generic matcher still fires for matcher / structure errors so the broader tip text is preserved on the broader path. Doctor surfaces tips automatically via the existing `ValidationErrorsList` + `formatZodError(suggestion + docLink)` chain.
- [-] `Skill-listing truncation is no longer shown as a startup notification — run /doctor for the full breakdown` — SKIP (N/A for our fork) Our `SkillTool/prompt.ts` only emits the `tengu_skill_descriptions_truncated` analytics event when descriptions get trimmed — there has never been a user-facing startup notification for it. Exhaustive grep across `src/` for `addNotification.*skill` / `setStickyFooter.*skill` / `truncated_count` notification surfaces returned only the two analytics call sites. Nothing to remove. Adding the /doctor breakdown is a forward-looking improvement but it's purely informational (no user is being interrupted today), so deferring until /doctor sections need a refresh anyway.
- [x] `Improved recovery from rare pre-response stream stalls — now retries streaming once instead of falling back to a slower non-streaming request` — DONE (`221f3a1`) `services/api/claude.ts` streaming path had two stall-detection triggers (watchdog timer fires before any chunks via `streamIdleAborted`, OR "Stream ended without receiving any events" throw). Both immediately fell back to a non-streaming `stream: false` POST — slower TTFT, no token-by-token feedback. Wrapped the streaming attempt block in `streamingAttemptLoop: for (;;) {...}` with a `streamingRetryAttempted` flag capped at 1. Pre-response stall gate: `!streamingRetryAttempted && !partialMessage && !signal.aborted && !(streamingError instanceof APIUserAbortError)` → `continue streamingAttemptLoop` for one retry of the streaming request. Second pre-response stall (or any mid-stream stall) falls through to existing non-streaming fallback. Mid-stream stalls bypass the gate to preserve inc-4258 protection against double tool execution. Reuses parent `signal` AbortController — Esc cancellation propagates. Telemetry: `tengu_streaming_pre_response_retry` event with retry_cause (watchdog | no_events). Live-verified end-to-end against z.ai/deepseek-v4-pro. Parallel-agent dispatch.
- [-] `Improved SDK/headless MCP startup: pre-wait now overlaps startup instead of blocking before the first turn (up to 2s faster with slow MCP servers)` — SKIP (Agent SDK headless entry path)
- [-] `The post-survey follow-up hint now appears after every non-dismiss survey response with context-aware copy, making it easier to share more detail via /feedback.` — SKIP (claude.ai survey + /feedback channel)

## 2.1.143

- [x] `Added plugin dependency enforcement: claude plugin disable now refuses when another enabled plugin depends on the target (with a copy-pasteable disable-chain hint), and claude plugin enable force-enables transitive dependencies` — DONE (`9cb53d9`) New `getTransitiveDependencies` helper in `dependencyResolver.ts`; `setPluginEnabledOp` gains `options.force` (passed through CLI `--force` flag). Disable refuses with disable-chain hint when dependents exist (unless --force). Enable cascade-enables all transitive deps in one settings write; success message reports the cascade.
- [x] `Added projected context cost (per-turn and per-invocation token estimates) to the /plugin marketplace browse pane` — DONE (`4fb0d0f`) Earlier DEFERRED rationale was the missing upstream source diff; resolved by extracting the exact `JP5` / `computePluginTokenCost` / `formatTokenEstimate` implementations from the 2.1.143 binary via `strings` analysis. Upstream hosts a public `plugin-details.json` catalog on GCS (`storage.googleapis.com/claude-code-dist-86c565f3-…/plugin-stats/plugin-details.json`, 173 plugins, 240K) carrying exact per-model `tokens.{model}.{always_on,on_invoke}` plus per-component `chars` for heuristic fallback at 3 chars/token. Ported as: `src/utils/plugins/pluginCostCatalog.ts` (catalog fetcher mirroring `installCounts.ts` patterns — 24h TTL cache, atomic temp+rename writes, axios timeout 10s, 5MB cap), `src/utils/format.ts:formatTokenEstimate` (rounds to nearest 10, `< 20` short-circuit, `~XX`/`~Xk` format), `src/components/plugin/PluginCostBadge.tsx` (React/Ink component matching stock JSX byte-for-byte — `Context cost[ (estimated)]:` + `· Every turn: <est> tokens` (warning color when `alwaysOn >= 2000`) + `· When invoked: <est> tokens` dim). Mounted in both `DiscoverPlugins.tsx` and `BrowseMarketplace.tsx` plugin-details views between metadata block and `<PluginTrustWarning />`. Live-verified against the GCS catalog from inside the container — context7 (MCP-only) renders `Every turn: < 20 tokens / When invoked: < 20 tokens`, 42crunch renders `~720 / ~12.1k` (exact, sonnet-4-6), amplitude renders `~2.3k / ~72.6k` with warning color, unknown plugins return null and the badge unmounts. Notice: `PluginCostBadgeNotice.tsx`.
- [-] `Added worktree.bgIsolation: "none" setting to let background sessions edit the working copy directly without EnterWorktree, for repos where worktrees are impractical` — SKIP-AS-DEFAULT (2026-05-17 re-eval: our `claude --bg` (`cli/bg.ts`) spawns subshells WITHOUT per-session EnterWorktree by design — `bgIsolation:none` is the de-facto behavior. The setting field exists in `utils/settings/types.ts` for forward compatibility. Adding `bgIsolation:'worktree'` support would be a separate feature; flipping from PARTIAL to SKIP because there's no asymmetric bypass branch to wire — the upstream "let background sessions edit the working copy directly" outcome IS our default and the inverse (worktree mode) doesn't exist for us yet.)
- [-] `PowerShell tool now passes -ExecutionPolicy Bypass. Opt out with CLAUDE_CODE_POWERSHELL_RESPECT_EXECUTION_POLICY=1` — SKIP (PowerShell tool is Windows-focused; we don't ship Windows-targeted execution paths)
- [-] `Background sessions now preserve the model and effort level you set after waking from idle` — SKIP (upstream's retire/wake lifecycle doesn't apply to our `--bg`. Our implementation in `cli/bg.ts` spawns long-lived tmux sessions — no idle-retire-then-respawn cycle. Model and effort flags persist within the tmux session naturally; nothing to preserve across a cycle that doesn't exist.)
- [x] `Shift+Tab in attached agent sessions now includes auto mode in the cycle` — DONE-BY-EXISTING-IMPL (our Shift+Tab cycle at `utils/permissions/getNextPermissionMode.ts:34-69` already includes auto mode when `canCycleToAuto()` is true — TRANSCRIPT_CLASSIFIER feature + `ctx.isAutoModeAvailable` + live `isAutoModeGateEnabled()`. Plan → bypassPermissions → auto, or bypassPermissions → auto, then auto → default. Attached agent sessions land in the same REPL via tmux attach, so they pick up the same handler — no separate cycle to unify. Upstream's bug was their attached-session-specific cycle missing auto mode; we always had unified handling.)
- [-] `Fixed a corrupt .credentials.json with a non-array scopes value hanging the CLI on startup or silently aborting OAuth token refresh` — SKIP (claude.ai OAuth credential file; our auth path is API-key via ANTHROPIC_AUTH_TOKEN, no rotating-scopes file)
- [-] `Fixed right-click paste in claude agents on Windows Terminal and WSL` — SKIP (Windows Terminal + claude agents view)
- [x] `Fixed stop hooks that block repeatedly looping forever — the turn now ends with a warning after 8 consecutive blocks (override via CLAUDE_CODE_STOP_HOOK_BLOCK_CAP)` — DONE (`query.ts`: added `stopHookBlockCount` field to `State`, increment on each stop-hook blocking iteration; once `nextBlockCount >= cap` (default 8, env `CLAUDE_CODE_STOP_HOOK_BLOCK_CAP` override), emit a clear API error message and `return { reason: 'stop_hook_prevented' }` instead of recursing. Non-stop-hook continuations don't carry the field so the counter resets on the next blocking sequence.)
- [-] `Fixed Esc/Ctrl+C not cancelling a pending /loop wakeup while Claude is idle between iterations` — SKIP (our `/loop` uses `ScheduleCronTool` rather than `ScheduleWakeup`. Cron-scheduled wakeups go through the cron daemon's regular lifecycle; Esc on an empty prompt already cancels pending sentinel-tagged cron-based loop wakeups (documented at `src/skills/bundled/loop.ts:120-121`). The upstream `ScheduleWakeup`-specific bug doesn't apply.)
- [-] `Fixed /goal evaluator firing while background shells or delegated subagents are still running` — SKIP (we don't ship /goal yet — see 2.1.139 L2)
- [x] `Fixed NO_COLOR/FORCE_COLOR in settings.json env stripping Claude Code's own UI colors — they now apply to subprocesses only` — DONE (`utils/managedEnv.ts` `filterSettingsEnv`: keys in `SUBPROCESS_ONLY_ENV_KEYS = {NO_COLOR, FORCE_COLOR}` are diverted to a separate `subprocessOnlySettingsEnv` stash instead of applied to `process.env`. `utils/subprocessEnv.ts` `subprocessEnv()` merges that stash into the returned subprocess env via `getSubprocessOnlySettingsEnv()`. User can now set `NO_COLOR` in settings.json to control `git` / `gh` / `python` color output without stripping our own Ink UI's colors.)
- [-] `Fixed agent view spawning repeated PowerShell processes on Windows when listing sessions` — SKIP (Windows + claude agents view)
- [-] `Fixed /bg without a prompt sending "continue" to the forked session — the fork now waits for input` — SKIP (our `cli/bg.ts handleBgFlag` doesn't auto-send a "continue" fallback when invoked without a prompt — tmux session opens at an empty prompt. The upstream daemon-mode auto-continue behavior doesn't apply.)
- [x] `Fixed --agent <name> not finding plugin-contributed agents without the plugin: prefix` — DONE (`cli/print.ts`: after the exact-match `agents.find(a => a.agentType === options.agent)` lookup, falls back to a suffix-match (`a.agentType.endsWith(':<name>')`) when the bare-name lookup misses. Plugin agents register as `plugin:<plugin-name>:<agent-name>` — users typing `--agent <agent-name>` now find them when the bare name uniquely identifies one plugin agent. Exact match wins; ambiguous suffix matches (multiple plugins exporting the same agent name) fall through.)
- [-] `Fixed deleting a session from agent view not removing its transcript file` — SKIP (we ship `/agents` via `commands/agents-view/AgentsPanel.tsx` but it has no "delete session" action — state machine covers list/peek/inline-reply/filter/help/exit only, no destructive action. The bug surface (delete leaves transcript dangling) doesn't exist for us. Adding delete would be a separate scope from this fix.)
- [-] `Fixed stale-fragment rendering when scrolling in attached background sessions on Windows Terminal` — SKIP (Windows Terminal)
- [-] `Fixed background agents false-positive worker-stall detection storm after host sleep or macOS App Nap` — SKIP (macOS App Nap)
- [x] `Fixed 5xx error messages pointing at status.claude.com instead of naming the configured gateway or cloud provider` — DONE (`services/api/errors.ts`: 5xx and 529 error message paths now build a gateway-aware status-suffix — firstParty stays on `https://status.anthropic.com`; non-firstParty surfaces the configured `ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL` hostname instead, falling back to a generic "check your gateway provider for an incident page" if the env var is unset. Third-party users no longer see misleading Anthropic status page references for z.ai/DeepSeek/NIM failures.)
- [-] `The PowerShell tool is now enabled by default on Windows for Bedrock, Vertex, and Foundry users. Opt out with CLAUDE_CODE_USE_POWERSHELL_TOOL=0.` — SKIP (PowerShell tool + Bedrock/Vertex/Foundry — we don't route through these)
- [-] `claude agents now accepts --add-dir, --settings, --mcp-config, and --plugin-dir and applies them to the dashboard and to background sessions dispatched from it` — SKIP (our `claude agents` (`main.tsx:4712`) accepts only `--cwd` because the dashboard is read-only — `AgentsPanel.tsx` has no session-dispatch UX (only attach/peek/inline-reply/filter). Sessions get dispatched via `claude --bg <prompt>` or `/bg`, where these flags already work at the top-level program option layer. The "applies to dispatched background sessions" surface doesn't exist for us until/unless we add dispatch into the dashboard — separate scope from this flag-pass-through fix.)
- [-] `claude agents accepts --permission-mode, --model, --effort, and --dangerously-skip-permissions to set defaults for sessions dispatched from the view` — SKIP (same as 2.1.142 L1 above — our dashboard has no dispatch UX; these are already top-level options that work for `claude --bg`.)
- [-] `claude --bg --dangerously-skip-permissions now persists across retire→wake` — SKIP (no retire→wake cycle in our tmux-based `--bg` — see prior entry. Flags persist within the tmux session naturally.)
- [-] `Fixed background sessions silently capturing IDE file references into the warm spare's input, which caused the reference to be prepended to the next prompt dispatched from claude agents` — SKIP (claude agents + warm-spare infra we don't ship)
- [-] `Worktree cleanup no longer falls back to rm -rf when git worktree remove fails, preventing loss of gitignored or in-progress files` — SKIP (already-compliant: `utils/worktree.ts cleanupWorktree` logs and continues when `git worktree remove --force` fails — no rm-rf fallback ever existed in our fork.)
- [-] `Fixed background-job sessions on macOS getting "Operation not permitted" errors when reading files under ~/Documents, ~/Desktop, or ~/Downloads, even with Full Disk Access granted.` — SKIP (macOS Full Disk Access)
- [-] `/bg now preserves --mcp-config, --settings, --add-dir, --plugin-dir, and --strict-mcp-config, so backgrounded sessions keep their MCP servers and settings across respawn.` — SKIP (no respawn in our tmux-based `--bg`. Parent-process CLI args are passed to the tmux session at spawn time and persist for its full lifetime; there's no respawn that would drop them.)
- [-] `Background sessions launched from claude agents now honor permissions.defaultMode from settings.json (was previously overridden to auto mode)` — SKIP (our dashboard doesn't dispatch sessions — see 2.1.142 L1 above. Sessions started via `claude --bg` already inherit `permissions.defaultMode` through the normal settings-cascade in `permissionSetup.ts`. The override-to-auto bug surface is in upstream's dashboard-side dispatch which we don't have.)
- [-] `Fixed: on Windows, pressing ← in claude agents while a response was streaming could leave the agents list unresponsive to all input` — SKIP (Windows + claude agents)
- [-] `/bg and ←-detach now preserve --fallback-model, so backgrounded workers degrade to the fallback model on overload instead of hard-failing.` — SKIP (no respawn in our tmux-based `--bg`; flags persist for the lifetime of the tmux session.)
- [-] `/bg and ←-detach now preserve --allow-dangerously-skip-permissions, so the forked worker keeps bypass-permissions available in its Shift+Tab cycle.` — SKIP (no respawn in our tmux-based `--bg`; flag persists for the lifetime of the tmux session.)
- [-] `Fixed: background daemon spawn now falls back to the running binary when the ~/.local/bin/claude launcher is missing or non-executable` — SKIP (our `cli/bg.ts` spawns tmux directly with the current `process.execPath` argv as the command — we don't depend on a `~/.local/bin/claude` launcher being present. The fallback pattern upstream needed doesn't apply.)
- [-] `Fixed claude agents --allow-dangerously-skip-permissions defaulting dispatched sessions to bypass mode instead of making it available in the permission cycle` — SKIP (our dashboard doesn't dispatch sessions — see 2.1.142 L1 above. The `--allow-dangerously-skip-permissions` flag at the program level (`main.tsx:1054`) already correctly gates bypass-mode-availability vs default-bypass through `permissionSetup.ts`'s `initialPermissionModeFromCLI`.)

## 2.1.142

- [-] `Added new claude agents flags: --add-dir, --settings, --mcp-config, --plugin-dir, --permission-mode, --model, --effort, and --dangerously-skip-permissions to configure dispatched background sessions` — SKIP (duplicate of 2.1.142 L1 immediately above — our dashboard doesn't dispatch sessions; these are top-level options on `program` that already work for `claude --bg`.)
- [x] `Fast mode now uses Opus 4.7 by default (previously Opus 4.6). Set CLAUDE_CODE_OPUS_4_6_FAST_MODE_OVERRIDE=1 to pin fast mode to Opus 4.6` — DONE (`utils/fastMode.ts`: `FAST_MODE_MODEL_DISPLAY` bumped to `'Opus 4.7'` to match what `getDefaultOpusModel()` already returns for firstParty (opus47) — the display label was stale. `getFastModeModel` honors `CLAUDE_CODE_OPUS_4_6_FAST_MODE_OVERRIDE=1` by pinning to `claude-opus-4-6`; otherwise stays on `'opus'` alias (resolves to opus47 firstParty / opus46 3P).)
- [x] `Plugins with a root-level SKILL.md and no skills/ subdirectory are now surfaced as a skill` — DONE (`7db72e0`) `pluginLoader.ts` step 4b — when `skills/` doesn't exist, probe for `<pluginPath>/SKILL.md` and point `plugin.skillsPath` at the plugin root. Existing `loadSkillsFromDirectory()` already handles the `skillsPath/SKILL.md` direct-skill case (no subdirectory scan).
- [x] `The /plugin details pane and claude plugin details now show LSP servers a plugin provides` — DONE `e972cdd`+`dd94eff` (`e972cdd` added LSP-server name collection in `commands/plugin/ManagePlugins.tsx` `PluginComponentsDisplay` across all three paths — built-in, inline `--plugin-dir`, and marketplace. Reuses the dedup-and-flatten pattern from the hook + MCP fix; handles the three shapes upstream permits on `pluginEntry.lspServers` — string path reference, record of inline configs, or array of both. Renders a new "• LSP Servers: a, b, c" line. `dd94eff` shipped the `claude plugin details <name>` CLI subcommand (2.1.139 L129) which inherits the same lspServers row from the shared `collectPluginComponents` helper extracted from PluginComponentsDisplay. Both surfaces now show LSP servers.)
- [-] `/web-setup warns before replacing an existing GitHub App connection` — SKIP (we don't ship /web-setup — claude.ai feature)
- [x] `Fixed MCP_TOOL_TIMEOUT not raising the per-request fetch timeout for remote HTTP and SSE MCP servers, which capped tool calls at 60 seconds regardless of the configured value` — DONE (`services/mcp/client.ts` `wrapFetchWithTimeout`: per-request fetch timeout was hardcoded to `MCP_REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS` (60s). Now takes `Math.max(MCP_REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS, getMcpToolTimeoutMs())` so users who set `MCP_TOOL_TIMEOUT` to a higher value actually see tool calls wait that long. Default-60s behavior preserved when no env var is set.)
- [x] `Fixed background sessions not recognizing pre-existing git worktrees, blocking Edit while EnterWorktree refused to create a duplicate` — DONE `451a757` (`tools/EnterWorktreeTool/EnterWorktreeTool.ts` — when `input.path` is omitted and cwd is already inside a worktree (findGitRoot != findCanonicalGitRoot), auto-fill path=cwdGitRoot and fall through to existing-worktree branch instead of failing on git worktree add duplicate)
- [-] `Fixed background sessions disappearing and daemon reconnect failing after macOS sleep/wake — the daemon now detects clock jumps instead of treating them as elapsed idle time` — SKIP (macOS sleep)
- [-] `Fixed daemon not exiting cleanly after the binary is upgraded (e.g. brew upgrade), causing dispatched agents to crash-loop on the deleted path` — SKIP (brew upgrade — we ship via GitHub Releases binary, not brew)
- [-] `Fixed background agents crash-looping when the Claude-in-Chrome extension is connected without a shared tab` — SKIP (Claude-in-Chrome extension is claude.ai)
- [-] `Fixed clicking links in an attached claude agents session — the background worker's headless browser shim no longer applies while attached` — SKIP (we don't ship Anthropic's headless browser shim for background workers — our `--bg` is a plain tmux session with no link-interception or browser launcher. The bug surface (shim swallows clicks) doesn't exist for us; clicking links in an attached tmux session falls through to whatever the user's terminal does (e.g. iTerm2 / Windows Terminal OSC-8 handling), which is the desired behavior.)
- [-] `Fixed claude agents "v to open in editor" using the daemon's default editor instead of your shell's $EDITOR/$VISUAL` — SKIP (our `/agents` (`AgentsPanel.tsx`) has no "v to open in editor" binding — useInput handler covers up/down/Enter/Space/r/Esc/?/`/`/`backspace` only. The bug surface doesn't exist for us. Adding the binding + editor launch is a separate scope.)
- [-] `Fixed claude agents deadlocking on Windows with network-drive working directories; Ctrl+C now works during startup` — SKIP (Windows network-drive + claude agents)
- [-] `Fixed background-color bleed when attaching to a claude agents session from Apple Terminal or other 256-color-only terminals` — SKIP (Apple Terminal + claude agents)
- [-] `Fixed claude --bg --dangerously-skip-permissions not persisting across retire/wake` — SKIP (same family as the 7 2.1.143 retire/wake items previously marked SKIP — upstream's retire/wake/respawn cycle for sleeping bg sessions doesn't apply to our tmux-based `--bg`. `cli/bg.ts handleBgFlag` spawns long-lived tmux sessions with `--dangerously-skip-permissions` already in argv; there's no respawn step that could drop the flag.)
- [x] `Fixed session titles being derived from the URL when the first message is a link` — DONE (`22e2554`) `localFallbackTitle` detects URL-only first messages and synthesizes a friendly title (e.g. `https://github.com/foo/bar/pull/123` → `"bar #123"`). Non-URL messages and Haiku-generated titles untouched.
- [-] `Fixed redundant set_model requests from remote clients injecting duplicate /model breadcrumbs into the transcript` — SKIP (Remote Control Sessions — we use claude ssh / acp-link / CCR instead)
- [-] `Fixed plugins using skills: ["./"] showing a false "path escapes plugin directory" error` — NOT-PRESENT-IN-OUR-FORK (verified: `resolveExistingPluginComponentPath` correctly accepts `"./"` and `"."` — they `resolve()` to plugin root and pass the `joined === pluginRoot || joined.startsWith(rootWithSep)` traversal check. `validatePluginPaths` uses plain `stat()` which also succeeds on trailing-slash paths on Linux. No "path escapes plugin directory" error message exists in our codebase. Combined with the 2.1.142 root-level SKILL.md fix (`7db72e0`), plugins using `skills: ["./"]` and a root SKILL.md now load cleanly.)
- [x] `Fixed plugin cache cleanup deleting the active plugin version directory when no installation metadata is present` — DONE (`227a3a0`) `cleanupOrphanedPluginVersionsInBackground` bails when `installedVersions.size === 0` (file empty/missing), preserving every version dir on disk and emitting a debug log with manual-cleanup guidance. Trades stale cache for non-deletion of active installs whose metadata was nuked.
- [x] `Fixed /plugin browse pane showing "0 installs" for newly published plugins` — DONE (`a02b45e`) `formatInstallCount(undefined) === "new"`; call sites suppress " installs" suffix for the new-plugin case. Plugins explicitly in the feed with `count: 0` still show "0 installs".
- [x] `Fixed plugin advisories not naming every plugin.json key that shadows a default folder` — DONE (`b89ced8`) Added `manifest-shadows-default-folder` PluginError emitted once per shadow check (commands/agents/outputStyles), so when multiple manifest keys shadow at once each gets its own advisory naming the specific key + path. Step 3 in pluginLoader probes every default folder unconditionally; the new advisory fires when `manifest.X !== undefined && defaultExists`.
- [x] `Improved reactive compaction: the first summarize attempt now seeds from the original request's overflow size, avoiding a wasted near-full-context retry` — DONE-BY-EXISTING-IMPL (the PTL recovery layer we already shipped — see CLAUDE.md "PTL / context-window-exceeded recovery" — does this. `services/compact/reactiveCompact.ts:107-120 computePivotIndex` uses `getPromptTooLongTokenGap(ptlResponse)` to size the peel on the FIRST attempt; `hasAttempted` guard at line 158 prevents re-entry, so there's no "first attempt without gap, retry with gap" anti-pattern. The upstream fix likely targeted that anti-pattern in stock; our implementation always seeded from the gap.)
- [x] `Improved hook configuration error: configuring a prompt- or agent-type hook for SessionStart/Setup/SubagentStart now shows a clear "use a command-type hook instead" error` — DONE (`b013b9d`) Runtime filter in `getMatchingHooks` extended — `http`/`prompt`/`agent` types on lifecycle events (SessionStart/Setup/SubagentStart) are filtered out with a warn-level message naming the hook type + the event + the fix. Replaces the previous silent skip.
- [-] `Removed stale /model claude-sonnet-4-20250514 suggestion from Usage Policy refusal messages` — SKIP (claude.ai Usage Policy refusal — not applicable to provider-agnostic z.ai/DeepSeek/NIM error paths)

## 2.1.141

- [x] `Added terminalSequence field to hook JSON output so hooks can emit desktop notifications, window titles, and bells without a controlling terminal` — DONE `29595b7` (`types/hooks.ts` + `utils/hooks.ts`: new optional `terminalSequence: string` top-level field on `syncHookResponseSchema`; `processHookJSONOutput` writes the raw escape bytes to `process.stdout` when present (matches the PushNotificationTool side-effect pattern). Skipped in non-TTY mode so piped output stays clean. Schema hint emitted on parse-failure lists the field next to `systemMessage` so authors discover it via normal error feedback. Hooks can now emit OSC 9 / OSC 99 / OSC 777 desktop notifications, OSC 0/2 window titles, or BEL without spawning a child process.)
- [x] `Added CLAUDE_CODE_PLUGIN_PREFER_HTTPS to clone GitHub plugin sources over HTTPS instead of SSH, for environments without a GitHub SSH key` — DONE `4d72a34` (`utils/plugins/marketplaceManager.ts` + `pluginLoader.ts` — new `shouldPreferHttpsForPlugins()` helper short-circuits SSH probe + fallback; applies to load, refresh, install, and git-subdir paths)
- [-] `Added ANTHROPIC_WORKSPACE_ID environment variable for workload identity federation — scopes the minted token to a specific workspace when the federation rule covers more than one` — SKIP (Anthropic workload identity federation; not our auth path)
- [x] `Added claude agents --cwd <path> to scope the session list to a directory` — DONE (`59cd56b`/2.1.141 follow-up) `main.tsx:4712` registers `--cwd <path>` option on the `agents` command; `cli/handlers/agentsView.tsx:22` threads it through as `cwdFilter` prop on AgentsPanel; `agents/sessionMerge.ts:94-107` does the prefix filter — `if (cwdFilter && !s.cwd.startsWith(cwdFilter)) continue`. Verified via `grep -n cwd src/cli/handlers/agentsView.tsx src/agents/sessionMerge.ts`.
- [-] `/feedback can now include recent sessions (last 24 hours or 7 days) for issues spanning more than the current session` — SKIP (/feedback is Anthropic feedback path; not applicable to our fork)
- [x] `Rewind menu: added "Summarize up to here" to compress earlier context while keeping recent turns intact` — DONE-BY-EXISTING-IMPL (`components/MessageSelector.tsx:126-127` has the `'summarize_up_to'` option labeled "Summarize up to here", with feedback prompt at `summarizeUpToFeedback` state and dispatch via `partialCompactConversation(direction: 'up_to')` at line 197. Already shipped via earlier rewind work.)
- [x] `Auto mode permission dialog now explains when a permissions.ask rule caused the prompt` — DONE `3e292e3` (`utils/permissions/permissions.ts` — added `result.decisionReason.type === 'rule' && ruleBehavior === 'ask'` short-circuit in the auto-mode block; mirrors the existing safetyCheck guard so explicit ask rules surface their reason in the dialog instead of being swallowed by the classifier)
- [x] `Restored the "view diff in your IDE" option on file-edit permission prompts when an IDE is connected` — DONE. New `view-diff-in-ide` `PermissionOption` type (`permissionOptions.tsx`); `getFilePermissionOptions` accepts `canShowDiffInIDE?` + `ideName?` and prepends a `View diff in <IDE>` option above Yes when an IDE diff feature is available, the file is editable (not `.ipynb`), and the user's `diffTool` setting isn't already `'auto'` (when it IS auto, `useDiffInIDE` auto-fires on mount and the option would be redundant). `useDiffInIDE` exposes a relaxed `canShowDiffInIDE` (IDE-availability + file-kind, drops the `diffTool === 'auto'` gate) and a `requestShowDiff` callback that flips an internal `manuallyRequested` state — when set, `showingDiffInIDE` becomes true and the dialog re-renders as `ShowInIDEPrompt` where the user accepts/rejects from inside the IDE. `FilePermissionDialog`'s Select onChange intercepts `view-diff-in-ide` selection and calls `requestShowDiff()` instead of routing through `PERMISSION_HANDLERS` (which would resolve the request prematurely). No-op handler registered in `PERMISSION_HANDLERS` to keep the Record exhaustive. Build clean, container hot-patch + smoke test passes.
- [-] `Background agents launched via /bg or ←← now preserve the current permission mode instead of reverting to default` — SKIP (same rationale as 2.1.143 `--bg` lifecycle items: our tmux-based `--bg` in `cli/bg.ts` has no respawn cycle. Permission mode persists for the tmux session's lifetime.)
- [-] `claude agents: agents that finish work but leave a background shell running now move to Completed instead of staying under Working` — SKIP-BY-DESIGN (our classifier at `jobs/classifier.ts:31-60 classifyState()` looks only at the last assistant message's text — keyword-based heuristics (`'all tasks complete'`, `'finished'`, `'successfully completed'`) → `'complete'`. It has no awareness of "background shell still running" because we don't track bg-shell state separately from session state. So our status transitions are already content-driven — finishing work moves to Completed regardless of any open bg shell. Upstream's bug was their classifier conflating "has bg shell" with "still working"; we don't have that coupling.)
- [x] `Improved spinner feedback during long thinking periods — the spinner now warms to amber after 10 seconds to signal Claude is still working` — DONE (`components/Spinner.tsx`: when `elapsedSnapshot >= 10_000` ms and no caller-provided `overrideColor` is set, `defaultColor` flips from `'claude'` to `'warning'` (amber) and `defaultShimmerColor` from `'claudeShimmer'` to `'warning'`. Caller-provided overrides (SuperMode, teammate-view explicit colors) continue to win.)
- [~] `Improved plugin menu navigation: →/Tab switch tabs, ↑ moves to the tab strip, and tab headers and search box are clickable in fullscreen mode` — PARTIAL (`d0ed4e2`) Added ↑ from content → header refocus (companion to existing ↓ defocus) in `Tabs.tsx`. ←/→ tab navigation already works when header is focused. Deferred: Tab-key alias for tab switching, and clickable tab headers + search box (needs onClick per tab Box + hover affordance — separate UX scope).
- [x] `Fixed background side-queries sending an unavailable Haiku model ID on Bedrock/Vertex/Foundry/gateway when no ANTHROPIC_SMALL_FAST_MODEL override is set — now falls back to the main-loop model` — DONE (`utils/model/model.ts` `getSmallFastModel`: when neither `ANTHROPIC_SMALL_FAST_MODEL` nor `ANTHROPIC_DEFAULT_HAIKU_MODEL` is set AND the gateway is non-firstParty (z.ai / DeepSeek / NIM / Bedrock / Vertex / Foundry), fall back to `getDefaultMainLoopModel()` instead of returning the hardcoded `haiku45`. Third-party gateways typically don't expose Anthropic Haiku and would 404 / "unavailable model" otherwise. First-party stays on the Haiku path.)
- [-] `Fixed claude daemon status and /doctor on Windows throwing when the daemon pipe key file is locked or unreadable — now shows the underlying error instead of an opaque failure` — SKIP (Windows daemon pipe)
- [-] `Fixed claude agents showing the agent-type list instead of the dashboard when launched through a wrapper that adds flags` — SKIP (`main.tsx:4712 program.command('agents')` registers the dashboard action with `.option('--cwd <path>')` only; Commander.js routes positional + unknown options to the action handler. The "agent-type list" surface upstream had was a fallback when their command parsing failed in wrapper-flag scenarios. Our dashboard always opens — the upstream fallback collision doesn't exist here.)
- [-] `Fixed claude agents opening a crashed session firing redundant dispatches when the working directory was deleted` — SKIP (our dashboard doesn't auto-dispatch on session-open — Enter on a row tmux-attaches to the existing session, doesn't spawn a fresh one. The "redundant dispatches on cwd deleted" surface is upstream-specific to their dashboard-managed worker pool, which we don't have.)
- [x] `Fixed background jobs on a custom ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL gateway not getting auto-named — the namer now uses the main model when no Haiku model is configured` — DONE (covered by the `getSmallFastModel` non-firstParty fallback in commit `58cf5f2` — the auto-namer routes through `getSmallFastModel()` which now falls back to `getDefaultMainLoopModel()` when no Haiku override is set on a non-firstParty gateway.)
- [x] `Fixed /model in one session silently changing the autocompact threshold in other concurrent sessions` — DONE (session-pinned snapshot of `userSettings.model` at session start in `src/utils/settings/sessionPinned.ts`. `main.tsx` initializes the snapshot from disk after the early model-resolution block. `getUserSpecifiedModelSetting()` reads the pinned snapshot in preference to the live cache so a sibling session's `/model` write can't leak into our autocompact-threshold calculation. `onChangeAppState`'s mainLoopModel writers (both the set-to-value and reset-to-null branches) call `markLocalModelSelection()` so this session's own `/model` updates the snapshot too. Disk write still happens so the pick persists across restarts. Same fix mechanism handles the L283 /effort leak. Approach (c) from the original DEFERRED rationale — trade-off "breaks legitimate hand-edit-while-running" is acceptable because: rare, and `/model` in-session updates the pin.)
- [x] `Fixed switching permission mode while a tool-permission prompt is open not auto-dismissing the prompt when the new setting permits the tool` — DONE `3f3b231` (`utils/settings/applySettingsChange.ts` — settings-change path now triggers the same recheck loop the mode-cycle path uses via `getLeaderToolUseConfirmQueue` bridge; covers `/permissions add` and direct settings.json edits)
- [x] `Fixed pressing Enter while a permission/dialog prompt is open also submitting text in the input box` — DONE `d09a83d` (`components/permissions/PermissionRequest.tsx` — added `useRegisterOverlay("permission-request")` so PromptInput's `!isModalOverlayActive` focus gate triggers while the dialog is open; covers every tool permission dialog that routes through PermissionRequest)
- [x] `Fixed hooks receiving a non-existent transcript_path after EnterWorktree switches the working directory` — DONE `cf2996f` (`bootstrap/state.ts` + `tools/EnterWorktreeTool/EnterWorktreeTool.ts` + `tools/ExitWorktreeTool/ExitWorktreeTool.ts` — new `pinSessionProjectDir()` sets STATE.sessionProjectDir without touching sessionId; EnterWorktree pins the pre-worktree project dir before setOriginalCwd, ExitWorktree clears it on restore)
- [x] `Fixed markdown tables with cell wrapping falling back to the vertical key-value layout instead of rendering as a bordered grid (regression in 2.1.136)` — DONE `612037c` (`components/MarkdownTable.tsx`: vertical-fallback check was `maxRowLines > MAX_ROW_LINES` — fired in wide terminals too when one cell happened to be long-form (200-char description proportionally allocated to a 20-char column wraps to 5 lines and trips the threshold). Now also requires `needsHardWrap` — set only when total minimum column widths exceed available width, meaning columns are sub-word-sized to fit at all. That's the actual regime where vertical reads better. Bordered grid stays for the typical wide-terminal-with-some-wrapping case.)
- [x] `Fixed cancelled prompts being removed from Up-arrow history when auto-restored into the input box, avoiding duplicate entries` — DONE (`098a560`) Both 2.1.141 entries are the same fix — auto-restore-on-interrupt path in `REPL.tsx` no longer calls `removeLastFromHistory()`. Trade-off resolved in favor of retrieval (the duplicate on next Up-arrow is dismissable; history loss was permanent).
- [x] `Fixed prompts cancelled with Ctrl+C/Esc before any response being dropped from Up-arrow history` — DONE (`098a560`) See above — same fix.
- [x] `Fixed Ctrl+C not interrupting a running turn while in vim INSERT/VISUAL mode` — DONE (`184fb9c`) Short-circuit Ctrl+C in `useVimInput.handleVimInput` — don't route it through `textInput.onInput`. Falls through so CancelRequestHandler's useInput hook fires.
- [x] `Fixed alternative chat:submit keybindings (e.g. meta+enter, ctrl+enter) not working when enter is rebound to chat:newline` — DONE (`3a5b50c`) `useTextInput.handleEnter` dropped `key.meta` from the hardcoded newline-modifier union; meta+enter now falls through to the keybinding system (or the default chat:submit). Shift+enter and backslash+enter retain inline-newline behavior.
- [-] `Fixed prompt suggestions being silently disabled when an output style was configured` — NOT-PRESENT-IN-OUR-FORK (verified: `services/PromptSuggestion/promptSuggestion.ts shouldEnablePromptSuggestion` + `getSuggestionSuppressReason` contain no `outputStyle` check. Our suppression conditions: env override, growthbook gate, non-interactive, swarm teammate, settings toggle, pending permission, elicitation active, plan mode, rate limit. The upstream offending check never landed in our fork — bug doesn't manifest.)
- [x] `Fixed spinnerVerbs setting not being honored in turn-completion messages` — DONE `1eda5b2` (new `getTurnCompletionVerbs()` mirrors `getSpinnerVerbs()` merge logic; wired into `SystemTextMessage._temp4` + `TeammateSpinnerLine`)
- [x] `Fixed AskUserQuestion popup hiding the last line of preceding chat content` — DONE `439d9ec` (`components/permissions/AskUserQuestionPermissionRequest/QuestionView.tsx` — t9 divider's `marginTop={-1}` pulled it up into preceding chat content; set to 0)
- [-] `Fixed Web Search status showing "Did 0 searches" when searches returned errors` — SKIP (we don't ship WebSearch — disabled in favor of zendriver MCP)
- [x] `Fixed multi-line statusline output dropping or corrupting rows when any line exceeds terminal width` — DONE (already-mitigated: our fuelgauge `components/FuelgaugeStatusLine.tsx` uses single-`<Text wrap="truncate">` per row (3 rows: folder/branch/model/ctx-bar / 5h-bar / 7d-bar). Each row is one Text node so Yoga measures the whole line and the truncate point follows the parent column's width. User-configured `statusLine` shell-out paths still hit the upstream hook-based renderer, which we don't separately ship.)
- [x] `Fixed light-ansi theme using invisible white for diff context lines on light backgrounds — now uses black` — DONE (`f799aa4`) Bug was in OUR TS port `src/native-ts/color-diff/index.ts` `buildTheme()`, not the native Rust module. The `if (isAnsi)` branch unconditionally set `foreground: ansiIdx(7)` (ANSI white) for ALL ansi-derived themes including light-ansi. Fixed with one-line ternary: `isDark ? ansiIdx(7) : ansiIdx(0)` — dark-ansi keeps white, light-ansi gets ANSI black (idx 0). DEFERRED rationale was wrong: we own the TS port (`src/native-ts/color-diff/`), there's no native theme-token edit needed.
- [-] `Fixed error overlay dumping minified bundle source that hid the original error message` — SKIP (already-compliant: our `components/SentryErrorBoundary.ts` renders only `error.message` + a "See stderr for full stack trace" hint. The componentStack goes to stderr (which Ink's `patchStderr` redirects to the debug log). No minified bundle source is rendered in the overlay.)
- [-] `Fixed pressing Enter after typing a feedback survey rating digit submitting it as a chat message instead of the rating` — SKIP (our `/feedback` (`components/Feedback.tsx`) has no rating-survey UI — only a description text field and error log collection. Upstream's feedback survey rating digit input doesn't exist in our fork.)
- [x] `Fixed pressing x on a selected subagent in the agent panel typing into the prompt instead of stopping the agent` — DONE `26a5b8e` (`hooks/useBackgroundTaskNavigation.ts` + `components/PromptInput/PromptInput.tsx`: 'x' in selecting-agent mode with `teammateCount === 0 && subagentCount > 0` calls `stopOrDismissAgent(taskId, setAppState)` (abort if running, dismiss if terminal). BaseTextInput drops `focus` when `viewSelectionMode === 'selecting-agent'` so the same keypress isn't ALSO inserted into the prompt buffer — useBackgroundTaskNavigation has its own useInput so nav keys still flow.)
- [x] `Fixed session title being derived from plugin monitor notifications before the user's first prompt` — DONE `26fa041` (`bridge/bridgeMessaging.ts:extractTitleText`: defense-in-depth content gate. The existing `origin.kind !== 'human'` filter handles affirmatively-tagged channel/coordinator messages, but notifications going through `enqueuePendingNotification` paths don't always set `origin`. After the origin/isMeta gates, content whose first non-whitespace character is `<` is matched against known notification opening tags (`task-notification`, `background-bash-notification`, `plugin-monitor`, `plugin-monitor-notification`, `monitor-notification`, `mcp-notification`, `mcp-monitor`, `system-notification`); a match rejects. Only top-of-content matches count so users pasting a literal `<task-notification>` mid-prompt still produce a title. 9/9 smoke cases pass.)
- [x] `Fixed "Allowed by PermissionRequest hook" repeating once per tool call under a collapsed read/search group` — DONE `1701e14` (`utils/collapseReadSearch.ts`: `flushGroup` now dedupes the `deferredSkippable` attachment list by `(decision, hookEvent, hookName)` fingerprint. Each tool call inside a collapsed group emits its own `hook_permission_decision` attachment to `deferredSkippable`; without the dedup the user sees N copies of "Allowed by PreToolUse hook" after the badge. With it, the same fingerprint renders once. Non-hook attachments pass through unchanged so other deferred messages — relevant_memories, etc. — still appear normally.)
- [-] `Fixed /tui silently dropping running background shells and subagents — now refuses and asks to wait for them to finish` — SKIP (our `/tui` (`commands/tui/tui.ts`) is a simple renderer-mode switcher — fullscreen ↔ default — with no detect-and-block logic. Upstream's pre-flight check against running background shells and subagents doesn't have a corresponding surface here.)
- [x] `Fixed welcome banner showing "API Usage Billing" on Bedrock, Vertex, Foundry, and other third-party providers — now shows the provider name` — DONE `c6677c5` (`utils/logoV2Utils.ts`: new `deriveBillingType()` returns `z.ai` / `DeepSeek` / `NVIDIA NIM` / `OpenRouter` / `AWS Bedrock` / `Google Vertex AI` / `Azure Foundry` via the existing provider helpers, falling back to `"API Usage Billing"` only for first-party Anthropic. Live-verified the four branches under synthetic `ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL` values.)
- [x] `Fixed /mcp server list not keeping the focused server visible in short terminals in fullscreen mode` — DONE `56b7cf9` (`components/mcp/MCPListPanel.tsx` + `commands/plugin/usePagination.ts`: MCPListPanel uses `usePagination` + `useTerminalSize` to window `selectableItems` against `terminalRows - 12`. Scroll offset auto-adjusts to keep `selectedIndex` in view — when the focused index would fall outside the visible window, `usePagination` recomputes `scrollOffset` to either `selectedIndex` (scrolling up) or `selectedIndex - maxVisible + 1` (scrolling down). Off-window items return null in `renderServerItem` / `renderAgentServerItem`, and scope-group headers skip entirely when their items are all clipped. Already shipped — flagged DONE retroactively.)
- [x] `Fixed redaction in /feedback bundles producing invalid JSON for quoted values like session IDs` — DONE `55153f0` (`components/Feedback.tsx:redactSensitiveInfo`: AWS / GCP / generic env-var regexes capture the opening AND closing quote into separate groups so the replacement emits them back. Previously the trailing `["']?` was consumed but dropped from the replacement → `"AWS_SECRET": "[REDACTED_AWS_VALUE]` with no closing quote. Also added `\b` to the generic `(API_KEY|TOKEN|SECRET|PASSWORD)` matcher so it doesn't double-fire inside `GOOGLE_TOKEN` after the GCP regex has already wrapped the value, producing a `[REDACTED]]` artifact. 4/4 smoke cases pass.)
- [x] `Fixed desktop and third-party provider sessions incorrectly inheriting apiKeyHelper/ANTHROPIC_AUTH_TOKEN from host managed-settings` — DONE `6f2eff0` (`services/api/client.ts`: `configureApiKeyHeaders` now gates the `getApiKeyFromApiKeyHelper` fallback on `getAPIProvider() === 'firstParty' && isFirstPartyAnthropicBaseUrl()`. `process.env.ANTHROPIC_AUTH_TOKEN` reads stay unconditional so z.ai/DeepSeek/NIM proxy Bearer auth keeps working — only the managed-settings fallback (which produces first-party Anthropic credentials) is gated. Bedrock/Vertex/Foundry sessions no longer pick up Anthropic creds from managed-settings.)
- [-] `Fixed early analytics events being silently dropped when fired before logger initialization` — SKIP (our `services/analytics/index.ts` + `firstPartyEventLogger.ts` + `sink.ts` are fully stubbed — `logEvent` / `logEventAsync` / `attachAnalyticsSink` / `initializeAnalyticsGates` / `initializeAnalyticsSink` are all no-ops with no buffering or init phase, so there are no events to drop early. `logForDebugging` (`utils/debug.ts:203-228`) lazily initializes its writer on first call and queues writes through that path, so it's also drop-safe. The bug doesn't apply to our fork.)
- [x] `Fixed claude plugin install failing for plugins whose marketplace ref no longer exists upstream when a sha is also pinned` — DONE `0a96bd1` (`utils/plugins/pluginLoader.ts:gitClone`: when `git clone --branch <ref>` fails AND a `sha` is pinned, retry the clone without `--branch` — the default branch always exists, and the existing SHA-fetch+checkout path immediately below lands on the pinned commit. Also adds post-checkout SHA validation: `git rev-parse HEAD` is compared against the pinned sha with `startsWith` tolerance for short-sha specs; a mismatch throws so a corrupted install fails loudly instead of silently shipping wrong code.)
- [x] `Fixed plugin details pane showing 0 MCP servers for plugins that declare them via .mcp.json` — DONE `1701e14` (`utils/plugins/pluginComponents.ts`: both inline and marketplace paths now call `loadPluginMcpServers(plugin)` instead of reading `plugin.mcpServers` directly. That helper merges manifest entries with `.mcp.json` (and `.mcpb`) — same merge the connection-side code uses, so a plugin shipping its servers exclusively via `.mcp.json` now contributes their names to the details-pane count.)
- [x] `Fixed plugin MCP servers with unset config variables showing a generic connection failure instead of a "config issue" message with a fix-it hint; malformed .mcp.json entries no longer drop other MCP servers` — PARTIAL `1701e14` (`services/mcp/config.ts`: the unset-env case is now surfaced as `"Config issue: missing environment variable(s) X"` with `suggestion: "Set X in your shell or settings.json env, then reload — server will be skipped until configured."` BEFORE any connection attempt; the broken config is skipped from `validatedServers` via a `continue`, so the user no longer sees a spurious "connection failed". The malformed-`.mcp.json` resilience side was already in place — `mcpPluginIntegration.ts` `loadMcpServersFromFile` validates each entry with `safeParse` and keeps the valid ones when others fail.)
- [x] `Fixed MCP server configs using POSIX shell parameter expansions (e.g. ${var%pattern}) being incorrectly flagged as missing environment variables` — DONE `c6677c5` (`services/mcp/envExpansion.ts`: `SIMPLE_VAR_NAME` and `VAR_WITH_DEFAULT` regexes split out the two supported shapes — `${VAR}` simple substitution and `${VAR:-default}` with default. Anything else (`${var%pattern}` / `${var##prefix}` / `${var/pat/rep}` / `${var:offset:length}` / `${var:+alt}` / `${var:?err}` / `${#var}`) is left unchanged AND not added to `missingVars`, so the downstream MCP-server shell handles the expansion at exec time. 14/14 smoke-test cases pass.)
- [x] `Fixed MCP HTTP/SSE servers returning 403 on connect showing as "failed" instead of "needs auth"` — DONE `c6677c5` (`services/mcp/client.ts`: SSE, HTTP, and claude.ai-proxy connect-error branches all now check `error instanceof UnauthorizedError || (error as {code?: number}).code === 401 || (error as {code?: number}).code === 403`. SDK throws `UnauthorizedError` for 401 and 403/`insufficient_scope`; bare 403s (server permission-denial) throw `StreamableHTTPError` with `.code === 403` which the existing checks didn't recognize. The duck-typed `.code` check covers both error classes without importing `StreamableHTTPError`.)
- [x] `Fixed remote MCP servers disconnecting unnecessarily when the optional server-events stream failed to reconnect — tool calls continue over POST` — DONE `6f2eff0` (`services/mcp/client.ts`: the SDK's "Maximum reconnection attempts" signal applies only to the OPTIONAL GET /events push channel — tool calls flow over POST and keep working. The branch now splits on transport type: legacy `sse` transport keeps the existing teardown (every message rides the single SSE stream there), `http` + `claudeai-proxy` log + propagate to the external onerror but skip `closeTransportAndRejectPending` so the connection stays alive.)
- [-] `Fixed Remote Control MCP connectors all failing with 401 when the worker session token rotated mid-session` — SKIP (targets Anthropic's hosted RCS cloud-worker infra. Our `/remote-control-server` (`services/remoteControl/`, `commands/remoteControlServer/`) is a LOCAL WebSocket bridge with no Anthropic-side worker session tokens, no MCP connector rotation, no cloud round-trip. The 401-on-rotation surface (claude.ai OAuth + `ConnectBridgeWorker`) doesn't exist on our side. `bridge/trustedDevice.ts` only fires when `tengu_sessions_elevated_auth_enforcement` is on AND `getClaudeAIOAuthTokens()` returns a token — both no-ops with `ANTHROPIC_AUTH_TOKEN` API-key auth.)
- [-] `Fixed Remote Control automatically re-enrolling a trusted device when the server rejects a stale token, instead of looping through /login` — SKIP (same RCS cloud-worker infra as above. Our local remote-control server has no device enrollment / `/login` flow; auth is bearer API-key.)
- [x] `Fixed a race where early OTel spans could be silently dropped in SDK/headless mode with beta tracing enabled` — DONE `26a5b8e` (`setup.ts`: hoisted the `initObserve` block to the top of `setup()`, BEFORE UDS bind / teammate snapshot / MCP probe / plugin scan, so any spans those phases fire land in the trace instead of being silently dropped by `getTracer() === null`. The CCB-b28de717 lazy-require gate (skip `@opentelemetry/*` requires when neither `CLAUDE_OBSERVE_URL` nor `OTEL_EXPORTER_OTLP_ENDPOINT` is set) is preserved.)
- [x] `Fixed custom voice:pushToTalk keybindings and "space": null unbinds being silently ignored` — DONE `c6677c5` (`keybindings/parser.ts` + `loadUserBindings.ts`). Parser now preserves `action: null` markers (previously filtered out by `typeof action !== 'string'`). `loadUserBindings` recognizes them as unbind directives: filters defaults on the matching chord via a new structural `chordsEqual` helper (key + ctrl/alt/shift/meta per keystroke), then strips the null entries from the merged list so the resolver still sees only `action: string`. `"space": null` now correctly removes the default `space → voice:pushToTalk` binding so Space falls back to inserting a literal space in the prompt.
- [ ] `Fixed Windows Alt+V image paste reporting "no image found" when the clipboard contains a screenshot` — DEFERRED (we DO ship Windows clipboard infra — `utils/imagePaste.ts:79-85` has win32 PowerShell `Get-Clipboard -Format Image` commands for checkImage/saveImage. Bug surface is specific: modern Windows screenshots (Snipping Tool / Win+Shift+S) write to private CF_PNG / CF_DIBV5 clipboard formats that `Get-Clipboard -Format Image`'s System.Drawing.Bitmap reader doesn't always recognize. Fix shape is plausible — add a fallback enumerating raw clipboard formats and reading image/png bytes directly — but multiple plausible PowerShell-incantation paths with different blast radii, and we have no Windows tester to verify any of them against a real Win+Shift+S clipboard. Our primary runtime is Docker on Linux so the bug doesn't manifest for the active userbase. Revisit when an upstream source diff lands or a Windows user reports the bug with a concrete repro.)
- [x] `Fixed SDK "Claude Code native binary not found" on Linux when both glibc and musl platform packages are installed` — DONE `55153f0` (`utils/envDynamic.ts`: the runtime musl probe now also stats canonical glibc paths — `/lib/<multiarch>/libc.so.6`, `/lib64/libc.so.6`, `/lib/libc.so.6`. If glibc exists, prefer it regardless of musl presence; treat the system as musl only when glibc is absent. Compile-time `IS_LIBC_MUSL`/`IS_LIBC_GLIBC` feature flags still short-circuit the runtime check for native builds. `nativeInstaller/installer.ts:104` reads `envDynamic.isMuslEnvironment()` to pick the linux-x64-{musl,glibc} binary suffix — dual-install systems no longer pick the wrong variant.)
- [-] `Bedrock: awsCredentialExport now always runs when configured instead of being skipped when ambient AWS credentials resolve, fixing auth for cross-account access` — SKIP (we don't ship Bedrock provider; openclaude routes through z.ai/DeepSeek/NIM/Anthropic only. `setup-bedrock` command exists for upstream-parity but our auth pipeline never hits AWS credential export.)
- [-] `[VSCode] Fixed in-chat mic showing no feedback when the microphone produced only silence — now shows "No audio detected"` — SKIP (VSCode extension UI we don't ship. Our IDE integrations are Emacs (claude-code-ide.el) and ACP (Zed/Cursor over stdio). The in-chat mic surface is the VSCode webview's React component, not our CLI core; even our `setupVscodeSdkMcp` is just the MCP bridge for the extension, not the mic UI.)
- [-] `[VSCode] Voice mode: the WSL error now suggests installing sox libsox-fmt-pulse for WSLg users` — SKIP (same — VSCode extension UI we don't ship. Our local voice path uses faster-whisper at `~/.claude/voice-stt/` and doesn't depend on `sox`.)
- [-] `claude agents: launching a session no longer fails when the pre-warmed background worker is unhealthy — now falls back to a fresh launch` — SKIP (our `/agents` (`commands/agents-view/`) doesn't use a pre-warmed background worker pool — each session is spawned fresh via tmux on dispatch. The unhealthy-worker fallback has no counterpart here.)
- [x] `claude agents no longer shows empty placeholder sessions left over from backgrounding a fresh REPL, and shows onboarding text when entered via ← with no other agents` — DONE `55153f0` (`agents/sessionMerge.ts:getMergedSessions`: skips empty placeholder rows — sessions with `status === 'running'`, `turnCount === 0`, and an empty `lastMessage` are the "backgrounded a fresh REPL before typing anything" case. They have nothing to attach to and create phantom rows in the dashboard. Only `running` status is filtered — completed/errored/awaiting_input sessions with zero turns are kept because the user explicitly opened them by some means. The existing `EmptyState` component already renders the onboarding text when all rows are filtered.)
- [-] `Empty idle background sessions left over from ← are now automatically retired by the daemon after 5 minutes` — SKIP (our daemon (`src/server/sessionManager.ts` / `server.ts`) is stubbed — no daemon-side session retirement logic. The cross-session `claude agents` dashboard filters empty placeholders at read time (L133, `55153f0`), which produces the same visible result without needing a daemon-side cleanup loop.)

## 2.1.140

- [x] `Improved Agent tool subagent_type matching to accept case- and separator-insensitive values (e.g. "Code Reviewer" resolves to code-reviewer)` — DONE (`tools/AgentTool/AgentTool.tsx`: after the existing exact-match `.find(agent.agentType === effectiveType)` lookup, falls back to a normalized comparison (`.toLowerCase().replace(/[\s_]+/g, '-')`). Same fallback applied to the deny-rule check below so error messages name the right agent. Exact match still wins so existing registrations are unaffected.)
- [-] `Updated agent color palette` — SKIP (cosmetic; without an upstream source diff naming the exact palette values, blindly editing `tools/AgentTool/agentColorManager.ts AGENT_COLORS` would risk diverging from upstream without benefit. Our palette already covers the standard 8 named-color slots; specific RGB swaps would be guesswork.)
- [x] `Fixed /goal silently hanging when disableAllHooks or allowManagedHooksOnly is set — now shows a clear message instead of an indicator that never resolves` — TODO (re-eval 2026-05-29: SKIP rationale was factually WRONG — /goal IS shipped in our fork: `src/commands/goal/goal-impl.ts` + `index.ts`, GoalNotice. Verify whether our goal-impl reproduces the hang when `disableAllHooks`/`allowManagedHooksOnly` is set and, if so, surface a clear message instead of a never-resolving indicator) — DONE (re-eval 2026-05-29: verified our architecture cannot hang. (a) disableAllHooks: checkGoalModeEnabled() blocks it (featureGate.ts:56-61) and /goal returns a clear gate message — no hang. (b) allowManagedHooksOnly: our goal continuation is a DIRECT call at stopHooks.ts:188-203, NOT a registered hook the policy could disable, so it keeps working. Deliberately NOT adding an allowManagedHooksOnly block — that would disable functionality that currently works)
- [x] `Fixed a regression in settings hot-reload where symlinked settings files caused misattributed change events and spurious ConfigChange hooks` — DONE (covered by the 2.1.139 L21 symlink-resolution fix in `8b70d3b` — `utils/settings/changeDetector.ts` `getSourceForPath` adds a `realpathSync`-comparison fallback so a change event on the realpath maps back to the right SettingSource instead of being misattributed. Same fix addresses both the 2.1.139 "not detecting" and 2.1.140 "misattribution" cases.)
- [-] `Fixed claude --bg failing with "connection dropped mid-request" when the background service was about to idle-exit` — SKIP (our `cli/bg.ts handleBgFlag` spawns tmux directly — no background-service idle-exit cycle. The race upstream describes doesn't apply.)
- [-] `Fixed background service startup failing on machines with enterprise endpoint security by allowing more time` — SKIP (no background service to start in our `cli/bg.ts` — tmux is the service, spawned directly via `spawnSync`. Enterprise endpoint security delays affect tmux startup, not us. The upstream daemon-startup timeout doesn't apply.)
- [-] `Fixed remote managed settings not retrying on 401 — now retries once with a force-refreshed token` — SKIP (Anthropic managed-settings remote-sync layer)
- [x] `Fixed managed extraKnownMarketplaces auto-update policy not being persisted to known_marketplaces.json` — DONE `136b5ad` (`utils/plugins/marketplaceManager.ts` + `reconciler.ts` — addMarketplaceSource accepts declaredAutoUpdate; reconciler passes declared[name].autoUpdate so managed/policy settings now propagate to materialized JSON)
- [-] `Fixed /loop scheduling redundant wakeups to poll for background tasks that already notify on completion` — SKIP (our `/loop` uses `ScheduleCronTool` rather than the upstream `ScheduleWakeup` mechanism the fix targeted. The cron-based path is one-shot via `recurring: false` for self-paced loops and doesn't have the same redundant-poll surface — the harness-notify-via-ScheduleWakeup pattern is documented in our system-tool prompt but isn't load-bearing for /loop itself.)
- [-] `Fixed a recurring event-loop stall on Windows when a missing executable (e.g. gh) triggered synchronous where.exe re-spawns on every check` — SKIP (Windows where.exe)
- [x] `Fixed Read tool calls failing validation when offset is passed as a whitespace-padded or +-prefixed string` — DONE (`utils/semanticNumber.ts`: preprocess now trims whitespace and accepts `+` prefix in addition to `-`. `" 30 "` → 30, `"+30"` → 30, `"\t-5"` → -5 (still rejected by the `nonnegative()` inner schema as expected). All other reject paths (empty string, `"foo"`) preserved.)
- [x] `Fixed native terminal cursor not staying at the input caret when the terminal loses focus` — DONE `0df46a0` (`components/BaseTextInput.tsx`: dropped `terminalFocus` from the `useDeclaredCursor` `active` gating. Previously a FOCUS_OUT event cleared the declaration and the cursor slid to `frame.cursor` (content bottom) — wrong anchor for any IME composition that happens while the terminal app isn't frontmost. Declaration now stays active as long as the input itself is focused + showing a cursor; OS-level IME panel renders inline at the caret as soon as the terminal regains focus.)
- [x] `Plugins now warn when a default component folder (e.g. commands/) is silently ignored because plugin.json sets the matching key. Shown in /doctor, claude plugin list, and /plugin.` — DONE (`b89ced8`) Same commit as 2.1.142's "name every shadowing key" follow-up — implemented both together. `manifest-shadows-default-folder` PluginError surfaces via the existing PluginError collector, which is rendered in /doctor, `claude plugin list`, and /plugin without additional wiring.

## 2.1.139

- [x] `Added agent view (Research Preview): a single list of every Claude Code session — running, blocked on you, or done. Run claude agents to get started. See https://code.claude.com/docs/en/agent-view` — DONE (`59cd56b`) Full MVP port: `claude agents` + `/agents` + `←`-on-empty-input all open the cross-session dashboard via local-jsx command shell (`src/commands/agents-view/`). AgentsPanel (`src/components/agents-view/`, 7 files / ~530 LOC) is a state machine with 6 modes (list/peek/inline-reply/filter/help/exit). Data layer at `src/agents/sessionMerge.ts` + `sessionWatcher.ts` (266 LOC) merges bg.ts SessionInfo with classifier state from `~/.claude/jobs/<id>/state.json`. Inline-reply uses `tmux send-keys` to feed the target session. Naming collision resolved: old `/agents` + `claude agents` (subagent-definitions menu) renamed to `subagents` with `agents-definitions` alias for back-compat. Companion `/bg` slash command added for daemonizing the current session. UI driver (`tools/ui-driver/`) used to capture stock 2.1.143 golden snapshots before implementing — `snap-container.ts` sibling drives the fork inside `docker exec -it openclaude-interactive` for tests that need container-side fixtures. PR-shipped status badge (`prUrl` field) stubbed for follow-up. Deferred to follow-up: `--cwd <path>` filter (2.1.141 L4), `--add-dir`/`--settings`/`--mcp-config`/`--plugin-dir` flags on `claude agents` (2.1.142 L1).
- [x] `Added /goal command: set a completion condition and Claude keeps working across turns until it's met. Works in interactive, -p, and Remote Control. Shows live elapsed/turns/tokens as an overlay panel` — DONE (`a5a528e`) — Option B (side-channel Haiku evaluator). Subcommands: `/goal <condition>`, `/goal status`, `/goal clear`, `/goal pause`, `/goal resume`. Bound clauses (`or stop after N turns/minutes`, `--budget 100k`). State at `~/.claude/goal/state.json`. Fuelgauge status-line row shows `goal <cond> │ turn N/M │ <last eval reason>`. `getActiveGoalSection()` injected into system prompt. Anti-runaway guard from watzon/opencode-goal. See CHANGELOG entry for full architecture.
- [x] `Added /scroll-speed command to tune mouse wheel scroll speed with a live preview` — DONE (`7736779`) Persists to `globalConfig.scrollSpeed`; `readScrollSpeedBase()` reads it before falling back to the legacy `CLAUDE_CODE_SCROLL_SPEED` env var. Args: `[N]` (set), `reset`/`default`/`unset` (clear), no args (show current). Skipped live preview — overhead not worth it for a one-off knob.
- [~] `Added claude plugin details <name> to show a plugin's component inventory and projected per-session token cost` — PARTIAL `dd94eff` Component-inventory CLI surface shipped — `claude plugin details <plugin>` mirrors the interactive `/plugin` details pane (id, version, scope, enabled, description, author, homepage, install path, then commands/agents/skills/hooks/MCP-servers/LSP-servers from `collectPluginComponents`). Accepts bare `name` or `name@marketplace`; bare-name ambiguity (same name across builtin/inline/multiple marketplaces) lists candidates. `--json` for structured output with non-zero exit on errors. Component-collection logic extracted from `commands/plugin/ManagePlugins.tsx` `PluginComponentsDisplay` into `utils/plugins/pluginComponents.ts` so both surfaces share one source of truth. **Partial**: projected per-session token cost not wired — that's a separate item (2.1.143 L12) and the same token-estimation pipeline used by `/context all` would feed both.
- [x] `Added transcript view navigation: ? for keyboard shortcuts, {/} to jump between user prompts, v to toggle shortcut panel` — DONE (`2a6cdd7`) `?` toggles a cheatsheet overlay in FullscreenLayout's bottom slot (parallel to the search bar); `{`/`}` jump to previous/next user-prompt message using `stickyPromptText !== null` for prompt detection. `transcript:exit` gated on `!transcriptHelpOpen` so Esc closes the overlay before bubbling up to exit transcript. `v` retained for "open in editor" (we ship that since 2.1.142); `?` is the sole opener for the shortcuts overlay.
- [x] `Added hook args: string[] field (exec form) that spawns the command directly without a shell, so path placeholders never need quoting` — DONE (`842e56b`) `schemas/hooks.ts` BashCommandHookSchema gains optional `args: z.array(z.string())`. `utils/hooks.ts` spawn block now has a third branch (exec-form, between PowerShell and legacy bash): uses `spawn(command, args, {shell: false})` so the shell-quoting layer is bypassed. Each arg gets `${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}` / `${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_DATA}` / `${user_config.X}` substitution like `command` does on the legacy path. CLAUDE_CODE_SHELL_PREFIX wrap + sandbox shell-wrap stay on the legacy branch (they're shell-specific concepts). Full back-compat — `args` is optional.
