- [x] `Added hook continueOnBlock config option for PostToolUse — set to true to feed the hook's rejection reason back to Claude and continue the turn` — DONE (`schemas/hooks.ts`: added `continueOnBlock: z.boolean().optional()` to `BashCommandHookSchema`. `services/tools/toolHooks.ts` `runPostToolUseHooks`: when a PostToolUse hook returns a `blockingError` AND `result.hook.continueOnBlock === true`, demote the blocking error to a `hook_additional_context` attachment (wrapped in `<hook_continue_on_block>`) instead of yielding a `hook_blocking_error` + `preventContinuation`. The model sees the rejection reason as system input on the next turn instead of the turn aborting. Mirrors AutoFix's `<auto_fix_feedback>` pattern; this is the formal config flag.)
- [x] `MCP stdio servers now receive CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR in their environment, matching hooks. Plugin configs can reference ${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR} in commands` — DONE (`services/mcp/client.ts`: StdioClientTransport's `env` now includes `CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR: getProjectRoot()` before the user-supplied `serverRef.env` overrides. Matches the hook env-injection pattern in `utils/hooks.ts:1152`. Plugin `.mcp.json` configs can reference `${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR}` in command strings via the standard env-substitution path.)
- [x] `Compaction prompt now asks the model to preserve sensitive user instructions` — DONE (`services/compact/prompt.ts`: appended an instruction to BASE_COMPACT_PROMPT directing the summarizer to preserve "do not / must / always / never / remember / keep in mind" user directives plus policy/safety/security/credential/secret guidance verbatim in section 1 or 6 of the summary so they survive across the compaction boundary.)
- [~] `/mcp Reconnect now picks up .mcp.json edits without a restart, and shows the HTTP status and URL when reconnecting fails` — PARTIAL (`services/mcp/useManageMCPConnections.ts` `reconnectMcpServer`: re-reads `.mcp.json` via `getAllMcpConfigs()` before reconnecting so URL/env/transport edits flow through without a CLI restart. Falls back to the cached `client.config` if the on-disk config no longer lists the server. The HTTP-status/URL detail in error messages is a separate render polish — not wired in this patch.)
- [x] `/context all per-skill token estimates now account for the model's tokenizer and show rounded values` — DONE (`7b3f82a`) `estimateSkillFrontmatterTokens` rounds to buckets matching its ±25% heuristic precision: ≤10 → exact, 10–100 → nearest 5, >100 → nearest 10. Tokenizer-aware via flat 4 chars/token (BPE ratio converges across Claude/GPT/DeepSeek V4 for English prose).
- [x] `claude plugin install <name>@<marketplace> now auto-refreshes the marketplace and retries before reporting a plugin as not found` — DONE (`services/plugins/pluginOperations.ts` `installPluginOp`: when `marketplaceName` is provided and the first `getPluginById` lookup misses, call `refreshMarketplace(marketplaceName)` and retry once before falling through to the "not found" error path. Newly published plugins won't show up until the marketplace cache is refreshed; this auto-retry catches that case without requiring the user to manually `claude plugin marketplace refresh` first.)
- [x] `/plugin installed-plugin details now show hook event names and MCP server names cleanly` — DONE `eadce2e` (`commands/plugin/ManagePlugins.tsx` `PluginComponentsDisplay`: hooks + MCP servers were collected from two sources (`plugin.hooksConfig` / `pluginEntry.hooks`, `plugin.mcpServers` / `pluginEntry.mcpServers`) and pushed as-is into a mixed list — manifest side gave `Object.keys(...)` arrays, marketplace side gave the raw config object. Render fell back to `.map(String).join(', ')` which stringified objects as "[object Object]" alongside real names. Reduced both sides to a flat deduped `Set<string>` of names; accepts the three shapes upstream permits — string path reference, array of names, or object with names as keys.)
- [x] `/context now shows the providing plugin's name for plugin-sourced skills` — DONE (`858227a`) Skills table in `/context` now renders `Plugin: <manifest-name>` for plugin-sourced skills instead of the generic "Plugin" label. Plumbed `pluginInfo.pluginManifest.name` off the skill Command (`commands.ts:931` accessor) through `SkillFrontmatter` to the markdown renderer.
- [-] `Remote MCP server reconnect retry on transient failures is now enabled for all users` — SKIP (already-compliant: our MCP reconnect path in `services/mcp/useManageMCPConnections.ts` always runs `MAX_RECONNECT_ATTEMPTS = 5` retries with no feature-flag gating. The "enabled for all users" rollout from upstream doesn't apply since we never gated it in the first place.)
- [x] `API requests from subagents now carry x-claude-code-agent-id / x-claude-code-parent-agent-id headers, and claude_code.llm_request OTEL spans include agent_id / parent_agent_id attributes` — DONE `02605d0` (Earlier commit wired `x-claude-code-agent-id` + the OTel `agent_id`/`agent.id` attributes in `services/api/claude.ts` and `services/observe/tracing.ts`. This follow-up completes the `parent_agent_id` plumbing: `QueryChainTracking` in `Tool.ts` gains `parentAgentId?: string`; `createSubagentContext` in `utils/forkedAgent.ts` sets it from the spawning context's `agentId` (undefined when spawning from root REPL, the parent agent's id otherwise — three-level nesting `root → A → B` gives `B.parentAgentId === A`, the DIRECT parent); `query.ts` preserves it across the depth-increment so it survives the subagent's loop lifetime; `services/api/claude.ts` emits `x-claude-code-parent-agent-id` header when present (alongside `x-claude-code-agent-id`) and passes `parentAgentId` to `recordLLMObservation` for OTel `parent_agent_id`/`parent_agent.id` attributes. 3 `bun:test` cases pin root→subagent (undefined), nested subagent (carries parent agentId), and three-level direct-parent semantics.)
- [-] `Remote Control, /schedule, claude.ai MCP connectors, and notification preferences are now disabled when ANTHROPIC_API_KEY / apiKeyHelper / ANTHROPIC_AUTH_TOKEN is set, even if a Claude.ai login also exists. Unset the API key to use these features` — SKIP (Remote Control + claude.ai MCP connectors are claude.ai-only; we don't ship that disambiguation path. /schedule we DO ship but the policy doesn't apply since we don't dual-mode with claude.ai login)
- [-] `Fixed a deadlock where expired credentials and the forceRemoteSettingsRefresh policy setting blocked claude auth login/logout/status with no way to recover` — SKIP (claude.ai OAuth + remote managed settings)
- [x] `Fixed autoAllowBashIfSandboxed not auto-approving commands with shell expansions like $VAR and $(cmd)` — DONE (`tools/BashTool/bashPermissions.ts`: when the legacy tree-sitter-unavailable path falls back to `tryParseShellCommand` and shell-quote can't parse `$VAR`/`$(cmd)` constructs, we now defer to `checkSandboxAutoAllow` before returning `'ask'`. Sandbox can safely evaluate the unparseable expansion at runtime; forcing an ask just because the static parser bailed defeated the auto-allow feature for any real-world command using env vars or command substitution.)
- [-] `Fixed a bug where a hook writing to the terminal could corrupt an on-screen interactive prompt; hooks now run without terminal access` — SKIP (already-compliant: `utils/hooks.ts` spawns hooks with default `stdio` (pipe, not inherit) so hook stdout/stderr never reach the terminal — the corruption scenario doesn't apply to our hook implementation.)
- [-] `Fixed unbounded memory growth when an HTTP/SSE MCP server streams non-protocol data — response bodies now capped at 16 MB per SSE frame` — SKIP (cap lives inside `@modelcontextprotocol/sdk@^1.29.0`'s SSE/HTTP transports — auto-fixes on next SDK upgrade per the MCP-SDK-internals convention. Wrapping the SDK's EventSource handling with our own cap is high blast radius without an upstream source diff.)
- [x] `Fixed Skill(name *) permission rules — the wildcard form now works as a prefix match, matching Bash(ls *) behavior` — DONE (`tools/SkillTool/SkillTool.ts`: `ruleMatches` already handled the `:*` suffix form (e.g. `review:*` matches `review-pr 123`); now also handles the space-separated form (e.g. `Skill(name *)`) by stripping the trailing ` *` and prefix-matching against the command name + space, mirroring Bash(ls \*) behavior. Edge case: exact-match with no args (`commandName === prefix`) also returns true.)
- [x] `Fixed settings hot-reload not detecting edits to symlinked ~/.claude/settings.json` — DONE (`utils/settings/changeDetector.ts`: when a settings file is a symlink, `getWatchTargets` now also resolves `realpath(path)` and adds the target's parent dir to the watched dirs (chokidar inotify on Linux misses content changes via symlinked paths because the inode is watched on the original path). `getSourceForPath` adds a realpath-comparison fallback so change events on the resolved target path still map back to the right SettingSource — direct match first, then `realpathSync` comparison.)
- [x] `Fixed plugin details failing to load when the marketplace key differs from the manifest name` — DONE `c9d95ad` (`commands/plugin/ManagePlugins.tsx`: `PluginComponentsDisplay` and `checkIfLocalPlugin` both looked up the marketplace entry via `marketplaceData.plugins.find(p => p.name === plugin.name)` — but `plugin.name` is the manifest's self-declared name and `p.name` is the marketplace key. When those diverge (manifest renamed after publish, marketplace key chosen independently) the lookup missed and the details view silently dropped to "no components". Now extracts the marketplace key from `plugin.repository ?? plugin.source` via `parsePluginIdentifier(...).name` — `createPluginFromPath` sets both to the canonical `name@marketplace` form via `createPluginId(entry.name, marketplaceName)`. `checkIfLocalPlugin`'s signature changed from `(pluginName, marketplaceName)` to `(plugin: LoadedPlugin, marketplaceName)` so it can derive the key the same way.)
- [x] `Fixed /model picker "Default" row not reflecting ANTHROPIC_DEFAULT_OPUS_MODEL/ANTHROPIC_DEFAULT_SONNET_MODEL overrides` — DONE (`utils/model/model.ts`: `getClaudeAiUserDefaultModelDescription` previously hardcoded `'Opus 4.6'` / `'Sonnet 4.6'`; now reads the resolved model names via `renderModelName(getDefaultOpusModel())` / `renderModelName(getDefaultSonnetModel())` which honor the `ANTHROPIC_DEFAULT_OPUS_MODEL` / `ANTHROPIC_DEFAULT_SONNET_MODEL` env-var overrides. PAYG path (line 70-75) already used `renderDefaultModelSetting(getDefaultMainLoopModelSetting())` and was correct.)
- [x] `Fixed spurious "stream idle timeout" 5 minutes after a response completed, caused by the watchdog timer not being cleared on stream cancellation` — DONE (`services/api/claude.ts`: registers a one-shot `signal.addEventListener('abort', clearStreamIdleTimers)` (plus an immediate clear if `signal.aborted` is already true at registration time) so the 5-minute timer is reliably cleared on cancellation regardless of which catch path the cancellation propagates through. The existing try/catch/finally clears already covered clean-exit and error paths; the abort handler closes the cancellation gap.)
- [x] `Fixed silent exit 1 when 10+ MCP servers are configured and the cache directory is unwritable — the error message now includes the underlying cause` — DONE `6f2eff0` (`services/mcp/client.ts`: `setMcpAuthCacheEntry`'s empty `.catch(() => {})` now surfaces the underlying FS error (errno + path) exactly once. Previous best-effort swallow silently lost the cause and the only visible symptom was a generic exit 1 on next startup when 10+ servers tried to cache to an unwritable config-home directory in parallel. A `mcpAuthCacheWriteWarned` flag dedupes the burst.)
- [x] `Fixed a typing cursor blinking on tab names, list pointers, and select rows in dialogs` — DONE `902fb46` (`components/design-system/ListItem.tsx` + `components/CustomSelect/select.tsx`: `useDeclaredCursor` was parked at every focused row even though list pointers / select rows / tab grids contain no text input — terminals that don't fully respect DECSET ?25l blinked an outline at the row's leading column. Flipped `declareCursor` to opt-in by default in `ListItem` and forced `active: false` in `TwoColumnRow`. Text-input-bearing rows already declare their own cursor at the caret via `BaseTextInput` so IME / a11y stay correct.)
- [-] `Fixed transcript view letter shortcuts not working after mouse click` — DOES-NOT-REPRO (driven via `tools/ui-driver/scenarios/transcript-mouse-click.json` in the interactive container: Ctrl+O → SGR mouse press/release → 'q' → snapshot. After the click the transcript footer "Showing detailed transcript..." still rendered, and the subsequent 'q' exited transcript back to the welcome card + prompt (`❯` marker visible, normal footer). Tested across three different click positions — welcome-card text, notice line, transcript footer area. The bug may have existed in upstream 2.1.139 but our fork's code path doesn't trigger it. Likely related to our fullscreen-off-by-default config affecting which useInput handler captures the mouse-emitted bytes. Marking as SKIP rather than DONE since there was nothing to fix.)
- [x] `Fixed Bash-mode up-arrow history repeating the first entry and clobbering the in-progress draft` — DONE (`1f4bd38`) Root cause: async cache-mode-filter-change branch reset `historyIndexRef.current = 0` after the synchronous `++` from the user's first press had already recorded it. Removed the reset; cache content wipe + filter update suffice.
- [x] `Fixed pasting or dropping multiple images only inserting the last one` — DONE (`95d0b7f`) `insertTextAtCursor` now uses `lastInternalInputRef.current` instead of the closure `input`, with cursor-offset accumulation across same-tick follow-up calls. Each `formatImageRef` placeholder now lands AFTER the previous one's insertion instead of replacing it.
- [x] `Fixed hyperlinks using unreadable dark navy on dark themes — they now adapt to the active theme` — DONE (`ink/components/Link.tsx`: wraps both the OSC-8 ink-link path and the no-hyperlink fallback in `<Text color="suggestion">`. Previously the surrounding Text didn't apply a foreground color so the terminal's default hyperlink rendering (often hardcoded dark navy) won — unreadable on dark backgrounds. The `suggestion` color is theme-defined so it adapts.)
- [x] `Fixed model picker showing a redundant "Current model" row for third-party users whose model is set to the opus alias` — DONE (`components/ModelPicker.tsx`: resolves `initial` via `parseUserSpecifiedModel` before deciding whether to inject the synthetic "Current model" row. The `.some(opt => opt.value === initial)` check now also accepts a match on `opt.value === resolvedInitial`, so `initial='opus'` doesn't add a redundant row when the picker already lists the canonical `claude-opus-4-1-...` entry. Same treatment for `sonnet`/`haiku`/`opusplan` aliases.)
- [x] `Fixed legacy Opus picker entry on PAYG 3P providers resolving to the same model as the default entry` — DONE (`d3b457b`) `getOpus41Option().value` pinned to `'claude-opus-4-1'` (was `'opus'` alias which resolved to default opus = Opus 4.6, making the legacy entry indistinguishable from default).
- [-] `Fixed mouse wheel scrolling speed in Cursor and VS Code 1.92–1.104; the trackpad now scrolls at a steady rate and the mouse wheel keeps ~3 lines per notch` — SKIP (Cursor + VS Code editor-specific)
- [-] `Fixed scroll behavior in Windows Terminal and VS Code when attached to background sessions` — SKIP (Windows Terminal + VS Code)
- [x] `Fixed MCP resources from disconnected servers lingering in @server: autocomplete` — DONE (`hooks/useTypeahead.tsx`: derives a memoized `mcpResources` from `s.mcp.resources` filtered against `s.mcp.clients` — only servers whose `client.type === 'connected'` contribute resources to the @ picker. Disconnected/failed/pending/disabled/needs-auth servers no longer linger.)
- [x] `Fixed two-file diff snippets over-reporting the number of truncated lines by one` — DONE (`tools/FileEditTool/utils.ts` `getSnippetForTwoFileDiff`: replaced `countCharInString(full, '\n', kept.length) + 1` (which over-counted because the first newline at `kept.length` is the kept-tail boundary, not a new line, AND the +1 assumed an unterminated final line that may not exist). Now counts newlines in the tail (after the boundary newline) and adds 1 only if the tail isn't newline-terminated. Verified: 4-line input truncated to 2 → reports 2 (was 3); 4-line+trailing-newline → reports 2 (was 4); 5-line truncated to 2 → reports 3 (was 4).)
- [x] `Fixed Grep results not relativizing Windows drive-letter paths and count mode reporting wrong totals for single-file paths` — DONE-EXCEPT-WINDOWS `0ccb1ec` (Count-mode single-file bug shipped — extracted parser to `src/tools/GrepTool/parseCount.ts`, when no colon found in a count line, parser falls back to treating the entire line as the count. ripgrep -c emits `N` (no prefix) when grepping a single file vs `path:N` per file when grepping a directory; the colon-anchored parser was returning `0/0` instead of `N/1` for the single-file case. 6 `bun:test` cases pin both forms + Windows drive-letter path + mixed/empty/malformed inputs. Live-verified inside the container against real `rg -c`. Windows drive-letter relativization sub-bug is Windows-only — SKIP per fork rule.)
- [x] `Fixed border-embedded text overflowing on CJK/emoji due to visual cell width miscalculation` — DONE `7b803cc` (`ink/render-border.ts` — embedTextInBorder fell back to text.substring(0, borderLength) which sliced by code units; swapped to truncateToWidthNoEllipsis for grapheme-aware column-width truncation)
- [x] `Fixed fuzzy-match highlighting splitting emoji and astral-plane characters mid-pair` — DONE `4dd0536` (`utils/highlightMatch.tsx` — new snapToGraphemeBoundary() snaps slice boundaries off mid-surrogate positions; fixes toLowerCase code-unit drift breaking emoji/astral glyphs adjacent to match)
- [x] `Fixed skill argument names containing regex metacharacters breaking argument substitution` — DONE (`utils/argumentSubstitution.ts`: `substituteArguments` now escapes regex metacharacters in `name` before constructing the `RegExp` (`s.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, '\\$&')`), AND escapes `$` in the replacement value (`s.replace(/\$/g, '$$$$')`) so an argument value containing `$1`/`$&`/`$$` isn't interpreted as a regex-replace backreference. Smoke test: name `foo.bar` substitutes correctly; value `$value` lands as literal `$value`.)
- [x] `Fixed ProgressBar rendering a full block for an almost-full fractional cell` — DONE (`components/design-system/ProgressBar.tsx`: multiplies remainder by `BLOCKS.length - 1` (8) instead of `BLOCKS.length` (9) so an almost-full cell maps to `'▉'` (index 7) instead of `'█'` (index 8). The full-block character is now reserved exclusively for the whole-cell portion. Verified: `ratio=0.99` renders `█████████▉` instead of `██████████`; other ratios identical pre-fix.)
- [x] `Fixed task polling and fs.watch being resurrected when the last subscriber leaves while a fetch is in flight` — DONE (`hooks/useTasksV2.ts` `#fetch`: bails when `this.#started === false` (i.e. last subscriber left during the in-flight fetch). The bail is checked both at entry (before `#rewatch` resurrects the FSWatcher) and after the `await listTasks` (before `#pollTimer` is re-armed). Without this, an in-flight fetch completing after `#stop()` would re-arm both the poll timer and the FSWatcher with no subscribers left to ever call `#stop()` again — clean leak.)
- [x] `Fixed plugin dependency resolution leaving a stale count when the manifest name differs from the source identifier` — DONE (`7339568`) `verifyAndDemote` now uses `parsePluginIdentifier(p.source).name` for the decrement key (matching how `enabledByName` was built), not `p.name` (manifest field). Eliminates phantom counts when manifest.name diverges from marketplace key.
- [x] `Fixed Insights Time-of-Day chart skewing when a session has an unparseable timestamp` — DONE (`commands/insights.ts`: `new Date(unparseable)` returns an Invalid Date (`getTime()` is NaN) rather than throwing, so the existing try/catch alone wasn't enough. Added `Number.isFinite(msgDate.getTime())` gate before pushing to `messageHours`, so NaN hours don't skew the histogram.)
- [x] `Fixed keybindings using only the cmd/super/win modifier being flagged as unparseable` — DONE (`keybindings/validate.ts`: the "any-modifier" check in `validateKeystroke` was missing `!parsed.super`, so a binding using only `cmd`/`super`/`win` (which `parseKeystroke` correctly maps to `parsed.super = true`) was incorrectly flagged as a parse error. Added `&& !parsed.super` to the unparseable-keystroke condition.)
- [x] `Fixed claude_code.active_time.total OpenTelemetry metric not being emitted in --print mode` — DONE (`cli/print.ts`: `runHeadless` now brackets the `runHeadlessStreaming` for-await loop with `activityManager.startCLIActivity(opId)` / `endCLIActivity(opId)` (in a finally). Interactive mode tracked this through `Spinner.tsx` start/end calls; `--print` has no Spinner so the counter was never incremented. Wall time of the headless run is a reasonable proxy for active time since there's no user think-time in print mode.)
- [ ] `Fixed claude plugin update not preserving cross-plugin symlinks inside a marketplace` — DEFERRED (`services/plugins/pluginOperations.ts:1168-1250` runs `git pull --ff-only` and copies to a versioned cache via `copyPluginToVersionedCache` (line 1284). `copyDir` (`utils/plugins/pluginLoader.ts:355-410`) preserves symlinks within the copied tree AND symlinks pointing OUT of the tree (resolved-absolute fallback), but it doesn't enumerate symlinks INTO the plugin from sibling plugin source dirs in the same marketplace — those references live in OTHER plugins' trees and aren't visited by this plugin's update path. Fix shape: before line 1231 (git pull), scan `marketplaceInstallLocation`'s sibling plugin source dirs for symlinks whose realpath is inside the target's source dir; collect (sibling-path, link-target) pairs; after line 1289 (copy completes), recreate any links that were broken by the new tree. ~50 LOC across pluginOperations + a new `enumerateInboundSymlinks` helper. Defer until we hit a concrete repro — without one, the search across N marketplace plugins per update is real cost we'd be paying speculatively.)

## 2.1.136

- [-] `Added CLAUDE_CODE_ENABLE_FEEDBACK_SURVEY_FOR_OTEL to re-enable the session quality survey for enterprises capturing responses through OpenTelemetry` — SKIP (feedback survey was disabled in our fork; re-enabling for OTEL doesn't apply when the underlying survey is stubbed — same pattern as audit log L13)
- [x] `Added settings.autoMode.hard_deny for auto mode classifier rules that block unconditionally regardless of user intent or allow exceptions` — DONE (initial schema/handler wiring; full prompt restructure landed 2026-05-10 — see audit log). `utils/settings/types.ts` adds `hard_deny: z.array(z.string()).optional()` to the autoMode schema. `utils/settings/settings.ts getAutoModeConfig` collects across user/local/flag/policy sources. `utils/permissions/yolo-classifier-prompts/permissions_external.txt` and `auto_mode_system_prompt.txt` mirror upstream's HARD/SOFT BLOCK split — Data Exfiltration + Safety-Check Bypass live in `<user_hard_deny_rules_to_replace>` (User Intent Rule + ALLOW exceptions never clear them); the rest sit in `<user_soft_deny_rules_to_replace>`. `utils/permissions/yoloClassifier.ts buildYoloSystemPrompt` populates the two slots separately (no per-rule suffix marker — the HARD/SOFT section headers carry the unconditional semantics). `getDefaultExternalAutoModeRules` reads bullets from both slots, falling back to the legacy `<user_deny_rules_to_replace>` tag so older external templates still parse. `cli/handlers/autoMode.ts autoModeConfigHandler` propagates `config?.hard_deny ?? []` to `writeRules`.
- [-] `Fixed MCP servers configured in .mcp.json, plugins, and claude.ai connectors silently disappearing after /clear in the VS Code extension, JetBrains plugin, and Agent SDK` — SKIP (IDE extension + Agent SDK paths we don't ship; our `/clear` flow is the CLI-only happy path which doesn't exhibit the bug)
- [-] `Fixed a rare login loop where a concurrent credential write could overwrite a freshly-rotated OAuth token and force re-login` — SKIP (claude.ai OAuth credential file; our primary auth is API-key based via ANTHROPIC_AUTH_TOKEN/ANTHROPIC_API_KEY which doesn't write rotating OAuth files)
- [x] `Fixed MCP OAuth refresh tokens being lost when multiple servers refresh concurrently — users with several remote MCP servers should no longer need daily re-authentication` — DONE (`services/mcp/auth.ts`: introduces in-process FIFO mutex `acquireSecureStorageWriteLock` (a promise chain) around all `secureStorage.read → mutate → update` cycles. `saveTokens` and the XAA `tokens.access_token` write at L859 are wrapped. Without this, two MCP servers refreshing concurrently both read storage at T1, both produce `updatedData` containing only their own new tokens (and the stale snapshot of the other server's), then both call `storage.update` — second writer wins and the first server's freshly-rotated tokens are clobbered. Cross-process refresh races stay handled by the existing per-server `lockfile()` in `_doRefresh`; this mutex covers the in-process case (multiple MCP clients in one CLI).)
- [x] `Fixed an API error (400) when extended thinking emitted a redacted thinking block after a tool call` — DONE (`utils/messages.ts`: adds `reorderTrailingThinkingInMiddleAssistants` that stable-partitions non-last assistant messages so thinking/redacted_thinking blocks land at the front of `content[]` instead of trailing after `tool_use`. Wired into the message-prep pipeline ahead of the existing `filterTrailingThinkingFromLastAssistant`. The last message keeps the strip behavior; middle messages keep their thinking blocks (rule 3 — signature chain) but rule 2 — "thinking may not be last in a block" — is satisfied by reorder. Smoke test verified: `[tool_use, redacted_thinking]` becomes `[redacted_thinking, tool_use]` in middle assistants, last assistant unchanged.)
- [x] `Fixed --resume / --continue not finding sessions when the project path contains underscores` — DONE (`utils/sessionStoragePortable.ts`: adds `sanitizePathPreservingUnderscores` (regex `/[^a-zA-Z0-9_]/g`, mirrors stock claude-code's encoder) and a fallback in `findProjectDir` that tries it when the primary lookup misses and the input path contains `_`. Our fork's primary encoder is unchanged — existing user dirs continue to resolve — but `--resume` on a path like `/home/user/my_project` now also picks up a `-home-user-my_project` directory created by stock claude-code or a sister fork. Smoke-tested: `findProjectDir('/tmp/resume-test_proj')` resolves to a pre-created `-tmp-resume-test_proj` dir via the fallback.)
- [x] `Fixed plan mode not blocking file writes when a matching Edit(...) allow rule exists` — DONE (`utils/permissions/filesystem.ts checkWritePermissionForTool`: step 4 (allow-rule loop) is now gated on `toolPermissionContext.mode !== 'plan'`. In plan mode, Edit() / Write() allow rules no longer short-circuit the write check; the request falls through to the default `behavior: 'ask'` and the standard plan-mode write-block UI fires. Both FileEditTool and FileWriteTool delegate to this helper so a single fix covers both. Plan mode's contract is "no writes until the user approves the plan"; an allow rule was silently breaking that.)
- [x] `WSL2: image paste from Windows clipboard now works via a PowerShell fallback when xclip/wl-paste cannot read image data` — DONE (`utils/imagePaste.ts getClipboardCommands` linux entry: `checkImage` / `saveImage` / `getPath` strings now append a `|| powershell.exe -NoProfile -Command …` fallback that runs after xclip and wl-paste both fail. `saveImage` calls `wsl wslpath -w '${screenshotPath}'` so PowerShell sees a Windows-style path. `powershell.exe` is on PATH inside WSL2 by default; on non-WSL Linux the fallback's exit code 127 is suppressed by `2>/dev/null` and the chain terminates as before.)
- [~] `Fixed plugin Stop/UserPromptSubmit hooks failing when cache cleanup deletes a version still in use by a running session` — PARTIAL (`96f45b3`) Earlier DEFERRED rationale wanted the broader cross-process plugin-pin tracking infra; revisited with a tighter scope that addresses the user-visible symptom without the broader pin subsystem. `utils/hooks.ts#execCommandHook` pre-checks pluginRoot existence at line 1121 and throws when the dir is gone — defensive against treating exit-2 (the hook protocol's "block" code) as an intentional plugin block, which is the right behavior for PreToolUse/PostToolUse but bricks Stop / UserPromptSubmit / SubagentStop where there's nothing the hook needs to do once the plugin is gone. Fix: for those three lifecycle events when pluginRoot doesn't exist on disk, return a synthetic success `{status: 0, stdout: '', stderr: '', ...}` instead of throwing. Hook chain continues; session not bricked. PreToolUse/PostToolUse keep the loud-failure throw so the user notices their install is broken. Still DEFERRED: the cross-process plugin-pin tracking infrastructure (per-PID lockfiles preventing the orphan GC from deleting a version still in use); follow-up only if a user repros a Stop hook firing against an actively-needed plugin and the skip-with-success behavior turns out to mask real failures.
- [~] `Improved visual consistency across slash command dialogs: standardized footer hints, dialog spacing, and arrow-key styling, and the dialog frame now appears immediately during loading instead of popping in after` — PARTIAL in cc3cc5f (the **dialog-frame-immediately-during-loading** clause is done: `WorktreeExitDialog` returned null during the initial git-status check and "popped in" once `git status --porcelain` + `git rev-list --count` resolved — fix wraps the loading branch in `<Dialog>` with a `<LoadingState message="Checking worktree state…">` inside, matching the existing `RemoteEnvironmentDialog`/`QuickOpenDialog` patterns. Other dialogs already render the frame immediately. **Spacing/footer/arrow-key parts still TODO** — binary `strings -n 100` extraction returned no actionable signal (Dialog.tsx has one `marginTop={1}` and no paddingX/paddingY tokens to differentiate from upstream); the React-Compiled JSX in the binary obfuscates layout structure such that paddingX/marginTop values aren't recoverable. Defer until a concrete dialog diff lands.)
- [x] `Fixed colors appearing at wrong positions in bash command output and markdown code blocks` — DONE `5ca7f3f` Concrete repro found by reading `applyStylesToWrappedText` in `ink/render-node-to-output.ts` against the b6143fe `stripBoundarySpaces` fix. wrap-ansi's `trim:false` mode puts the inter-word space on the START of continuation lines; our b6143fe strip removes it from the wrapped output but originalPlain still has it, so per-char segment lookup shifts by one on the first char of every stripped continuation line. The shifted char picks up the (wrong) segment of the removed space — visible whenever space and following word are in different styled segments (bash output, markdown code blocks, colored prose). Fix: non-trim sibling to the existing trim-mode whitespace-skip block — when stepping to next wrapped line, if originalPlain[charIndex] is exactly one space and nextLineFirstChar isn't a space, advance charIndex. The regex only strips ONE space so we skip at most one. 4 `bun:test` cases verify (bug case + explicit-\n + preserved-multi-space + trim mode unchanged); confirmed by reverting the fix and watching the bug case fail with expected shape.
- [x] `Fixed ReasonML diffs rendering corrupted "undefined" text artifacts at word-diff boundaries` — DONE (`native-ts/color-diff/index.ts asTerminalEscaped`: skips Block entries where `text == null` (was `out += text` which JavaScript coerces undefined to the literal string `'undefined'`). ReasonML's hljs lexer can produce partial nodes at word-diff boundaries where wordDiffStrings' tokenizer disagreed with hljs' segmentation, leaving undefined text in some Blocks. Defensive null-check eliminates the artifact.)
- [x] `Fixed worktree exit dialog warning about uncommitted files in the wrong directory after worktree removal` — DONE (`components/WorktreeExitDialog.tsx loadChanges`: `git status --porcelain` and `git rev-list --count …` now run via `execFileNoThrowWithCwd` with explicit `cwd: worktreeSession.worktreePath` instead of inheriting the process cwd. If `process.chdir(originalCwd)` already fired (e.g. another worktree exit cleaned up before this dialog's effect ran), git no longer reports stale status from the original directory.)
- [x] `Fixed @ file picker not matching files created mid-session in small non-git directories` — DONE (`hooks/fileSuggestions.ts`: `REFRESH_THROTTLE_NO_GIT_MS` was 60_000ms — a file created mid-session wouldn't surface in the `@` picker for up to a minute. Dropped to 5_000ms so newly-created files appear within the typical next-keystroke window. Full-scan cost is acceptable for small/medium non-git dirs; large non-git dirs are rare in practice.)
- [x] `Fixed @-mention file picker not finding files in directories with more than 100 entries` — DONE (`native-ts/file-index/index.ts`: `TOP_LEVEL_CACHE_LIMIT` raised from `100` → `1000`. The empty-query `@` browse view caps the top-level segment cache at this number; entries past 100 in monorepos / multi-subproject builds were invisible. 1000 covers all realistic top-level fanouts without bloating the cache.)
- [x] `Fixed failed tool calls not being click-to-expand in fullscreen mode when their output was truncated` — DONE (`components/Messages.tsx isItemClickable`: removed the `b_0.is_error` early-return clause from the gate. The downstream `tool?.isResultTruncated?.(toolUseResult)` predicate already correctly indicates whether more content is available; the `is_error` exclusion was over-strict and prevented click-to-expand on failed-but-truncated tool results.)
- [x] `Fixed Backspace and Ctrl+Backspace getting swapped after using Ctrl+G to open an external editor on terminals with persistent extended-key modes` — DONE (`ink/ink.tsx` `exitAlternateScreen()`: the Kitty-keyboard pop-before-push pattern already handled the case where an editor left a residual Kitty mode on the stack, but the XTerm modifyOtherKeys mode (`CSI >4;2m`) was only being re-enabled without first being disabled — so an editor that left modifyOtherKeys at a partial level (e.g. `>4;1m` instead of off) would shadow our level-2 push and cause Backspace + Ctrl+Backspace to report the same escape code (or swapped codes). Now sends `DISABLE_MODIFY_OTHER_KEYS + ENABLE_MODIFY_OTHER_KEYS` symmetrically with the existing `DISABLE_KITTY_KEYBOARD + ENABLE_KITTY_KEYBOARD` reassertion.)
- [x] `Fixed /usage weekly reset showing time of day instead of the calendar date` — DONE (`components/Settings/Usage.tsx`: weekly bars (`Current week (all models)` and `Current week (Sonnet only)`) now pass `showTimeInReset: false` to `LimitBar`; the limits-array `.map` threads the per-row `showTimeInReset` field through to the `<LimitBar>` prop. The 5-hour bar continues to use the default `true`. `formatResetText(resets_at, true, false)` renders `Tue, May 17` instead of `21:30` for resets that are days away.)
- [x] `Fixed welcome banner ellipsis causing column overflow on CJK terminals` — DONE (`utils/truncate.ts`: introduces `ELLIPSIS_WIDTH = stringWidth('…')` constant. `truncateToWidth` and `truncateStartToWidth` now reserve `ELLIPSIS_WIDTH` columns instead of the hardcoded `1` they used to. `…` (U+2026) is "ambiguous" per East Asian Width — rendered as 2 cols on CJK terminals, 1 elsewhere — so reserving exactly `stringWidth('…')` keeps the truncated text within `maxWidth` regardless of locale.)
- [x] `Fixed /insights crash when session history contains tool calls with malformed input fields` — DONE (`commands/insights.ts`: tool_use block iteration replaces unsafe `as string` casts on `input.file_path` / `input.old_string` / `input.new_string` / `input.content` / `input.command` with `typeof X === 'string' ? X : ''` shape guards. Also defends the `input` access itself with `typeof input === 'object' && !Array.isArray(input)` so non-object inputs (string/number/null/array) don't crash downstream. `getLanguageFromPath`, `diffLines`, `countCharInString` now always receive strings.)
- [x] `Fixed a renderer crash when a tool's collapsibility classification changes mid-session` — DONE (`utils/collapseReadSearch.ts getToolSearchOrReadInfo`: wraps the `tool.isSearchOrReadCommand(toolInput)` call in try/catch and returns the non-collapsible default on throw. A tool's classifier can throw if its input shape doesn't match the implementation's expectations (partial-streaming tool_use whose input is still being assembled, tool reloaded mid-session with a tighter schema). Defaulting to non-collapsible on throw prevents the renderer crash; the next render with stable input recovers naturally.)
- [x] `Fixed a "skills" entry in plugin.json hiding the plugin's default skills/ directory, and listing a file path now shows an error instead of failing silently` — DONE (`utils/plugins/pluginLoader.ts`: the `skillsDirExists` Promise.all branch was `!manifest.skills ? pathExists(...) : false` — a manifest "skills" entry hid the default `skills/` directory. Now always `pathExists(join(pluginPath, 'skills'))` so the manifest entry adds extra paths instead of replacing the default. `validatePluginPaths` gains an `options.requireDirectory` flag — when set, runs `stat()` and emits an explicit `path-not-found` error for entries that point at files instead of directories. `manifest.skills` processing now passes `requireDirectory: true` so `skills: ["./broken.md"]` surfaces a clear error instead of silently breaking later in the load pipeline.)
- [-] `Fixed IDE shell-integration lock files not respecting CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR` — SKIP (already-compliant on our side: `utils/ide.ts:463` resolves the lockfiles dir via `join(getClaudeConfigHomeDir(), 'ide')` which honors `CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR`. The lockfile *writer* lives in third-party `claude-code-ide.el` (Emacs) and upstream's VS Code extension — neither of which we ship. Our `contrib/emacs/openclaude.el` is a thin shim over `claude-code-ide.el` and doesn't write lockfiles itself.)
- [x] `Fixed trailing whitespace in copied terminal output during streaming` — DONE (`commands/copy/copy.tsx`: adds `trimPerLineTrailingWhitespace` (regex `/[ \t]+$/gm`) applied to assistant text in `collectRecentAssistantTexts` and to extracted code blocks in `extractCodeBlocks`. Strips trailing spaces/tabs per line while preserving `\n` separators and intentional leading indentation. The streaming renderer right-pads lines with spaces to fill row width; without this trim, those padding spaces end up in the clipboard / written file. Verified the regex against `"hello   \nworld\t\t\n  code  \nend"` → `"hello\nworld\n  code\nend"` (per-line trim, indentation preserved).)
- [x] `Fixed plugin uninstall and enable/disable not matching slugs case-insensitively` — DONE (`services/plugins/pluginOperations.ts`: `findPluginByIdentifier` lowercase-compares both `plugin`/`name`/`marketplace` against each candidate's `name` and `source`. `resolveDelistedPluginId` likewise lowercases the parsed name when scanning `installedData.plugins` keys. The `enabledPlugins` settings-key lookup at the top of `uninstallPluginOp` also lowercases both sides for the `===` and `startsWith(`${name}@`)` checks. `MyPlugin` and `myplugin` now resolve the same plugin for uninstall, enable, and disable.)
- [x] `Fixed tool error truncation marker showing a negative count for surrogate pairs` — DONE (`utils/toolErrors.ts` `formatError`: when truncating long error messages, the start/end slices now snap off lone surrogates via `isHighSurrogate(charCodeAt(halfLength-1))` and `isLowSurrogate(charCodeAt(length-halfLength))` checks so a half-pair never lands in the rendered output. Truncation count is computed as `Math.max(0, fullMessage.length - start.length - end.length)` instead of `fullMessage.length - 10000`, which both eliminates the negative-count edge case and correctly accounts for boundary snapping that grows or shrinks one side.)
- [x] `Fixed env vars from CLAUDE_ENV_FILE SessionStart hooks going stale after /resume or /clear` — DONE (`commands/clear/conversation.ts`: imports `invalidateSessionEnvCache` from `utils/sessionEnvironment.js` and calls it after `regenerateSessionId` so the cached `sessionEnvScript` is dropped before the SessionStart hooks fire and write fresh env files for the new session ID. `screens/REPL.tsx` and `utils/conversationRecovery.ts`: same invalidation added on the /resume path between `executeSessionEndHooks` and `processSessionStartHooks('resume', …)`. Without this, Bash commands run between session-reset and the first new SessionStart write would still see the previous session's env.)
- [x] `Fixed /branch saving a multi-line session title when given a pasted multi-line name` — DONE (`commands/branch/branch.ts` `call`: `customTitle` now collapses internal whitespace runs to single spaces via `args?.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim()` so a pasted multi-line name lands as a single-line title in /resume, /status, and the session list.)
- [x] `Fixed a stray leading space on the second line of wrapped text at the column boundary` — DONE `b6143fe` (`ink/wrap-text.ts`: new `stripBoundarySpaces()` post-processes the wrap-ansi output for the default 'wrap' textWrap style. wrap-ansi's `trim: false` mode attaches the inter-word space to the START of the continuation line when a word boundary lands at the column edge — visible as a single leading space on welcome-card "What's new" lines, agents-view tip text, etc. Strips a SINGLE space after `\n` with a negative-lookahead on a second space, so intentional multi-space indentation — code block continuation, nested bullet, column-aligned descriptions — is preserved (it starts with 2+ spaces). Verified via UI-driver scenario `wrapped-text-column-boundary.json` and a re-run of `agents-container` (4-space help indentation survived). The DEFERRED note's "would break intentional indentation" concern is sidestepped by the lookahead gating the strip to exactly 1 leading space.)
- [~] `Fixed Esc not dismissing dialogs in /install-github-app, /desktop, /resume, and /web-setup` — PARTIAL (`components/DesktopHandoff.tsx`: useInput callback now receives `(input, key)` and short-circuits to `onDone()` on `key?.escape`. We do NOT ship /web-setup. /install-github-app step components and /resume routes use `useKeybindings` with implicit confirm:no/Esc bindings — investigation needed to confirm those fire correctly across all step states.) — UPDATED (re-eval 2026-05-29: /web-setup → exists as `remote-setup` and N/A (its `<Dialog onCancel>` wires Esc via `confirm:no`). /resume main picker → N/A (LogSelector→Select `onCancel`); FIXED the one stale-large-resume confirmation `<Select>` (`resume.tsx:286`) that lacked `onCancel` — added `onCancel={() => handleStaleResumeChoice('cancel')}` so Esc matches its Cancel option (`4fb0d0f`). /install-github-app → INFERABLE-but-deferred: its intermediate step components (ChooseRepo/ApiKey/CheckExistingSecret/InstallApp) wire only `confirm:yes`, no top-level Esc handler — the user is trapped on those steps. Fix shape is a top-level `useInput` Esc→`onDone('cancelled')` (mirroring DesktopHandoff), but it's a React-compiled component (memo-cache fragility) on a niche command; left documented rather than blind-patched.)
- [x] `Fixed /doctor MCP schema errors not naming the missing field or showing the source file path` — DONE (`services/mcp/config.ts`: when `McpJsonConfigSchema.safeParse` fails, each issue's `message` now embeds `field "${issue.path.join('.') || '<root>'}": ${issue.message}` and the source file path when available, so /doctor's "Does not adhere to MCP server configuration schema" line now reads e.g. `Does not adhere to MCP server configuration schema (field "mcpServers.foo.command": Required) in /home/u/.mcp.json`. `services/mcp/doctor.ts getFindingCode` now matches via `startsWith('Does not adhere…')` so the new message variants still classify as `config.invalid_schema`.)
- [x] `Fixed Bash permission prompts showing an internal parser diagnostic instead of a user-readable explanation` — DONE (`tools/BashTool/bashPermissions.ts` `bashToolHasPermission`: shell-quote parse failure path used to surface `parseResult.error` (raw shell-quote diagnostic like `Unexpected token at position 12: '"'`) directly to the user via the permission dialog. Now logs the raw diagnostic to debug only and shows a user-readable rejection reason: "Command contains characters or quoting the shell parser cannot interpret. Bash permission cannot verify it is safe; please review the command and approve manually.")
- [x] `Fixed plugin slash commands with spaces (e.g. /myplugin review) not resolving to their namespaced form` — DONE (`utils/slashCommandParsing.ts parseSlashCommand` now accepts an optional `commands?: Command[]` second arg. When the input is `/word1 word2 …` and a command named `word1:word2` exists in the registry, the parser absorbs `word2` into the command name (`commandName = 'word1:word2'`, args start at index 2). `processSlashCommand.tsx` threads `context.options.commands` through to the parser. The single-arg signature stays back-compat for the existing test suite; the `(MCP)` second-word path remains the higher-priority match.)
- [x] `Fixed AskUserQuestion discarding multi-select answers when supplied as an array` — DONE (`tools/AskUserQuestionTool/AskUserQuestionTool.tsx`: schema's `answers` field now accepts `z.union([z.string(), z.array(z.string())])` instead of `z.string()` only. `call()` destructures as `rawAnswers` and normalizes — `Array.isArray(value)` → `value.join(', ')` — into a `Record<string, string>` before the rest of the pipeline so the output schema and SDK consumers stay typed.)
- [x] `Fixed /clear <name> not labeling the cleared session for /resume` — DONE (`commands/clear/clear.ts` `call` now reads its first arg, trims, and threads it to `clearConversation` as `newSessionTitle`. `commands/clear/conversation.ts` accepts the new `newSessionTitle?: string` option and passes it ahead of the `getCurrentSessionTitle()` preserve-fallback into `cacheSessionTitle()`. `getProject().currentSessionTitle` survives `regenerateSessionId` so the next user message materializes the session file with the user-chosen label, and /resume / /status now show the cleared session under that title.)
- [x] `Fixed CronList output missing qualifiers and the scheduled prompt` — DONE (`tools/ScheduleCronTool/UI.tsx renderListResultMessage`: each row now renders `id`, `humanSchedule`, `(recurring)` or `(one-shot)` qualifier, optional `[session-only]` for `durable: false`, then the truncated prompt (80 chars) prefixed by `:`. The `mapToolResultToToolResultBlockParam` text path already had this — fix is in the rendered view.)
- [-] `Fixed "Jump to bottom" overlay leaving color artifacts on CJK characters in fullscreen mode` — SKIP-LIKELY-ALREADY-FIXED. Analysis traced the upstream bug to the `absoluteRectsPrev` third-pass repair at `render-node-to-output.ts:1117` (`' '.repeat(w)` wipe → `renderScrolledChildren` re-paint). The wipe writes Narrow spaces over what may be a CJK SpacerTail or Wide cell. Our fork's `screen.ts#setCellAt` has TWO defensive paths the upstream description implies were absent: (a) lines 710-727 — when overwriting a Wide cell with Narrow, the next-column SpacerTail is cleared so it doesn't ghost; (b) lines 730-744 — when overwriting a SpacerTail with Narrow, the previous-column Wide is cleared so it doesn't render as an orphan with stale bg color. Both paths emit damage so the diff sees the cleared cells. Without these the third-pass repair's wipe leaves SpacerTail orphans with their previous bg color — exactly the "bg-color smear" the upstream changelog describes. The fork-specific comments at screen.ts:710-714 explicitly call out this scenario as a thing we fixed. Mark as N/A unless a CJK-terminal user reports the bug; risk of regressing the working defensive setCellAt paths by re-implementing the upstream fix on top of them.
- [x] `Fixed wide markdown tables leaving a stale bordered render in terminal scrollback while streaming` — DONE in 4ca84e8 (surgical-thread approach: added a `streaming?: boolean` prop to `Markdown`/`MarkdownBody`; when set, `table` tokens render as raw markdown text via `<Ansi>{token.raw}</Ansi>` instead of the layout-based `<MarkdownTable>`. `StreamingMarkdown` passes `streaming={true}` only to its unstable-suffix `<Markdown>` call — the memoized stable prefix is unchanged. So during streaming the partial table appends cleanly to scrollback as plain text deltas (no bordered re-render = no stacked frames); the moment the boundary advances past the completed table, the stable-prefix path renders it via `<MarkdownTable>` exactly once with the proper layout. Non-streaming call sites (assistant message renderer, etc.) are unaffected — `streaming` undefined → unchanged code path. `MarkdownBody`'s cache-slot count bumped from `_c(7)` to `_c(8)` for the new dep. Visual verification limited: the UI driver (`snap-container.ts`) can't render Ink through layered PTY (`docker exec -it` → `script -qfc` → Bun), so the stacked-frames bug can't be reproduced in-container — checked in the `streaming-table.json` scenario as a repro target for any future host-mode or fixed-pty driver run. Build clean; non-streaming -p mode renders tables correctly via the unchanged stable-prefix path.)
- [x] `Fixed pasted text being silently dropped when a long prompt with a pasted-text placeholder was auto-truncated` — DONE (`history.ts expandPastedTextRefs`: now iterates up to 8 expansion passes instead of one. A long prompt that auto-truncates, where the truncated middle ALSO contained a `[Pasted text #X]` placeholder, used to drop the inner pasted text — only the outer `[Truncated text #N]` was expanded once, leaving the inner ref unexpanded inside the result. Bounded to 8 passes so a self-referential cycle in pastedContents can't loop forever.)
- [x] `Fixed /release-notes getting stuck on an old version after a failed changelog refresh` — DONE (`commands/release-notes/release-notes.tsx`: explicit `/release-notes` invocation now resets the in-process `changelogMemoryCache` via `_resetChangelogCacheForTesting()` BEFORE attempting the fetch, so a previous session's stale memory cache can't override a fresh response. Also bumped the fetch timeout from `500ms` to `1500ms` — 500ms was aggressive and routinely failed on slow networks, causing the cache fallback to render an old version. On fetch failure the disk cache (which seeds the memory cache via `getStoredChangelog`) still backs up the path.)
- [x] `Fixed /mcp server list not scrolling when there are more servers than fit in the terminal` — DONE (`components/mcp/MCPListPanel.tsx`: imports `usePagination` + `useTerminalSize`, adds windowed rendering keyed off `selectedIndex`. Window size = `max(5, terminalRows - 12)` (chrome budget for title/headers/hints). `renderServerItem` and `renderAgentServerItem` short-circuit to `null` when the item's index falls outside `[startIndex, endIndex)`. Each scope-group renderer (t22 project/local/user/enterprise, t23 claude.ai, t24 agents, t25 dynamic) skips its header entirely when none of its servers are in-window so headers don't dangle. Scroll indicators (`↑ N more above` / `↓ N more below`) render inside the dialog body when content is clipped. `needsPagination` short-circuits all windowing for small lists, so the common case (≤12 servers) is byte-for-byte identical pre-fix. Memo invalidation chain `selectedIndex → renderServerItem → t22..t25 → t29` keeps the scroll indicators' captured values fresh on every navigation step.)
- [x] `Fixed mid-input slash command autocomplete not working after an initial slash command` — DONE (`utils/suggestions/commandSuggestions.ts findMidInputSlashCommand` was bailing with `if (input.startsWith('/')) return null` whenever the input started with a leading slash — preventing autocomplete on a SECOND slash command typed after the first. Removed the early return; the regex requires whitespace (or CJK punctuation) before the captured slash so it can't accidentally match the leading slash. `/foo arg1 /bar<cursor>` now surfaces ghost-text completion for `/bar`.)
- [x] `Fixed scrolling to bottom re-engaging auto-follow with autoScrollEnabled: false` — DONE (`ink/components/ScrollBox.tsx scrollToBottom` accepts new `opts?: { stick?: boolean }` parameter; default `true` preserves existing behavior. `components/FullscreenLayout.tsx jumpToNew` now reads `getSettings_DEPRECATED()?.autoScrollEnabled` and passes `{ stick: false }` when explicitly disabled — manual jump-to-new scrolls once but doesn't re-engage auto-follow for subsequent assistant output.)
- [x] `Fixed prompt suggestions being auto-submitted by Enter on an empty input instead of requiring Tab or arrow to accept` — DONE (`components/PromptInput/PromptInput.tsx`: `inputMatchesSuggestion` was `inputParam.trim() === '' || inputParam === suggestionText` — the empty-input clause auto-submitted the suggestion as a query when the user pressed Enter on a blank input. Now `inputParam === suggestionText` only — empty Enter is a no-op, user must explicitly accept via Tab or right-arrow first. The `useTypeahead.tsx` Tab/right-arrow accept paths still gate on `input === ''` because that's the explicit-gesture path which is correct.)
- [~] `Fixed keyboard shortcut hints not reflecting rebound keys from keybindings.json` — PARTIAL (infra already exists: `ConfigurableShortcutHint` + `useShortcutDisplay` resolve hints against the live `KeybindingContext`, and some sites — e.g. `Feedback.tsx` confirm:no — already use it. Many sites still pass static strings to the raw `KeyboardShortcutHint`, especially Enter/Esc/arrows in dialogs. Without an upstream source diff naming which sites the fix migrated, mass migration risks picking wrong action names / contexts across 30+ files. Marking partial — site-by-site migration to `ConfigurableShortcutHint` is the follow-up.)
- [x] `Fixed /settings language change being reverted on Escape after confirming` — DONE-AS-CORRECT (rationale-correction during 2026-05-17 audit pass: prior SKIP was wrong — we DO ship `src/components/LanguagePicker.tsx` and `src/components/Settings/Config.tsx`'s Language submenu. However the upstream confirm-then-revert bug doesn't manifest in our flow: `Config.tsx:1816-1833`'s `onComplete` writes to userSettings immediately + closes submenu; `onCancel` only closes the submenu without touching settings. There is no intermediate "confirmed but unsaved" state for Escape to revert. No code change needed.)
- [x] `Fixed /terminal-setup only appearing in autocomplete on exact name match instead of partial prefixes` — DONE (`utils/suggestions/commandSuggestions.ts` `generateCommandSuggestions`: after Fuse search, scan `commands` for any name/alias whose lowercase form `startsWith(query)` and isn't already in `searchResults`; inject as synthetic Fuse hits with score 0.05 so they survive the `threshold: 0.3` filter and get ranked ahead of fuzzy matches by the existing prefix-name comparator. Fixes `/term` → `/terminal-setup` and similar long-name commands where Fuse's threshold drops short-query/long-name pairs.)
- [-] `Fixed "Chat about this" on an AskUserQuestion dialog erasing the question text` — SKIP-AS-COMPLIANT (verified 2026-05-17: `handleRespondToClaude` at `components/permissions/AskUserQuestionPermissionRequest/AskUserQuestionPermissionRequest.tsx:293-318` and `handleFinishPlanInterview` at `:332-356` both interpolate `q_1.question` directly into the feedback string sent via `toolUseConfirm.onReject(feedback, imageBlocks)`. The question text IS preserved in our fork. Either we never had the bug (different fork point) or it was fixed independently. No surface in our codebase drops the question text on the Chat-about-this path.)
- [x] `Fixed MCP tool results being invisible when the server returns content blocks` — DONE (`tools/MCPTool/UI.tsx renderToolResultMessage`: when the MCP output is an array of content blocks, the array map now handles non-text/non-image blocks (resource, audio, embedded JSON, etc.) instead of dropping them silently. Best-effort extraction tries `text` → `content` → `data` → `uri` → JSON-stringified rest fields, with a `[<type>] <content>` header so users can see what kind of block they got. Was `textContent = ''` for any non-text block, which rendered nothing.)
- [x] `Improved error message when --worktree collides with an existing or stale worktree` — DONE (`utils/worktree.ts` `doCreateWorktreeLocked`: when `git worktree add` fails, parse `git worktree list --porcelain` to find which worktree owns the colliding path or branch and check its `prunable` flag. Stale (prunable) → suggest `git worktree prune`; active → name the live owner path. New helper `parseCollidingWorktreePath` reads porcelain blocks for `worktree`/`branch`/`prunable` lines.)
- [x] `Changed plugin marketplace removal key to d (matching delete elsewhere) instead of r which collided with retry` — DONE (`commands/plugin/ManageMarketplaces.tsx`: `useInput` handler now matches `'d'`/`'D'` instead of `'r'`/`'R'` for removal; `KeyboardShortcutHint shortcut="d" action="remove"` updated; eslint-disable comment refreshed to mention u/d.)

## 2.1.133

- [x] `Added worktree.baseRef setting (fresh | head) to choose whether --worktree, EnterWorktree, and agent-isolation worktrees branch from origin/<default> or local HEAD. Note: the default fresh changes EnterWorktree's base back to origin/<default> (it has been local HEAD since 2.1.128) — set worktree.baseRef: "head" to keep unpushed commits in new worktrees` — DONE in 8032c4f (`settings/types.ts` adds `baseRef: z.enum(["fresh","head"]).optional()` to the worktree schema; `worktree.ts:doCreateWorktreeLocked` reads `getInitialSettings().worktree?.baseRef ?? "fresh"` and chooses origin/<default> vs local HEAD with full fallback chain. Live-verified: fresh→origin SHA, head→local HEAD with unpushed commit.)
- [-] `Added sandbox.bwrapPath and sandbox.socatPath managed settings (Linux/WSL) to specify custom bubblewrap and socat binary locations` — SKIP (managed settings infra; we don't ship the managed-settings remote-sync layer this hooks into. Live-verified: hook payload contains `effort:{level:"medium"}`, Bash subprocesses see `CLAUDE_EFFORT=high`, env override path works (CLAUDE_CODE_EFFORT_LEVEL=max wins over appState=low → CLAUDE_EFFORT=max).)

Without an upstream source diff or a specific user-facing issue, blind tweak risks regressing the working fullscreen reassertion (3237b90) or away-summary idle path (commit 5faed8e) that depend on the existing focus state machine.)

Session-log writes in `sessionStorage.ts` don't use proper-lockfile so no fix needed there. Pattern matches the existing onCompromised handler in config.ts:1217.)
- [x] `Fixed pressing Esc during conversation compaction showing a spurious "Error compacting conversation" notification` — DONE in 8032c4f (`compact.ts:addErrorNotificationIfNeeded` extends the abort-error filter from a single exact-message match (`'API Error: Request was aborted.'`) to also call `isAbortError(error)`, which catches `AbortError`/`APIUserAbortError`/`Error{name:"AbortError"}` shapes that Esc produces below the API layer. Verified `isAbortError` returns true for the `name='AbortError'` shape and false for unrelated errors.)
- [x] `Fixed HTTP(S)_PROXY / NO_PROXY / mTLS not being respected for the full MCP OAuth flow including discovery, dynamic client registration, token exchange, and token refresh` — DONE in b493b3e (`services/mcp/auth.ts:createAuthFetch` now wires `getProxyFetchOptions({targetUrl})` into every OAuth-flow request — NO_PROXY hostname matching applies per-request on the Bun path; on Node/undici NO_PROXY is handled inside EnvHttpProxyAgent. `fetchAuthServerMetadata`'s optional fetchFn parameter now defaults to `createAuthFetch()` unconditionally, so SDK's `discoverOAuthServerInfo` + `discoverAuthorizationServerMetadata` calls always inherit proxy-aware fetch (previously they fell through to SDK's raw-fetch default when callers omitted fetchFn). Threaded `fetchFn: createAuthFetch()` through both `sdkAuth()` calls (initial REDIRECT + authorization-code completion) so DCR + token exchange honor proxy/mTLS. Token-refresh path at `_doRefresh` line 2290 already created authFetch and threaded it through discovery + metadata + sdkRefreshAuthorization, so refresh was already covered. Token revocation uses axios.post (line 467) which is covered by `configureGlobalAgents()` axios httpsAgent setup. All four OAuth phases (discovery, DCR, exchange, refresh) plus revocation now honor proxy/mTLS uniformly. Live-verified: HTTPS_PROXY=http://example.invalid:8888 → proxy field set; NO_PROXY=example.com → proxy field omitted for example.com target; TLS CA bundle present in both cases.)

## 2.1.132

- [x] `Added CLAUDE_CODE_SESSION_ID environment variable to the Bash tool subprocess environment, matching the session_id passed to hooks` — DONE in 3237b90 (`subprocessEnv.ts` injectSessionId() lazily reads `getSessionId()` from bootstrap/state, threaded through the same chain as injectAiAgent/injectTraceparent)

- [x] `Added a "Pasting…" footer hint while a Ctrl+V image paste is being read from the clipboard` — DONE in 3237b90 (`PromptInput.tsx` handleImagePaste sets isPasting(true) before getImageFromClipboard, .finally(() => setIsPasting(false)))
- [x] `Fixed external SIGINT (e.g. IDE stop button, kill -INT) not running graceful shutdown — terminal modes are now restored and the --resume hint is printed instead of an abrupt exit` — DONE in 3237b90 (`main.tsx:615` early-startup SIGINT handler now defers to gracefulShutdown's handler when isGracefulShutdownInitialized() returns true; new export added to gracefulShutdown.ts)

- [x] `Fixed --resume failing with "no low surrogate in string" when a tool error truncation split an emoji; pre-corrupted sessions are sanitized on load` — DONE in 3237b90 (`conversationRecovery.ts` sanitizeLoneSurrogates + sanitizeMessageSurrogates walk text/thinking/tool_result content blocks and replace lone surrogates with U+FFFD before any UTF-8 encoding can throw)
- [~] `Fixed --permission-mode flag being ignored when resuming a plan-mode session with -p --continue/--resume, and plan mode not being re-applied after ExitPlanMode within the same session` — PARTIAL in fbe1d08 (primary clause done: `currentSessionPermissionMode` field added to `Project` class, `PermissionModeEntry` (`{type:"permission-mode",...}`) added to entry union, `appendEntry` + `reAppendSessionMetadata` + metadata-extract maps wired, `savePermissionMode()` called from `onChangeAppState` choke-point so every mutation path persists + eagerly seeded at `initializeToolPermissionContext` for sessions that never toggle mid-run; `loadConversationForResume` returns `permissionMode` and both `processResumedConversation` (interactive) and `runHeadless` (`-p`) apply via `setAppState` unless user passed `--permission-mode` / `--dangerously-skip-permissions` on this run. Three-way precedence verified end-to-end: no flag → restored mode wins; `--permission-mode X` → CLI wins; `--dangerously-skip-permissions` → bypass wins. **Secondary clause** ("plan mode not re-applied after ExitPlanMode within same session") still TODO — that's a separate `prePlanMode` state-machine concern for users who started in plan mode explicitly: `prepareContextForPlanMode` returns early when already in plan (no `prePlanMode` saved), so ExitPlanMode → falls back to `default` instead of `plan`. Fix shape: detect user-initial-plan-mode separately and use it as the fallback when `prePlanMode` is undefined. Multiple plausible approaches with different blast radii — defer until concrete repro confirms the right one.)
- [x] `Fixed fullscreen mode showing a blank screen after laptop sleep/wake or Ctrl+Z/fg until the next keystroke or stream output` — DONE in 3237b90 (`ink.tsx` adds 1s event-loop stall detector — if wallclock advances >5s between ticks, reassertTerminalModes(true) + forceRedraw() on alt-screen)
- [x] `Fixed cursor landing mid-grapheme on Ctrl+E/A/K/U/arrow keys when an Indic conjunct or ZWJ emoji wraps across lines` — DONE in 7eba589 (`Cursor.ts MeasuredText.getOffsetFromPosition` now snaps the final offset through `snapToGraphemeBoundary` so wrap-induced mid-grapheme positions get pulled back to the cluster's start)
- [x] `Fixed vim operators corrupting text containing decomposed (NFD) accented characters` — DONE in 7eba589 (`vim/operators.ts applyOperator` snaps `from`/`to` to grapheme boundaries via `snapToGraphemeBoundary` before the slice — guards against any caller passing offsets that landed between a base char and a combining mark)
- [x] `Fixed pasting text starting with / silently swallowing the input or triggering an unknown-command reply` — DONE in 3237b90 (`PromptInput.tsx` onTextPaste: looksLikePathPaste heuristic — input empty, starts with '/', length>1, has second separator before space — routes through paste-ref system so isSlashCommand check at REPL.tsx:3566 doesn't fire)
- [x] `Fixed pasting dumping stray escape sequences into the prompt when focus events or mouse-tracking reports interleave with the bracketed paste` — DONE in 3237b90 (`parse-keypress.ts` filters focus events `\e[I`/`\e[O` and mouse SGR/X10 reports out of pasteBuffer when inPaste=true)
- [x] `Fixed mouse wheel scrolling being too fast in Cursor and VS Code 1.92–1.104 due to an upstream xterm.js bug` — DONE in 7eba589 (`ScrollKeybindingHandler.tsx`: new `WHEEL_BURST_MS_XTERMJS_BUGGY=40` constant + `isAffectedXtermJsVersion()` parser of `TERM_PROGRAM_VERSION`. Catches duplicate xterm.js wheel events at 5-40ms gaps at the 1-row-per-event rail. Covers VS Code 1.92.x–1.104.x and Cursor 0.40.x–0.46.x.)
- [x] `Fixed scroll-wheel handling in JetBrains IDE 2025.2 terminals (spurious arrow keys, wrong-direction events, runaway acceleration)` — DONE in 7eba589 (same `isAffectedXtermJsVersion()` check additionally returns true for `TERM_PROGRAM=JetBrains-JediTerm` + `TERMINAL_EMULATOR=JetBrains-JediTerm` 2025.2; treats it as a known-buggy host so wheel bursts dampen)
- [x] `Fixed /usage Ctrl+S hanging when copying the stats screenshot to the clipboard on Linux/X11` — DONE in 7eba589 (`screenshotClipboard.ts`: dropped the broken xsel fallback — xsel doesn't support image MIME types and was hanging on stdin EOF. Replaced with wl-copy via `child_process.spawn` + `stdin.end(pngData)` for Wayland users; xclip remains the X11 happy path)

- [x] `Fixed /effort picker not reflecting the CLAUDE_CODE_EFFORT_LEVEL env var override` — DONE in 3237b90 (already fixed in our fork — verified `EffortSlider` (effort.tsx:170) initializes from `getDisplayedEffortLevel(model, effortValue)` which calls `resolveAppliedEffort()` which prioritizes `getEffortEnvOverride()` over appState; env var has highest priority in the chain)
- [-] `Fixed /status showing the wrong default model for some users` — REVERTED in 6ac523f (initially gated on `is1PApiCustomer()` in 7eba589, but per user direction USER_TYPE=ant is openclaude's universal feature-unlock signal — every fork user is "an ant" and should see the "Default for Ants (…)" label regardless of provider. Upstream's gate doesn't apply here.)
- [x] `Fixed slash command autocomplete popup being capped at ~3–5 visible commands instead of scaling with terminal height` — DONE in 3237b90 (`PromptInputFooterSuggestions.tsx` adds computeMaxVisibleSuggestions(rows) — floor 5, ceil 15, scales with `rows - 6`; replaces the flat `Math.min(6, rows-3)` cap and the OVERLAY_MAX_ITEMS=5 constant)
- [x] `Fixed statusline context_window token counts reflecting cumulative session totals instead of current context usage` — DONE (`f799aa4`) Extracted upstream `SQ5` payload builder from binary via `strings -n 100`: `total_input_tokens = currentUsage ? (input + cache_creation + cache_read) : 0`, `total_output_tokens = currentUsage?.output_tokens ?? 0`. Hook payload field names unchanged; semantics flipped from cumulative-session-sum to current-context-size. Third-party statusline scripts that displayed these as "current usage" now get the right number; cumulative totals still available via `cost.total_*`.
- [-] `Fixed Alt+T (thinking toggle) not working on macOS terminals without "Option as Meta" enabled (iTerm2, Terminal.app defaults)` — SKIP (macOS terminal specific)
- [-] `Fixed dead keyboard input on Windows after re-opening a background session from claude agents` — SKIP (Windows specific)
- [x] `Fixed unbounded memory growth (10GB+ RSS) when a stdio MCP server writes non-protocol data to stdout` — DONE `1e80ed3` Capped at the consumer layer via prototype patch on `ReadBuffer.prototype.append` in new `services/mcp/readBufferCap.ts` (side-effect import at top of `client.ts` so the cap is in place before any `StdioClientTransport` is constructed). 8 MiB limit — orders of magnitude larger than any legitimate JSON-RPC message but well below the unbounded-growth blowout. On overflow: drops accumulated buffer, throws Error whose message contains "Expected JSON-RPC framing" so the existing `stdioParseErrorCount` classifier in `client.ts` treats it as a parse error and disconnects after MAX_STDIO_PARSE_ERRORS (10). Idempotent via `Symbol.for('openclaude.mcpReadBufferCapped')` sentinel so re-imports don't double-wrap. 5 `bun:test` cases cover normal chunks / single oversize / cumulative oversize / clear-on-overflow / idempotent-reimport. Convention exception: usually we fix MCP SDK internals on next upgrade, but the production-crash severity (10GB+ RSS) + low-risk prototype patch (one method, sentinel-guarded) tilts toward shipping now. Patch becomes a redundant lower-bound when the SDK's own fix lands in a future minor.
- [x] `Fixed MCP servers that connect but fail tools/list silently showing 0 tools — they now retry once and show "connected · tools fetch failed" in /mcp` — DONE in 3237b90 (`mcp/client.ts` fetchToolsForClient: try the request once, on error log + retry once; persist failures in the new module-level `_toolsFetchFailed` Set; `MCPListPanel.tsx` renders "tools fetch failed" pill via new `hasToolsFetchFailed(name)` helper)
- [-] `Fixed unauthorized claude.ai MCP connectors showing as "failed" instead of "needs auth", and headless -p mode retrying non-transient 4xx connection failures` — SKIP (claude.ai MCP connector specific; we don't route through claude.ai OAuth)
- [-] `Improved visual consistency in slash command dialogs and /login, /upgrade, /extra-usage dialog spacing` — SKIP (pure polish without upstream source diff to validate the specific paddingX/marginTop tweaks). We don't ship `/login`, `/upgrade`, or `/extra-usage` (claude.ai-OAuth + billing surfaces). Our `/plugin`/`/status`/`/permissions` dialogs render through the shared `design-system/Dialog` component that already enforces consistent spacing via its outer padding contract; per-command paddingTop/paddingBottom adjustments without a concrete reference would risk introducing inconsistencies in the OPPOSITE direction. Revisit only with a screenshot showing a specific glitch in a dialog we ship.
- [x] `Updated the /tui fullscreen startup banner to describe additional renderer benefits (lower memory usage, mouse support, auto-copy on select)` — DONE in 3237b90 (`tui.ts` expands fullscreen success message with a 4-bullet renderer-benefits block + CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_ALTERNATE_SCREEN opt-out hint)
- [-] `Fixed Bedrock and Vertex 400 errors when ENABLE_PROMPT_CACHING_1H is set` — SKIP (Bedrock/Vertex specific; we don't route through these providers)

- [x] `The /model picker now lists models from your gateway's /v1/models endpoint when ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL points at an Anthropic-compatible gateway` — DONE (already in our codebase, richer than upstream's. `utils/model/apiModels.ts#fetchApiModels` hits `/v1/models` with provider-specific config from `getFetchConfig`: DeepSeek (`/models`), OpenRouter (`/api/v1/models`), NVIDIA NIM (`/v1/models` with Bearer), and a generic Anthropic-compatible fallback (`/v1/models` with `x-api-key` + `anthropic-version`). Cache primed by `main.tsx:422` and refreshed via setup commands)

Sizable port; useful but not urgent — users can `rm -rf ~/.claude/projects/<encoded-path>/` manually until then)

New `isBypassableDangerousPath()` helper sets `bypassableUnderDangerousSkip: true` on the safety result so the existing 2.1.121 bypass mechanism just works. UNC paths (`\\\\` / `//`) NEVER bypass — they can reach network resources. Catastrophic-rm safety still kicks in via `pathValidation.ts#checkDangerousRemovalPaths`. Live-verified: `.git/config`, `.vscode/settings.json`, `.bashrc`, `.idea/workspace.xml`, `.husky/pre-commit` all flip bypassable; `.mcp.json` / `.claude.json` still require explicit approval; `foo.bashrc` (non-exact filename match) returns safe)

- [x] `Fixed API retry countdown sticking at "0s" instead of counting down between attempts` — DONE (verified structurally correct in `components/messages/SystemAPIErrorMessage.tsx:52-74` — `setCountdownMs` increments by 1000ms via `useInterval`, `retryInSecondsLive = Math.max(0, Math.round((retryInMs - countdownMs) / 1000))` clamps the live value, interval stops on `done = countdownMs >= retryInMs`. - [-] `Fixed "Stream idle timeout" error after waking Mac from sleep mid-request` — SKIP (macOS-sleep-specific clock-jump behavior. Our fork doesn't run primarily on macOS — Linux/WSL container is the production runtime — so the wake-from-sleep regression doesn't manifest in our primary deployment)
- [x] `Fixed background and remote sessions falsely aborting with "Stream idle timeout" during long model thinking pauses` — DONE in ea19371 (`services/api/claude.ts` — new `thinkingBlockActive` flag tracks open thinking content blocks. While thinking is in progress, `resetStreamIdleTimer()` uses `STREAM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS * 3` instead of the standard cap (15 min main thread / 30 min subagents instead of 5/10). The flag flips on `content_block_start` for type='thinking' and resets on `content_block_stop` for that block. Does NOT affect tool-use or text streams — those keep the standard idle ceiling. Warning log includes `(thinking active)` annotation when applicable so the diagnostic trail explains why the timer is generous)
- [x] `Fixed a hang where the assistant could finish thinking but show no output after a run of empty turns` — DONE in ea19371 (`query.ts` — empty-turn safety. After each stream completes, check whether the turn produced ANY visible content (text with non-whitespace chars, tool_use, or server_tool_use). If it produced only thinking/redacted_thinking/empty-text content, increment `consecutiveEmptyTurns`; reset to 0 on the next turn that produces visible content. At `MAX_CONSECUTIVE_EMPTY_TURNS=3`, abort with a synthetic `createAssistantAPIErrorMessage` explaining that the model produced thinking-only responses for 3 turns and stopping to avoid a silent hang. The API error message hint suggests rephrasing the request, reducing thinking depth, or starting a fresh session. Telemetry event `tengu_empty_turn_loop_aborted` fires on the abort path. Doesn't false-trigger on api-error messages (those have non-empty content and their own recovery paths))

Now only triggers `instances.get(process.stdout)?.forceRedraw()`, matching readline-style "redraw without losing typed text". `onInputChange` removed from the `useCallback` deps array since the callback no longer references it; the prop signature stays unchanged so other handlers in the hook can still consume it)

With ToolSearch on (default), `claude.ts:1219` filters deferred tools to only those in `extractDiscoveredToolNames(messages)`; a fork/subagent's first turn has no `<discovered-tools>`/tool_reference markers, so WebSearch/WebFetch/MCP are excluded on turn 1. Gated the early-return on `operation !== 'uninstall'` — uninstall has nothing to configure anyway)

New caps: `CHANGED_FILES_TOTAL_MAX_BYTES = 64KB`, `CHANGED_FILES_MAX_COUNT = 24`. Keeps oldest-first up to the budget; synthesizes a single summary `edited_text_file` with a sentinel filename `<modified-files-summary>` listing the omitted filenames (capped at 200 lines + "and N more files" trailer) so the model still knows the full scope of changes)
