- [x] `Fixed Ctrl+- not triggering undo in terminals using the Kitty keyboard protocol (iTerm2, Ghostty, kitty, WezTerm, Windows Terminal)` — DONE in 08490c3 (registered `ctrl+-` → `chat:undo` alongside existing `ctrl+_` and `ctrl+shift+-` bindings)
- [x] `Fixed Cmd+Left/Right not jumping to line start/end in terminals that use the Kitty keyboard protocol (Warp fullscreen, kitty, Ghostty, WezTerm)` — DONE in 3f1e9f9 (added `key.super` branches for leftArrow/rightArrow → startOfLine/endOfLine, placed before the word-jump branch)
- [x] `Fixed Ctrl+Z hanging the terminal when Claude Code is launched via a wrapper process (e.g. npx, bun run)` — DONE in 73aa439 (SIGTSTP to process group pid=0 instead of SIGSTOP to leaf pid, so the bun wrapper stops too and shell job-control reclaims the TTY)

DEFERRED: **stale resolved versions after update** — `installed_plugins.json` versions stay accurate via `addInstalledPlugin`'s preserve-on-update logic, but the upstream description hints at a deeper scan-and-repair pass for cases where the version was computed from gitCommitSha but the cached artifact since drifted; needs concrete repro)

- [x] `Fixed transcript write failures (disk full) being silently dropped instead of logged` — DONE in 19f2d6c (logError on tombstone removal + metadata re-append)
- [x] `Fixed diacritical marks (accents, umlauts, cedillas) being dropped from responses when language setting is configured` — DONE in 19f2d6c (combining marks \p{M} guard in sanitization.ts)
- [x] `Fixed policy-managed plugins never auto-updating when running from different project than first installed` — DONE (root cause: policy-managed plugins live in `policySettings.enabledPlugins` and get loaded with `scope='managed'` at runtime, but our codebase never persists them to `installed_plugins.json` with that scope. The autoupdate iterator walked only `installed_plugins.json`, so policy plugins were silently invisible — and only updated incidentally when the user happened to ALSO have a `scope='user'` install of the same plugin from the original project. Patched `updatePluginsForMarketplaces` in `pluginAutoupdate.ts` to also pull plugin IDs from `policySettings.enabledPlugins`. For policy-only entries (no installed_plugins.json row) we synthesize a single `{scope:'managed', projectPath:undefined}` installation; `updatePluginOp('managed')` finds it via `getProjectPathForScope('managed')` returning undefined, and the update proceeds regardless of which project the CLI launched from)

If the first rename fails with EBUSY/EPERM (file lock), both staging and installLocation stay intact and the marketplace remains usable; if the second rename fails, the backup is restored before re-throwing. Best-effort cleanup of leftover `<installLocation>.backup-<ts>` dirs from prior crashed swaps runs at the start of each fetch)

Both default OFF — opt-in only — matching upstream policy ergonomics and the L169 USER_PROMPTS precedent. Tool name + use_id always ride regardless, so observability dashboards still see which tools fired without leaking sensitive inputs/outputs to organizations that haven't opted in)

- [-] `Added interactive Google Vertex AI setup wizard from login screen` — SKIP (Anthropic cloud infra)
- [x] `Added CLAUDE_CODE_PERFORCE_MODE env var: Edit/Write/NotebookEdit fail on read-only files with p4 edit hint` — DONE in 35a791c

- [x] `Added subprocess sandboxing with PID namespace isolation on Linux (CLAUDE_CODE_SUBPROCESS_ENV_SCRUB, CLAUDE_CODE_SCRIPT_CAPS)` — DONE in cb7e5ca (unshare --pid --fork when CLAUDE_CODE_SCRIPT_CAPS=1 or IS_SANDBOX=1)

- [x] `Added workspace.git_worktree to status line JSON input` — DONE in 4950e3e
- [x] `Added W3C TRACEPARENT env var to Bash tool subprocesses when OTEL tracing enabled` — DONE in 307c770 (`services/observe/tracing.ts` now exposes a module-level `_currentTraceHandle` written by `createTrace` and cleared by `endTrace`, accessed via `getCurrentTraceHandle()`. `utils/subprocessEnv.ts` gains `injectTraceparent()` that lazy-requires the observe module to avoid pulling the BasicTracerProvider chain into every consumer at import time; reads the active `agent-run` span's `spanContext()`, formats `00-<traceId>-<spanId>-<flags>` per W3C, and sets `TRACEPARENT` (plus `TRACESTATE` when the span has trace state). Chained into both the GHA-scrub and non-scrub return paths of `subprocessEnv()`. Pre-existing `process.env.TRACEPARENT` from a parent process (CI runner, `claude self-hosted-runner`, etc.) is preserved by the shallow copy when no agent-run span is active. `TRACEPARENT`/`TRACESTATE` are W3C-standard names and don't start with `OTEL_`, so the existing OTEL-namespace scrub leaves them alone)
